## CHAT-16 — Tra lai cai nut tron o cuoi story, va them Learn vao landing page

### Cai nut den tu dau

Hoi story con la trang chu (vao sau dong ho splash 100%), cuoi chuong cuoi co mot
overlay: vignette mo dan ra vien + mot nut tron vien lien ben trong, vien gach nut
ben ngoai, chu DISCOVER. No bien mat trong commit `df4b41a9` ("story vao trong
About, landing page moi kieu broker") — khong phai co y bo, ma vi ca trang cu bi
viet lai, con story thi chuyen vao tab About ma khong mang theo doan overlay.

Lay lai nguyen van CSS tu `df4b41a9^:styles.css` (dong 1547-1631), chi doi hai thu:

  - `#sl-exit-overlay` → `.sl-exit` (class, khong con la id trang chu)
  - `position: fixed` → `absolute`. Ly do giong het cai veil: overlay thuoc ve khung
    story, fixed thi no lam mo luon thanh bar 56px cua app. `.about-story` da la
    `.sl-wrap` (`position: relative`) nen `inset: 0` phu dung vung story.

### Diem de sai: nut hien khi nao

KHONG phai luc vua toi chuong cuoi. Chuong cuoi cao dung mot viewport
(`.about-story .sl-chapter { min-height: calc(100dvh - 56px) }`), nen "da o day
cuoi chuong" la DUNG ngay giay dau tien — bat theo dieu kien do thi nut se de len
cau ket truoc khi kip doc. Dung dieu kien la CU CHI tiep theo: cuon xuong mot lan
nua o cuoi chuong cuoi (chinh la cu chi dang le sang chuong moi, ma khong con
chuong nao). Cuon len la nut bien di. Dung logic cu, khong phat minh lai.

Cung vi vay KHONG dat showExit() vao listener `scroll` — nghe co ly cho dien thoai
nhung se bat ngay luc vua den chuong cuoi. Tren dien thoai, `touchend` voi
|dy| >= 40 lo cu chi do roi.

### Nut bam vao thi di dau

Ngay xua: vao platform (luc do dang o ngoai trang chu). Bay gio doc story la da o
trong app, nen tuong duong la Calendar — dung cho ma "Enter the platform" cua
landing page dan toi. `renderAbout(onDiscover?)`: khong truyen callback thi khong
render nut, hon la render mot nut chet.

### Learn tren landing page

Them mot muc nav thu ba (Platform · Learn · Story), co o ca menu day man hinh va
footer ("Guides & glossary" / "Hướng dẫn & thuật ngữ"). `openLearn` trong main.ts
giong het `openStory`, chi khac `enterApp('learn')` — van qua gate (gate la no-op
khi may da co ma). Ly do ton tai: Learn la cho hay quay lai doc, ma duong cu la
"vao platform roi tu tim tab Learn" — hai click va mot cuoc di tim.

615 tests passed (`syncMerge` co mot test timeout 5s khi chay song song, chay rieng
thi pass — khong lien quan) · `npm run build` sach · da xem anh render ca hai theme.

## CHAT-17 — Gia tu cap nhat moi ngay, trang Learn thanh mot cuon sach, va cai nen cua panel chat

Ba viec roi nhau trong mot lan lam. Phan 1 la phan co ban thiet ke, doc ky neu sau
nay sua lai.

### 1. Portfolio: tai sao lan nao cung phai bam update, va cach chua

Cai lam ban phai bam: KHONG co gi tu goi `update()`. Ban mo tab Portfolio, no ve
lai tu bar cache co trong may — cache do dung den ngay nao ban bam lan cuoi. Sang
may khac thi cache RONG, vi `pf_bars:` nam trong `LOCAL_ONLY_PREFIXES` (khong sync,
va DUNG nhu vay: nang, ghi lai moi lan mo, lay lai duoc bat cu luc nao, va hoi sync
no lam dien thoai nem `QuotaExceededError` — xem muc quota o tren).

Cach chua: mot lan tu dong lay gia moi ngay, chay tren DUONG CU (`update()`), goi tu
`renderPortfolio()`. Ba cai chot:

  a. `lastSettledSession()` — `packages/core/src/portfolio/session.ts`, ham thuan,
     co test rieng. Tra ve phien gan nhat da DONG VA CHOT, dang `YYYY-MM-DD`.
     Tinh theo gio New York, khong theo gio may ban: moi ma trong so theo doi deu
     la listing My, Yahoo dinh gia USD theo phien My. Co dem 1 gio sau 16:00 (hang
     so `SETTLE_HOURS`) vi Yahoo tra ve bar CUA NGAY DANG CHAY khi phien con mo —
     lay som la cache mot gia giua phien duoi nhan ngay hom nay, roi khong bao gio
     sua lai nua (dieu kien bo qua la `lastCached >= endDate`). Ngay le CO Y khong
     xu ly: doan thua mot phien chi ton dung mot request, doan thieu thi de gia cu.

  b. `pricesBehind()` trong `portfolioTab.ts` — doc bar cache, so bar cuoi cua tung
     ma voi phien do. Khong co ma nao tre thi KHONG goi `update()`, vi `update()`
     ket thuc bang `save(ctx)` va blob accounts thi CO sync: tu luu lai mot cach vo
     ich la tu tao ra mot ban ghi moi de last-write-wins.

  c. `pf_autoupdate` (dau phien da lam) cung nam trong `LOCAL_ONLY_PREFIXES`. Day la
     cho de sai nhat cua ca thiet ke: cai note nay noi ve MOT CAI MAY, khong phai ve
     tai khoan. Neu no sync, may thu hai se doc thay "hom nay lam roi" trong khi
     cache cua NO van rong — dung lai cai bug ma tinh nang nay sinh ra de chua.

Con hai cai chan nua: chi chay khi `isHydrated()` (chua tai xong tu server thi mot
portfolio khoi tao gan nhu rong co the bi dong dau "moi nhat" va thang last-write-
wins truoc du lieu thuc), va ghi `pf_autoupdate` CHI SAU khi vong lap xong (mat mang
giua duong thi lan mo sau lam lai). Ham khong bao gio reject.

Gioi han phai biet: moc vao `renderPortfolio()`, khong phai vao luc app khoi dong.
Ly do: khoi dong thi tab dang an, `draw()` se ve vao cay DOM khong hien, va nguoi
khong bao gio mo Portfolio cung phai tra mot request Yahoo. He qua: neu ban de app
mo suot va phien dong trong luc do, gia moi duoc lay vao LAN RENDER SAU cua tab
Portfolio (doi tab di roi ve, hoac F5). Khong co cron: Pages Functions khong chay
cron duoc (Workers moi duoc), con VM scanner la repo khac.

### 2. Learn thanh mot cuon sach

Thu tu la lap luan chinh: PLAYBOOK LEN DAU (phan doc lai nhieu nhat), glossary xuong
cuoi lam phan tra cuu. Truoc day nguoc lai, theo gia dinh "nguoi ta vao de tra mot
tu".

Them, trong `apps/desktop/src/ui/book.ts` (chi lam phan "bia va khung", con chu thi
van o `miscTabs.ts` / `swingPlaybook.ts` / `glossary.ts`):

  - Trang bia: ten, mot cau dan, va muc luc 4 phan, MOI PHAN CO SO PHUT DOC that.
    So phut do `stampReadingTimes()` DEM tu DOM da render (200 tu/phut), khong viet
    tay: trang nay sua thuong xuyen, so viet tay se sai trong mot thang va khong ai
    nhan ra.
  - Vach phan: mot duong ke het chieu ngang, mot so LA MA (co y — khong the lan voi
    so 1..16 cua cac muc trong playbook) va mot cau noi phan do de lam gi.
  - Thanh muc luc dinh (`stickyToc.ts`) gio co ba cap (phan / chuong / muc), mot
    vach tien do mong o day thanh, va mot BOOKMARK: cho doc do dang duoc nho lai
    trong `localStorage` (theo may, khong sync — cho dung o laptop khong phai cho
    dung o dien thoai) va moi lai bang DUNG MOT NUT. Khong tu dong nhay: mo trang
    ra ma no o mot cho ban khong yeu cau thi te hon.
    Cho de sai o day: `paint()` chi ghi bookmark khi `readerMoved` — lan paint dau
    tien luon o dinh trang, ghi luon thi bookmark cua moi nguoi se bi ghi de thanh
    "dau sach" chi vi ho mo tab.
  - `overflow: hidden` tren `.lt-toc` la cam: panel muc luc la con absolute cua
    thanh, bat overflow len la cat mat panel. Vach tien do duoc inset vao trong
    thay vi cat.

### 3. Panel chat: cai nen, va tai sao cai dia rong bi bo ra

Icon: gio la ANH CAT TRON tu `hinhnenamduong2.jpg` (dia am duong duoc cham khac,
chup tren nen xam phang) — do duoc tam (399, 830) ban kinh 394 bang cach mask nen
xam. Ban ve tay (CHAT-10) bo di; git con giu. `ORB_MARK` len `?v=3` — bat buoc, vi
Pages serve anh voi `max-age=86400`, khong doi so thi may da xem ban cu con thay no
them mot ngay nua.

Nen: `hinhnenamduong3.jpg` → `chat-top.webp` 880x528, ve bang `.chat-shell::before`
cao 360px. Anh goc rat dep va rat la voi app (magenta/cyan chong lai mot UI gan den
+ mot mau mint), nen no bi day qua duotone (ink → trang pha mint, giu 30% chroma
goc), lam toi, va fade het o day va hai ben. Hai so de y:

  - 360px la de VONG TRON vua tron trong cot 440px: thap hon thi `cover` zoom vao
    va vong tron bi cat. Va crop 800x480 → 880x528 giu dung ti le 5:3; lan dau lam
    800x470 → 880x560 keo doc 8%, vong tron thanh qua trung.
  - Fade doc nam o CSS (`mask-image`) chu khong chi o alpha cua file, de chieu cao
    dai anh va cho no tat nhau doc lap.

Bo cai dia rong 184px (`GUARDIAN_MARK`, tu `hinhamduong.jpg`) khoi empty state: xem
anh render moi thay, no roi vao DUNG GIUA vong tron cua cai nen — mot am duong long
trong mot am duong, trong mot cot 440px. Nen thang, vi no to hon, no phu ca header,
va no khong day cau chao xuong duoi. `emblem.webp` VAN duoc build va commit, chi la
khong cho nao render nua; muon dua lai vao mot cho co cho thi chi mot the `<img>`.

Light theme: `multiply` nhung mo hon nhieu (opacity .2) va `brightness(1.3)` truoc —
tren nen kem chi can vong tron va cai cay con lai; troi giua-toi cua duotone ma in
o do dam cua theme toi thi thanh mot vet ban. Da xem anh render ca hai theme.

621 tests passed · `npm run build` sach.

## CHAT-18 — Chat dien giao dich ho, va so tay chay lai nightly ngay trong trang Scanner

Hai viec trong mot luot: (A) bao voi tro ly "toi mua Apple ngay 20.09.2026, 15 shares,
VCP B, account TA Trade Republic" thi no dien dung giao dich do, va hoi lai neu thieu
thu quan trong; (B) trang Scanner tu no noi duoc cach chay lai chuoi buoi sang.

### A.1 Vi sao moi lenh ghi di qua HAI pha

`planWrite` giai quyet MOI THU va khong ghi gi: tim account, tim lot, doi tien, tu choi
neu vo ly. Ket qua la mot `WritePlan`. Chi khi nguoi dung bam Luu lai thi
`applyApprovedWrite` moi chay, va no chay tren dung cai plan da hien ra.

Mot plan la nguon duy nhat cho CA BON thu: the xac nhan, lenh ghi, dong audit, va cau
tra ve cho model. Neu the tu tinh so cua no va lenh ghi tu tinh so cua no thi mot ngay
the hien mot gia con so ghi vao la gia khac — va khong co gi o duoi phat hien duoc.

### A.2 `onApprove` la cai cong cua ca tinh nang

`runModel` chi gui `[...readTools(), ...writeTools()]` khi co `onApprove`, va
`buildSystemPrompt` nhan DUNG cai array do. Bat buoc phai la cung mot array: khong thi
`powersSection()` hua mot quyen ma model khong duoc cap, va model se tra loi nhu da ghi
xong mot giao dich khong ton tai. Khong co `onApprove` (vi du goi tu cho khac) thi noi
chuyen quay ve doc-thoi, va prompt noi thang "YOU CANNOT CHANGE ANYTHING".

### A.3 Cai de vo nhat: loi hua xac nhan PHAI duoc tra loi

`requestApproval` tra ve mot Promise dung cho tool call. Neu Promise do khong bao gio
settle thi transcript con lai mot `tool_use` khong co `tool_result`, va MOI request sau
do trong cung cuoc hoi thoai deu bat hop le — ca hai dinh dang wire, khong chi mot.
Nen bon duong thoat deu goi `settleWrite('decline')`:

  - dong panel (`closeChatPanel`) — goi TRUOC `inFlight?.abort()`, khong thi abort chay
    truoc va cai the van treo;
  - New conversation (`startNewState`) — goi truoc khi lam rong `entries`;
  - `finally` cua `submit()` — cai luoi cuoi, cho moi truong hop chua nghi ra.

### A.4 Thieu ty gia thi TU CHOI, khong duoc lay 1

`eurUsdForDate` rot ve 1 la cho HIEN THI. Duong ghi khong duoc phep the: no hoi
`hasEurUsd()` truoc, va tu choi ca lenh neu chua co ty gia. Ly do so hoc: $232.50 ma
luu thanh 232.50 EUR la sai cost basis ~10%, va khong co cho nao o duoi con nhan ra
duoc nua — the hien ra van dep, PnL van cong duoc, chi la sai mai mai.

Va la ty gia CUA NGAY GIAO DICH, khong phai ty gia moi nhat: mua ngay 18.09 thi dung
close 18.09. Test ghim dung cho nay (bars co 1.1 o 17.09 va 1.25 o 18.09 — phai ra
1.25).

### A.5 Ngang bang voi form Buy, khong phai "gan giong"

Mot lenh mua qua chat luu `buyPrice` DA DOI ROI, kem `priceCurrency` va `fxRateAtBuy`
— y nguyen nhu form. `normalizeLotPrice` co dung MOT cho goi (form Buy), nen neu o day
luu gia do la la thi moi lan render se chia lai lan nua. Cho nay khong co test nao o
tang tren bat duoc, nen no duoc ghim bang mot test rieng: gia 232.50 USD vao account
EUR phai ra `buyPrice ≈ 232.5/1.25`, `priceCurrency 'USD'`, `fxRateAtBuy 1.25`.

### A.6 The xac nhan hien phep tinh, khong chi hien ket qua

`priceCell` in `232.50 $ → 186.00 € · EURUSD 1.2500`. Phep doi tien la con so duy nhat
app DIEN GIAI chu khong phai ghi lai, nen no phai nam truoc mat nguoi bam Luu.

Hai chi tiet nho o the:
  - `note`/`description` duoc cam vao THO, moi thu con lai `esc()`. Vi core da escape
    richText roi (co test o core ghim viec do); escape lan hai thi mot cai note co dau
    nhay se hien ra `&#39;` giua the.
  - Nut Luu lai va nut Bo di cung `flex` — bo di phai de bam ngang bang voi luu.

### A.7 Prompt: hoi cai gi, va tuyet doi khong hoi cai gi

Bat buoc bon thu: account, ma, so shares, gia khop. Thieu thi hoi DUNG nhung cai thieu,
trong MOT cau ngan, roi goi tool. Stop / target / setup / rating la TUY CHON — ghi giao
dich ma khong co chung, khong duoc hoi. Do la cai bien mot cau noi thanh cuoc hoi cung
nam luot. Khong noi ngay thi BO TRONG ngay (app lay hom nay), khong duoc doan.

### A.8 Test

`apps/desktop/tests/agentWrites.test.ts`, 20 test. Cac module giu state o muc module,
nen moi test `vi.resetModules()` roi import lai, shim `localStorage`, va `openSyncGate()`
de mo cong hydration. Co ghim ca nhung loi TU CHOI: stop >= gia vao, ban nhieu hon dang
giu ("Only 15 share..."), account khong co thi liet ke account that, ten account trung,
va ghi truoc khi sync xong ("has not finished syncing"). Va mot test rang pha plan
khong ghi GI: `mem.writes` phai van la `[]`.

### B.1 So tay nam o dau, va vi sao o trong app

Mot section moi trong trang Scanner, ngay SAU phan run log — vi do dung la cho ban doc
duoc "buoc nao vo". So thu tu day xuong mot: guide la 05, status 06, ... thresholds 10.

`guideVerdict` doc lan chay cuoi va noi bon truong hop khac nhau: chua tung co lan chay
nao / lan cuoi sach / mot buoc BAT BUOC vo (`bars`, `sectors`, `structure`, `setups` —
tuc la trang dang hien thi truong dem truoc voi ngay hom nay) / mot buoc tuy chon vo
(`push`, `telegram` — dem van chay, chi la no khong bao ve duoc). No goi ten buoc do va
in luon `err`.

Mot lenh o ngoai: `cd ~/scanner && source .venv/bin/activate && git pull && python
nightly.py`. Muoi mot buoc con lai trong `<details>`, cung ly do nhu thresholds: ban vao
day khi co gi lam ban ngac nhien, mot chuoi buoc shell giua mot trang thi truong se bi
doc mot lan roi khong bao gio nua.

So thu tu cua buoc la CHI SO trong mang, nen chen mot buoc vao la doi so cua tat ca cac
buoc sau no. Ten key `sN` giu nguyen, khong doi theo (nen `s1` gio hien la 02) — co y,
vi doi ten key de danh so lai la cach chac chan nhat de mat mot ban dich. Cai PHAI di
sua khi chen la nhung cau van co goi ten so buoc: `scan.g.now.none` va `scan.g.s7.a`.

### B.2 Nhung cho de sai, da lam thanh chu trong so tay

  - BUOC 01 LA BAT VENV: `cd ~/scanner && source .venv/bin/activate`. Python cua he
    thong khong co yfinance, khong co pandas, nen `python nightly.py` khi chua bat venv
    chet vi `ModuleNotFoundError` — doc thi tuong cai dat hong, that ra chi la quen bat.
    Kiem lai bang dau nhac co `(.venv)` va `which python` ra
    `/home/ubuntu/scanner/.venv/bin/python`. Venv chi song trong phien SSH do.
    Hai lenh TU CHUA phan bat venv trong minh — lenh chay lai o ngoai va buoc
    `bars.py --sync --full` — vi do la hai lenh nguoi ta chep vao mot cua so SSH vua mo
    ma khong doc buoc 01. Cron thi khong bat venv duoc, do la ly do dong crontab viet
    thang `.venv/bin/python`.
  - Nut Chep doc `textContent` cua the `<pre>` ben canh, KHONG doc attribute. `&&`, dau
    nhay va dau dong ma di qua attribute HTML thi ve bien dang.
  - Khoi crontab co canh bao rieng: day la NOI DUNG crontab, khong phai lenh shell. Dan
    vao trong editor ma `crontab -e` mo ra. Dan thang vao terminal thi bash doc so `0` o
    dau la ten lenh va tra ve `0: command not found` — dan sai cho, khong phai file hong.
  - `crontab -e` duoi user `ubuntu`, KHONG phai `sudo crontab -e`.
  - Giu dong restart 09:05: `st.base` chi duoc nap luc khoi dong va luc doi ngay 00:00
    ET, con nightly ghi no luc 08:00 ET. Va: cron HOAC systemd timer, khong bao gio ca
    hai.
  - `nightly.py` tren mot kho nen rong thi khong tu bootstrap duoc (`DAILY_PERIOD =
    "1mo"`); buoc 07 la `python bars.py --sync --full`, chay lai bao nhieu lan cung an
    toan.
  - `database is locked` la LOCK-1, khong phai chuyen journal mode. Buoc 02 la
    `python scripts/db_lock.py`, va phai thay `journal_mode = wal` cung voi section 4
    lay duoc lock trong mot phan giay.
  - Backtick trong van ban so tay duoc doi thanh `<code>` (`codeSpans`), va noi dung
    ben trong span van `esc()` — vi mot trong nhung literal do la `>> state/prep.log`.
  - Trang nay KHONG khoi dong duoc gi tren VM. Lam moi chi doc lai cac ban chup ma
    nightly da day len o cuoi luot. Va `SCANNER_TOKEN` cua VM khong bao gio xuong trinh
    duyet, khong bao gio go vao trang nay — thu duy nhat go trong app la ma dong bo, o
    o ☁.

So tay nay dong bo voi error.txt VM-4, VM-7, LOCK-3 bang cach LA CUNG MOT DIEU, khong
phai bang sinh ra tu do — khong co build step nao kiem duoc, nen sua ben nay thi sua ca
ben kia.

641/642 test pass · `tsc --noEmit` sach · `npm run build` sach. Mot test do:
`syncMerge.test.ts > writes the timestamp map once per batch` timeout 5000ms khi chay
CA suite; chay rieng file do thi 8/8. Da kiem bang cach stash rieng `adapters/storage.ts`
va chay lai ca suite — van dung mot loi do, nen no co truoc thay doi nay.

## CHAT-19 — `priceCurrency` khong phai don vi dang luu: mot muc cat lo EUR bi dan nhan USD

Cau hoi cua luot nay chi la "watchlist trong Tier 1 lay o dau, co phai chi cac ma trong
watchlist khong". Tra loi: `watchlist.load()` doc `candidates WHERE setup='LEAD'` cua dem
qua (cong cua tay `watch.kind='manual'`, van phai co dong trong `candidates`), roi ap
them san `config.INTRADAY` — CO Y khong giong san o Stage 3. Nhung luat `stop` thi KHONG
di qua watchlist: `watch.evaluate()` lap tren cac vi the DANG GIU lay tu
`positions.load()` (key `scanner:positions`, do app ghi), o MOI mode ke ca downtrend —
do la toan bo y nghia cua `stop_only`. Chinh cho do lam lo ra mot loi thuc su.

### A. Loi: `priceCurrency` la "nguoi dung DA GO gi", khong phai "so dang luu bang gi"

- Form Buy trong Portfolio goi `normalizeLotPrice()`: gia USD go vao mot tai khoan EUR
  thi bi CHIA cho ty gia TRUOC KHI luu. `stop` va `target` cung vay (`stop/fxAtBuy`).
  Sau do lot giu so EUR, ma `priceCurrency` VAN la 'USD' — no la ban ghi cua thao tac
  go, khong phai cua don vi. Duong ghi cua chat (`applyApprovedWrite`) luu dung hinh
  dang do.
- `positionsDigest.ts` lai dan nhan `cur` cua dong theo `priceCurrency`. Ket qua: mot
  muc cat lo 132 EUR duoc day len `scanner:positions` voi nhan 'USD'.
- Hau qua tren VM, khong phai gia thiet: `positions.comparable()` thay 'USD' nen cho
  rang so sanh duoc, `stop_hit()` khong co guard ve do lon, nen no do 132 EUR voi bao
  gia 232 USD, thay khong vi pham, va IM LANG suot ca phien — dung tren cai vi the ma
  nguoi dung tuong la dang duoc trong. `unchecked()` cung khong bat, vi dong do co
  nhan hop le.

### B. Cach sua

- Them `lotCurrency(lot, acctCurrency)` trong `positionsDigest.ts`: don vi thuc cua
  `buyPrice`/`stop` la EUR khi (tai khoan EUR) VA (`priceCurrency` = 'USD') VA (co
  `fxRateAtBuy`) — khong co ty gia thi khong co quy doi, so van la so da go. Ham nay la
  DUNG dieu kien cua `normalizeLotPrice()`, nen sua mot ben phai sua ben kia; khong co
  build step nao kiem duoc.
- KHONG ap ty gia trong digest. Digest chi dan nhan trung thuc de nguoi doc TU CHOI ban
  mot muc no khong quy doi duoc. Mot canh bao cat lo sai con te hon mot canh bao bi
  danh dau.
- Nguong cua lenh cho la khong gian BAO GIA: `processOrders` khop STOP_LOSS voi bar
  ngay THO, nen `o.threshold` la so USD du no nam trong tai khoan nao. Tach ra
  `orderStops` rieng, chi tron vao `stops` khi dong da la USD; dong khong phai USD thi
  giu lai VA them mot dong `warn` — de viec giu lai nhin thay duoc, khong im lang.
- Dong `warn` EUR doi chu thanh "gia va muc cat lo dang luu bang EUR", vi cau cu ("go
  vao bang EUR") chinh la cach hieu sai da sinh ra loi.

### C. Kiem

- 6 test moi, file len 24 xanh: dong EUR-quy-doi-tu-USD, truong hop khong co ty gia,
  tai khoan USD co ty gia, MIXED khi cung mot ma nam o mot tai khoan EUR va mot USD,
  lenh cho bi giu lai o dong khong USD, lenh cho van tron vao dong USD.
- Da chung minh test ghim dung loi: stash RIENG `positionsDigest.ts` thi file bao
  3 failed | 21 passed — dung ba test hanh vi moi.
- 647/648 test pass. Test do van la `syncMerge.test.ts > writes the timestamp map once
  per batch` (timeout 5000ms khi chay ca suite, 8/8 khi chay rieng) — co truoc thay doi
  nay, xem CHAT-18.

### D. Trien khai — khong can sua gi tren VM

- VM chi DOC `scanner:positions`; nhan dung hay sai deu la do app ghi. Nen khong co
  buoc nao tren VM lan nay.
- Nhung gia tri da sua chi len toi key do o lan LUU PORTFOLIO DAU TIEN sau khi app moi
  duoc deploy. `positionsFeed.ts` bo qua khi body khong doi, ma `lastBody` la bien
  module — nap lai trang la quen, nen lan luu dau tien sau deploy se day.
- Va noi ro hau qua cho nguoi dung: sau ban sua nay, cac vi the o tai khoan EUR se bi
  DANH DAU la khong trong duoc, thay vi duoc trong o muc sai. Muon co canh bao cat lo
  trong phien cho ma My: hoac de lenh khop o mot tai khoan USD, hoac dat mot lenh cho
  STOP_LOSS (nguong cua lenh la khong gian bao gia).

## CHAT-20 — App day ty gia len cung anh chup vi the, de VM quy doi duoc muc cat lo EUR

Luot nay bat dau bang mot cau hoi dung: "nhung ban than trong tai khoan cua minh co
phan chuyen doi currency roi ma nhi". Co — nhung co BA thu tien te khac nhau trong app,
va CHAT-19 noi ve mot thu khac voi thu nguoi dung dang nghi:

- `st.account.currency` — don vi LUU. Viet cung `'EUR'` o `portfolioTab.ts:751`, khong
  co giao dien nao doi duoc no.
- `displayCurrency` (`:207`, ham `toDisplay()` o `:218`, nut o `:500`) — CHI de hien.
  Doi no khong lam thay doi mot byte nao trong store. Day la cai nguoi dung dang nghi
  toi khi noi "tai khoan se chuyen qua USD".
- `lot.priceCurrency` / `#b-price-ccy` — don vi cua CON SO VUA GO. Cho quy doi that su
  la luc GHI (`portfolioTab.ts:1075-1080`), va `priceCurrency = priceCcy` duoc gan o
  `:1072` TRUOC phep chia roi khong bao gio duoc sua lai. Do dung la loi CHAT-19.

Nen sau CHAT-19, ket qua dung nhung khong du: vi the o tai khoan EUR duoc DANH DAU la
khong trong duoc — thay vi bi trong sai o muc lech 17%. Ma tai khoan luu bang EUR la
mac dinh, nen "khong trong duoc" ap cho gan nhu MOI vi the. `stop_only` mat gan het y
nghia. Luot nay lam not phan con lai: cho VM quy doi duoc.

### A. Quyet dinh: APP gui ty gia, VM quy doi — khong phai VM tu lay

- yfinance co `EURUSD=X`, de VM tu lay thi de hon nhieu va khong phai sua app.
- Nhung luc do muc cat lo trong tin nhan Telegram duoc tinh bang MOT TY GIA KHAC voi
  ty gia ma bang Portfolio dang hien. Nguoi doc co HAI con so cho cung mot muc stop, va
  khong co cach nao biet so nao dung. Mot nguon thi sai cung sai o mot cho.
- Nen anh chup mang them: `"fx": { "eurUsd": 1.1725, "asOf": "2026-09-28" }`.

### B. `asOf` la bat buoc, va ty gia cu thi TU CHOI chu khong "co con hon khong"

- Key `scanner:positions` duoc ghi theo SU KIEN (luc luu portfolio). Mot so khong ai
  cham ba thang van mang theo ty gia ba thang tuoi. Quy doi bang no la dung lai DUNG cai
  loi ma ca co che nay sinh ra de tranh: mot muc tu tin nhung sai.
- Nen ben scanner co knob moi `config.INTRADAY["pos_fx_stale_d"] = 7`. Day la HAN SU
  DUNG THAT, khac `pos_stale_h` (30 gio) — cai do chi lam tin nhan NOI la cu va khong
  bao gio bo du lieu di.
- Phia app: `fx` VANG MAT khoi anh chup khi khong co ty gia, va khong bao gio la mot so
  1 lam day — `1` co nghia "EUR va USD la cung mot so", do la mot loi im lang.

### C. Sua gi trong repo nay

- `fx.ts`: them `latestEurUsdAsOf()` tra `{rate, date}`. Truoc day module chi giu
  `latest` (mot so) va mot map theo ngay; nay giu ca `latestDate`. Cai NGAY moi la phan
  cho phep nguoi doc ngoai app ket luan "ty gia nay qua cu, khong dung".
- `positionsDigest.ts`: kieu moi `PositionsFx {eurUsd, asOf}`, tham so thu ba
  `fx?: {rate, date} | null`, va `fx` chi duoc gan khi CO dong can no (`eur > 0`).
  - MIXED CO Y khong phai ly do de gui ty gia: dong do co so nam o hai don vi, khong co
    MOT don vi de quy doi TU do. Canh bao cua no la "phai tach tai khoan".
  - Canh bao cua EUR doi theo: co ty gia thi noi ra ty gia va ngay; khong co thi noi
    "chua co ty gia de gui kem".
- `positionsFeed.ts`: truyen `latestEurUsdAsOf()` vao, va `bodyOf()` dua `fx` vao phan
  so sanh — mot ty gia moi LA thong tin moi voi nguoi doc. Vi `fx` chi xuat hien khi co
  dong EUR, viec nay khong the lam mot so toan USD bi ghi lai moi lan ty gia nhay.
- `index.ts`: xuat `PositionsFx`.

### D. Kiem

- `positionsDigest.test.ts` len 28 xanh, 4 test moi: gui ty gia kem ngay khi co dong can
  no (`stops` GOC khong doi); khong co ty gia thi noi ra va khong bao gio gui so 1 lam
  day (lap qua `null`, `undefined`, `rate:0`, `NaN`, `date:''`); so toan USD khong bi
  ty gia lam body doi; MIXED khong duoc gui ty gia.
- `apps/desktop/tests/positionsFeed.test.ts` — FILE MOI, 3 test: ty gia den duoc
  payload cung ngay; vang mat khi chua nap ty gia; va `'ok' -> 'skip' -> 'queued'` khi
  CHI ty gia doi (chung minh `bodyOf` that su nhin `fx`).
- 654/655 workspace. Test do van la `syncMerge.test.ts > writes the timestamp map once
  per batch` (timeout 5000ms trong ca suite, 8/8 khi chay rieng) — xem CHAT-18/19.
- `tsc --noEmit` sach, `npm run build` sach.
- Cai bay da sap mot lan: test app moi bao warn CU va `d.fx` undefined, vi app resolve
  `@screener/core` ve `packages/core/dist`. Phai `npm run build` trong `packages/core`
  moi thay thay doi cua core.

### E. Trien khai — CO thu tu, va can ca hai ben

1. Deploy app (Pages) — buoc nay dang bi chan, xem ghi chu ve `wrangler pages deploy`.
2. LUU PORTFOLIO mot lan sau khi deploy. Chua luu thi key `scanner:positions` cu van
   chua co truong `fx`, va VM se xu su y nhu truoc (danh dau khong trong duoc). Vi
   `lastBody` la bien module, nap lai trang la quen, nen lan luu dau tien sau deploy
   chac chan se day.
3. Ben VM: `git pull` trong repo scanner roi restart `watchd`. Hai ben KHONG phu thuoc
   thu tu nhau: VM ban moi doc anh chup cu thi khong quy doi (khong chet), app ban moi
   gui `fx` cho VM ban cu thi truong do bi bo qua.
4. Sau do ty gia phai duoc lam moi it nhat 7 ngay mot lan — tuc la mo app mot lan moi
   tuan. Qua han thi tin nhan mo phien NOI RA va noi cach sua, chu khong im lang.

## CHAT-21 — Chon Setup la he thong tu dat cat lo, muc tieu va so co

Yeu cau: "khi minh chon co phieu va gia va chon Setup thi system nen generate luon
stop and target ... position size dua vao so cash con lai ... dua vao phan swing
playbook ... va minh thich co mot phan configure place de change nhung con so default".

Ba quyet dinh da chon truoc khi viet mot dong nao:
- Pham vi vong nay: core + form Buy + trang cau hinh. Trade Planner GIU NGUYEN.
- Trang thai thi truong: tinh bo 4 trang thai CUA CAM NANG tren SPY, khong dung
  `detectRegime()` 3 trang thai san co.
- Rui ro mac dinh moi lenh: tu nhan tu so lenh da dong, van cho phep ghi de.

### A. Vi sao lai co HAM TRANG THAI THI TRUONG THU HAI

`momentum/marketRegime.ts` da phan loai thi truong roi, nhung thanh 3 trang thai
(BULL / TRANSITION / BEAR) tu chong EMA cua QQQ. Thang co vi the cua cam nang lai
duoc danh chi muc bang mot bo 4 KHAC: SPY so voi MA50/MA200 cong do doc MA50.

Anh 3 sang 4 nghia la phai BAY RA cai phan biet con thieu — va cai bi thieu dung la
cai thang can: `UPTREND_UNDER_STRESS` (MA50 con tren MA200 nhung gia da mat MA50) la
truong hop giam NUA co. Nen `planning/playbookRegime.ts` KHONG phai ban trung lap:
`detectRegime` tra loi "thi truong co dang bat cho cac bo loc momentum khong", ham moi
tra loi "hom nay duoc phep giao dich trang nao cua cam nang, va voi co nao". Hai cau
tra loi duoc phep khac nhau, va gop lai thi mot trong hai phai sai.

Vi sao SMA khong phai EMA: cam nang viet MA50/MA200, va moi bieu do nguoi dung doi
chieu (TradingView mac dinh) hieu do la trung binh gian don. EMA lech vai phan muoi
phan tram — dung ngay o cho ranh gioi phan loai bi lat.

Vi sao tra `null` chu khong phai `RANGE` khi thieu du lieu: can 210 nen (200 cho MA,
10 cho do doc). `RANGE` la mot trang thai THAT va no giam nua co. Tra `RANGE` cho
"toi khong co du lieu" la am tham giam nua moi lenh trong khi man hinh bao la thi
truong di ngang.

Vi sao `atrRatio` la `null` chu khong phai `1.0` khi chua co moc 100 phien: so 1.0 lap
cho doc thanh "bien dong binh thuong", va nhu vay se am tham cho vao FULL size trong
mot thi truong chua he do. Cung ho ly le voi viec `fx` khong bao gio mac dinh la 1
(CHAT-20).

Mot loi da sap NGAY TRONG luc viet test: dieu kien chu cua cam nang ("MA50 > MA200 va
gia < MA50") doc nguyen van thi mot thi truong da tao dinh tu nhieu thang truoc se bi
goi la "tang nhung dang cang" MOI NGAY gia dong cua thap hon mot cai MA50 phang lan.
Do la di ngang — va cam nang giao dich di ngang khac han (dao chieu trung binh moi duoc
phep, pha canh thi thoi). Nen nhanh do doi thom do doc con duong: "dang cang" nghia la
nhip tang CON hieu luc (50 tren 200 VA con len) va gia vua mat no. Co mot test hoi quy
rieng ten han hoi cho chuyen nay.

### B. Vi sao bang luat nam trong core, va vi sao chi luu PHAN KHAC BIET

Hai cho phai tra loi "cat lo dat dau, mua bao nhieu co": form Buy, va Trade Planner
(`buildTradePlan`). Neu moi cho tu mang mot ban luat thi Planner goi 3R con form dien
2R cho cung mot thiet lap, va nguoi dung khong co cach nao biet cai nao moi la luat.
Cung ly do `fx.ts` da duoc tach ra khoi `portfolioTab.ts`.

Trang cau hinh chi luu nhung o KHAC mac dinh. Ghi lai ca bang khi bam Luu se dong bang
ban luat cua phien nay vao du lieu dong bo mai mai: mot sua doi sau nay cho, vi du, so
phien cua Surge se khong bao gio den duoc nguoi dung, va khong con cach nao phan biet
con so ho CHON voi con so ho chi NHIN THAY roi bam Luu. "Ve mac dinh" vi the la mot
phep XOA, khong phai mot phep ghi.

`pf_playbook_cfg` DONG BO (la luat nguoi dung go — de tren mot may thi laptop goi 3R,
dien thoai goi 2R, khong man hinh nao noi tai sao). `pf_spy_bars` thi KHONG dong bo (du
lieu thi truong: giong nhau tren moi may, tai lai bang mot request) — da them vao
`LOCAL_ONLY_PREFIXES` canh `pf_eurusd_bars`.

### C. Ba luat khong duoc lam mem

1. CAT LO DAT THEO CAU TRUC, KHONG THEO TIEN. Khi cat lo theo cau truc ra xa hon thuoc
   do ATR, module CANH BAO va nguoi dung giam so co — KHONG keo cat lo lai gan. Keo lai
   la dich cat lo vi ly do tien, tuc la dung cai loi cam nang xep dau bang.
2. EMA10 LA MUC KEO THEO, KHONG PHAI CAT LO BAN DAU. Yeu cau ban dau de nghi breakout
   dung EMA10 hom truoc lam cat lo; cam nang (muc 13) dat EMA10 o cot "keo theo", con
   cat lo ban dau la duoi NEN pha. EMA10 nam gan gia hon nhieu, dung lam cat lo ban dau
   se thoi phong so co va bi quet som. Da ma hoa ca hai, moi cai dung vai cua no
   (`trailEma` rieng, `anchor` rieng).
3. DUOI 2R THI CANH, KHONG TU DONG DIEN. Cam nang: "duoi 2R thi bo qua, bat ke mau hinh
   dep den dau". `belowMinRR` la mot canh bao, khong phai mot phep nhan len cho du 2R.

Mot cach doc can noi ro: cat lo breakout cua cam nang la "duoi diem pha", ma diem pha
cung chinh la diem vao. Doc nguyen van thi do la cat lo cach 0,1% — khong ai y nhu vay.
Cach doc thuc dung (va la cach da chon) la DAY CUA NEN da pha.

Bay thiet lap co bang luat. VCP / Breakout / Pullback / Mean Reversion ghi `playbook`
vi cam nang noi ro. EP / Surge / Other ghi `derived` — cam nang khong cho con so, day
la suy ra. Nhan `source` ton tai de sau nay con biet cai nao sua duoc thoai mai.

### D. "Mot lenh da dong" la mot LO ban het, khong phai mot dong ban

Thang rui ro dem so lenh da dong (duoi 50 lenh: 0,25%; ky vong duong: 0,5%; 100 lenh
on dinh: 1%). App thi ban tung phan: chot nua o 2R roi phan con lai theo muc keo la
MOT lenh, nhung HAI dong `SellRecord`. Dem dong se cham moc "50 lenh" khi moi co 25
lenh that, len bac som mot nhip, va doc mot lan chot nua thanh mot chuoi hai lenh
thang (roi tha cai cat chuoi thua 3 lenh). Nen `closedTradePnls` gop theo `lotId` va
chi tinh lo nao het co.

Len bac con doi ky vong DUONG, khong chi doi so lenh: 200 lenh thua khong phai la
bang chung de nhan doi co.

Lo KHONG CO cat lo gop 0 vao tong rui ro dang mo — co y, va day la lua chon kho chiu.
Rui ro that cua no la CA vi the, nen tinh vao cung co ly. Nhung khi do mot so sach
toan lo khong cat lo se tu bao minh vuot han muc va chan moi lenh moi, tuc la day
nguoi dung den cho tat con so do di. Thieu cat lo da co canh bao rieng (o
`positionsDigest`), de no o day la du.

### E. Duong mach tien te — cho duy nhat core khong kiem duoc

Nen gia la USD tho. Von, tien va `PriceMap` la tien CUA TAI KHOAN (EUR). Gia trong form
la don vi cua o chon ben canh no. Sai mot chieu chia thi so co lech dung bang ty gia —
25% o muc 1,25 — am tham, moi lenh, voi mot con so trong rat binh thuong tren man hinh.

Nen: tinh muc gia trong khong gian USD, quy ve tien tai khoan DUNG MOT LAN o ranh gioi
(`portfolio/playbook.ts`), y nhu cach `prices.ts` da ghi: nguoi doc map gia can gia,
khong can ty gia. `tests/buyPlan.test.ts` co mot test rang $100 cat lo $90 va 80EUR
tren cung bo nen phai ra CUNG mot so co (15).

### F. Form Buy khong bao gio ghi de len so nguoi dung tu go

Mot o chi duoc dien khi no dang TRONG hoac dang giu dung goi y truoc do cua chinh no.
Da tu go cat lo thi goi y xuong thanh mot loi de nghi kem link "dung goi y". Auto-fill
de len mot cat lo go tay con te hon la khong co: nguoi dung vao lenh o muc ho DA TU
CHOI, va app trong nhu vua lam mat cai ho nhap.

Nguoc lai: mot so DO CHINH NO dien ma ke hoach moi khong con bao ve thi phai BI XOA. De
lai 250 co cua lan truoc sau khi han muc rui ro cat ke hoach ve 0 la vao dung cai lenh
ke hoach vua tu choi.

Vi sao tai nen gia o day chu khong o luc ve tab: cat lo can nen gia ngay cua mot ma tai
khoan co the chua he giu. Chon Setup la hanh dong "len ke hoach cho lenh nay" — dung
luc de ton mot request, va `wirePriceHint` da tai o cung cai cu chi do. Ve tab thi
`ensureRegime` KHONG co `refresh`, tuc la chi doc cache: mo mot tab khong duoc phep bat
dau tai du lieu thi truong (luat `ensureEurUsd` da theo).

### G. File nao doi

- `packages/core/src/planning/playbookRegime.ts` — MOI. 4 trang thai tren SPY, `atrRatioOf`.
- `packages/core/src/planning/setupPlaybook.ts` — MOI. Bang luat 7 thiet lap, thang rui
  ro, `suggestLevels`, `suggestSize`, `openRiskOf`, `closedTradePnls`.
- `packages/core/src/planning/index.ts` — xuat hai file tren, kem ghi chu la co Y khong
  phai `detectRegime`.
- `apps/desktop/src/portfolio/playbook.ts` — MOI. Cau hinh (dong bo), cache SPY (cuc
  bo), `buildBuyPlan` va ranh gioi tien te.
- `apps/desktop/src/ui/playbookSettings.ts` — MOI. Trang cau hinh; luu PHAN KHAC BIET.
- `apps/desktop/src/tabs/portfolioTab.ts` — nut cau hinh trong the Buy, `wireBuyPlan`,
  nap cau hinh + trang thai thi truong luc ve, lam moi SPY trong `update()`.
- `apps/desktop/src/adapters/storage.ts` — `pf_spy_bars` vao `LOCAL_ONLY_PREFIXES`.

### H. Kiem

- `tests/planning/playbookRegime.test.ts` MOI, 12 test.
  `tests/planning/setupPlaybook.test.ts` MOI, 54 test.
  `apps/desktop/tests/buyPlan.test.ts` MOI, 13 test.
- 733/734 workspace. Test do van la `syncMerge.test.ts > writes the timestamp map once
  per batch` (timeout 5000ms trong ca suite, 8/8 khi chay rieng) — xem CHAT-18/19/20.
- `tsc --noEmit` sach ca hai goi, `npm run build` sach.
- Bay cu, lai gan sap lan nua: phai `npm run build` trong `packages/core` TRUOC khi
  chay test ben app, vi app resolve `@screener/core` ve `packages/core/dist`.
- Bon test da that su that bai truoc khi dung, ghi lai vi ba trong so do la loi FIXTURE
  chu khong phai loi code — nen doc ky truoc khi sua code: cat lo rong hon ATR khong no
  (nen fixture rong 30 diem nen 2xATR con rong hon), so co ra 250 thay vi 333 (bi chan
  boi tran 25% von, khong phai boi lam tron), va DOWNTREND thay vi UPTREND_UNDER_STRESS
  (fixture roi 72 diem, xuong duoi ca MA200). Cai thu tu — di ngang bi goi la "dang
  cang" — la loi CODE that, va la ly do co nhanh do doc o muc A.

### I. Con lai cho vong sau (da HOAN LAI co y, khong phai bo quen)

- Trade Planner / `buildTradePlan` chua dung bang luat moi — van mu ve trang thai thi
  truong va ve thiet lap. Day la phan "ung dung vao trade planner nua" cua yeu cau.
- (DA LAM, xem muc J) Duong tu chuong cam nang sang trang cau hinh.

### J. Duong tu cuon cam nang sang trang cau hinh

Muc 12 (tinh size) va muc 13 (thoat lenh) moi muc gio co mot khung "Nhung con so nay
sua duoc" kem nut mo thang cau hinh.

Vi sao can: nguoi doc vua duoc day "chot nua o 2R" khong co cach nao biet so 2 la MOT
MAC DINH chu khong phai mot luat, va cang khong biet no nam o dau. Va cuon sach la cho
DUY NHAT giai thich vi sao tung con so la nhu vay — nen no cung la cho dung de moi nguoi
doi con so do. Mot trang cai dat vao tu thanh cong cu thi bi doi theo cam tinh.

Cach noi hai ben: tab Learn KHONG co `AppContext` (`renderLearn()` khong nhan tham so
nao), ma hop thoai thi can ctx de doc/ghi cau hinh. Nen cuon sach chi DAT YEU CAU
(`requestPlaybookSettings`) roi doi hash sang `#portfolio`; tab Portfolio — cho co ctx
— nhan yeu cau do sau khi `draw` xong va mo hop thoai. Dung hash vi do la duong di giua
cac tab co san cua app (`tabFromHash` -> `show` -> render), khong phai them ong dan moi,
va Back tra nguoi doc ve dung chuong ho dang doc.

Yeu cau la MOT LAN (`takePlaybookSettingsRequest` xoa co ngay khi doc). Co ma nam mai
thi hop thoai se bat lai moi lan nguoi dung tro ve tab Portfolio trong ca phien, khong
man hinh nao noi tai sao. Co mot test rieng cho tinh mot-lan nay.

Mo sau `draw`, khong truoc: dong hop thoai thi nguoi dung o lai mot tab da ve xong, chu
khong phai mot tab trong.

O man hinh Tong quan hop thoai mo voi tai khoan `null`: "so lenh cua anh" khong co cau
tra loi duy nhat khi dang xem nhieu tai khoan, nen hop thoai NOI RA thay vi tu chon mot
tai khoan ho. Phan luat van sua binh thuong — luat la chung, khong theo tai khoan.

File doi: `tabs/swingPlaybook.ts` (khung tro + wiring), `portfolio/playbook.ts` (co mot
lan), `tabs/portfolioTab.ts` (nhan yeu cau sau `draw`), `tests/buyPlan.test.ts` (+1).

### K. Vi sao thu o may that thi KHONG tu dien gi (loi that, da sua)

Chay local, chon Setup, dien gia, so co, xep hang — khong co cat lo hay muc tieu nao
duoc dien. Khong phai loi tinh toan: **cai nghe su kien sai**.

`#b-price` chi duoc nghe su kien `change`. `change` cua mot o text chi no khi nguoi dung
TU GO roi roi khoi o. Nhung duong nap gia thuong dung nhat la link "use" o dong goi y
gia: no gan `.value` roi tu phat su kien `input` (gan `.value` bang code khong tu phat
su kien nao). Tuc la dung cai cu chi ma nhieu nguoi lam nhat thi ke hoach KHONG bao gio
chay. Trong ma con mot dong ghi chu noi ro "gia den tu link use, no phat `input`" — ngay
ben canh doan nghe `change`. Ghi chu dung, ma nghe sai.

Da sua: nghe CA `input` (tre 250ms vi `input` no theo tung ky tu) VA `change`.

Hai thu di kem, vi loi nay am den muc khong the bao cao duoc:
- Khi con thieu truong, o goi y gio NOI RA dang cho gi ("Chon Loai thiet lap…", "Dang
  cho ma va gia…") thay vi xoa trang. Truoc do, "dang cho ban" va "tinh nang khong
  chay" nhin giong nhau y het.
- Moi loi trong `run()` truoc day thanh unhandled rejection — tren man hinh khong khac
  gi tinh nang khong ton tai. Gio bat va in ra dong goi y.
- Va goi loi moi mot lan luc ve form, nen nguoi dung lan dau BIET la co tinh nang nay.

Khong co test cho phan nay: cau hinh test cua app la `environment: 'node'` co y (xem
dau file `vitest.config.ts`), va them `jsdom` se doi `package-lock.json` — dieu khong
duoc phep vi `npm ci` cua Cloudflare. Nen day dung la khoang trong da de loi nay ra
duoc: moi phan tinh toan deu co test, con day dan su kien thi khong.

## CHAT-22 — Trade Planner dung chung bang luat: Setup, xep hang, tai khoan, bieu do va Ghi chu

Yeu cau: "for the watchlist, the trade plan, we should have a dropdown to choose the
setup and rating as well ... Rating will decide how position size is: A will be full, B
will be 75%, C will be 50%, D will be only 25%. This should be also configurable. And in
the Trade Plan we should be able to choose the account, so that the system can
automatically base on the cash available and decide how 100% position size should be
(market regime, chosen setup) ... and based on rating to decide on the size, and then the
share, the Entry Price is adjustable to automatically change the stop and target ... In
the trade plan we will see then also how does it look like in the graph (entry, stop,
target, ...) and Note will additionally explain also those figures, risk, and so on
(explanation will come with market regime, setup, rating ...)".

Day chinh la phan da HOAN LAI co y o CHAT-21 muc I ("Trade Planner chua dung bang luat
moi"). Vong nay tra no.

### A. Vi sao Planner goi `buildBuyPlan` chu khong tu tinh size

CHAT-21 muc B da noi ly do: hai cho tra loi "cat lo dat dau, mua bao nhieu co" thi mot
trong hai se sai va nguoi dung khong co cach nao biet cai nao moi la luat. Truoc vong
nay Planner van co phep nhan size RIENG cua no (% von), tuc la dung cai loi do.

Gio Planner goi DUNG `buildBuyPlan` cua form Buy. `buildTradePlan` / `explainPlan` VAN
duoc goi — nhung cho mot cau hoi KHAC: "thiet lap co o day khong, diem nay co dang mua
khong" (`qualityScore`, `actionable`, va muc gia khoi diem). Phan biet nay dang ghi lai
vi no khong hien ra tu ten ham: mot cai xet CO NEN vao, cai kia xet vao BAO NHIEU.

Van con che do % von, nhung gio la mot LUA CHON hien tren the ("Playbook" hay 5/10/15/
20/25%) chu khong phai duong duy nhat. Bo han se lay mat cong cu cua nguoi da quen no.

### B. Xep hang nhan o BEN TRONG `riskBudget`, khong o ngoai

Cho nhan quan trong hon ve mat hien thi. Xep hang nhan sau `losingStreak` va TRUOC san
`minRiskPct`.

Neu nhan o ngoai: bac hoc viec 0,25% gap hang D (25%) ra 0,0625% — duoi san 0,1%. Ham se
tra ve `pct` da duoc san do LAM TRON, roi minh moi nhan tiep, va man hinh se in ra ca
`flooredAtMin` ("da chan o muc san") LAN mot con so nam duoi cai san do. Hai cau dung
theo tung ve, gop lai thi vo ly, va nguoi dung khong co cach nao biet cai nao that.

Nhan ben trong: 0,25% -> 0,0625% -> san keo len 0,1%, `cuts` co CA `rating` LAN
`flooredAtMin`, va so co > 0. Co mot test ten han hoi ly le nay.

BO TRONG XEP HANG = SIZE DAY. `ratingScale(null) === 1`. Cau hinh vo nghia (am, khong
phai so) cung tra 1. Mot o chon de trong ma am tham cat con 1/4 se day sai bai hoc: xep
hang la mot ky luat duoc MOI vao, khong phai mot cai cong chan.

Xep hang KHONG dau duoc lenh veto downtrend. Mot hang A trong ngay downtrend van ra 0
co. Cung ly le CHAT-21 muc C: to bao nhieu va co nen vao hay khong la HAI luat.

### C. Vi sao trang cau hinh ghi CA BON hang hoac khong hang nao

`ladderConfig()` gop MOT CAP: `{...DEFAULT_RISK_LADDER, ...cfg.ladder}`. Nen mot
`ratingPct` da luu se THAY THE ca ban ghi mac dinh, khong phai gop vao no.

Nghia la neu chi ghi chu KHAC mac dinh — dung nguyen tac "chi luu phan khac biet" cua
CHAT-21 muc B — thi ba hang con lai ve `undefined`, `ratingScale` doc thanh "khong co
xep hang", va mot hang C se am tham duoc len ke hoach o size DAY. Nen: he co MOT hang
khac mac dinh thi ghi DU bon. Ly le nay nam ngay tai cho ghi trong
`playbookSettings.ts`, vi doc code ma khong biet ham gop chi sau mot cap thi "chi luu
phan khac biet" trong nhu dung.

### D. Mot tai khoan gia, khong phai mot nhanh code thu hai

Planner cho chon tai khoan (thi lay tien va von that) HOAC go von tay. Duong go tay
khong co nhanh tinh rieng: no dung mot `AccountState` DUNG RA, `initialCapital` la so
nguoi dung go, khong lo, khong lenh da dong.

Khong lenh da dong nghia la bac HOC VIEC (0,25%) — va do dung la cau tra loi cua cam
nang cho mot nguoi chua co so sach. Nhung dieu do phai NOI RA, nen dong trang thai in
`wl.plan.manualnote`. Am tham thi nguoi dung se thay so co nho hon minh tuong va khong
biet tai sao.

Mot loi THAT da tim thay khi viet lai: canh bao "khong du tien" truoc day so voi VON,
khong so voi TIEN. Tuc la mot tai khoan da giai ngan het van im lang — dung cai luc canh
bao co nghia. Gio so voi `computeCash`.

`prices.ts` them `accountPrices(accId)` / `hasPrices(accId)` de goi ham co the noi "toi
chua co gia". `computePositionsValue` roi ve `lot.buyPrice`, nen `prices: {}` ra von
theo GIA GOC — mot con so dung du, nhung phai ghi ro tren man hinh (`wl.plan.costbasis`)
vi no khong phai von theo gia thi truong.

### E. Duong mach tien te — lan thu hai, va lan nay nguoc chieu

CHAT-21 muc E: nen gia USD, tien tai khoan EUR. Trong Planner con mot nhip nua: `planConv`
cu GIA DINH dau vao la USD, nhung so no nhan thuc te la tien TAI KHOAN (`BuyPlan.size.*`,
`BuyPlan.equity`, `computeCash` deu la tien tai khoan). Da viet lai dung chieu, va them
`usdToAcct` cho chieu con lai (muc gia USD -> tien tai khoan).

Vi the `planLines` nhan HAI ky hieu tien: `levelSym` cho MUC GIA (cat lo, muc tieu) va
`moneySym` cho TIEN (rui ro, gia tri vi the). Trong form Buy hai cai la MOT o nen truyen
cung mot ky hieu; trong Planner thi khac nhau — muc gia USD canh tien EUR. Mot ky hieu
dung cho ca hai se in sai gia bang dung ty gia trong khi phep tinh van dung.

### F. Vi sao tach `portfolio/planWords.ts`

Core tra ve MA (`'contractionLow'`, `'heatExceeded'`, `'rating'`); app dat cau. Truoc
vong nay bang chu nam trong `portfolioTab.ts`, nen Planner muon giai thich cung mot ke
hoach thi phai co ban thu hai — va hai ban chu la cach mot con so co hai loi giai thich.
Cung y le da tach `fx.ts` va da dat bang luat vao core.

`planLines` tra ve MOT MANG CHUOI chu khong phai HTML: form Buy noi bang `<br>`, cai the
trong Planner con nhet them phan `explain` cua scanner vao sau. Tra ve HTML da dinh la
tra ve mot bo tri.

Thu tu dong co y: cat lo + muc tieu -> so co / rui ro / bac / do nong -> XEP HANG (mot
dong rieng, vi day la dau vao duy nhat app khong kiem duoc) -> danh sach phep cat ->
trang thai thi truong -> canh bao muc gia -> canh bao size -> ghi chu "luat suy ra".
Nguoi doc dung sau hai dong dau van da doc duoc cat lo va so co.

Phan lon test cua `planWords.test.ts` la KIEM DU, khong kiem cau chu: mot `StopAnchor`
moi trong core ma thieu o trong `ANCHOR_MEANS` se in `undefined` ngay canh gia cat lo —
dung cai so khong duoc phep trong nhu loi. Va mot test doi hai ngon ngu ra CUNG so dong:
mot ban dich lam roi mot canh bao se giau no khoi dung nguoi can nhat.

### G. Vi sao ve nen thay vi ve mot thanh ngang

`ui/miniChart.ts` da co `candleChart(cfg)`, nen khong them thu vien (them phu thuoc la
doi `package-lock.json`, dieu `npm ci` cua Cloudflare khong cho). Hai chi tiet dang ghi:
thang gia tinh CA `levels`, nen mot muc tieu xa se lam nen NHO lai chu khong roi ra ngoai
khung; va mau dung bien CSS nen bieu do theo giao dien sang/toi san co.

`QM_TO_SETUP` anh `QmSetupType` sang `SetupKey`. `BOTH` -> `'VCP'`, vi VCP la dong CHAT
hon trong bang luat: khi hai cach doc cung mot do thi khong dong y ve viec cho cat lo bao
nhieu cho, lay vi the NHO hon la cai sai con cuu duoc.

### H. Go phim vao o Entry khong duoc lam mat con tro

`recalcPlan` KHONG ve lai ca the. No ghi `.value` len nhung o dang khong duoc focus, va
thay MOI BA khung khong co listener (bieu do, dong suy ra, ghi chu). Ve lai ca the moi
lan go mot chu so se lam mat con tro sau ky tu dau tien.

Va no chi dien nhung o CON THUOC VE NO: da tu go cat lo thi `ownStop` bat, tu do ke
hoach khong ghi de nua — dung luat CHAT-21 muc F. Ghi chu tu sinh cung vay:
`noteEdited` bat sau khi nguoi dung sua ghi chu bang tay.

Khi chua chon Setup thi chua co ke hoach de doc von ra, nhung cac chip % va canh bao tien
van can mot so von — va phai dung CAI SO ke hoach se dung, khong duoc la mot so khac,
neu khong % vi the se nhay ngay luc chon Setup.

### I. File doi

- `packages/core/src/planning/setupPlaybook.ts` — `ConvictionRating`, `RATING_KEYS`,
  `isRating`, `ratingScale`; `ratingPct` trong `RiskLadderConfig` va mac dinh
  100/75/50/25; ma cat `'rating'`; `riskBudget` nhan `rating`.
- `packages/core/src/planning/index.ts` — xuat nhung cai tren.
- `apps/desktop/src/portfolio/playbook.ts` — `BuyPlanInput.rating`, `BuyPlan.rating`,
  `pinnedBudget` nhan xep hang ma van giu veto downtrend.
- `apps/desktop/src/portfolio/planWords.ts` — MOI. Bang chu dung chung, `planLines`.
- `apps/desktop/src/tabs/portfolioTab.ts` — xoa nam bang chu cuc bo, doc + nghe
  `#b-rating`.
- `apps/desktop/src/ui/playbookSettings.ts` — phan "Co theo xep hang", chu cho phep cat
  `rating`, ghi ca bon hang; `setupName` chuyen sang `planWords.ts`.
- `apps/desktop/src/tabs/miscTabs.ts` — viet lai Planner: hai o chon, chon tai khoan,
  Entry keo theo cat lo/muc tieu/size, bieu do nen, Ghi chu tu sinh.
- `apps/desktop/src/ui/i18n.ts` — 12 khoa moi. `src/styles.css` — `.tp-playfields`,
  `.tp-chart`.

### J. Kiem

- `tests/planning/setupPlaybook.test.ts` 63/63 (+9). `tests/buyPlan.test.ts` 20/20 (+7).
  `tests/planWords.test.ts` MOI 7/7.
- 756/757 workspace. Test do van la `syncMerge.test.ts > writes the timestamp map once
  per batch` — xem CHAT-18/19/20/21.
- `tsc --noEmit` sach ca hai goi, `npm run build` sach ca hai.
- Mot test SAI ky vong cua chinh minh, dang ghi lai vi no day lai code: da tuong mot thi
  truong RANGE voi `atrRatio: 1.6` se bi cat CA `volExpanded` LAN `regimeRange`. Doc lai
  `setupPlaybook.ts` thi `volExpanded` chi ap dung khi `regime === 'UPTREND'` — trong mot
  thi truong di ngang thi phep giam nua VI di ngang da noi dung dieu do roi. Sua ky vong,
  khong sua code.
- Van khong co test cho phan day day su kien: `environment: 'node'` co y, va them jsdom
  se doi `package-lock.json` (CHAT-21 muc K). Nen cai gi tinh duoc thi tach ra thanh ham
  thuan — `planLines` chinh la vi the.

## CHAT-23 — Hang tu cham diem theo tieu chi, va loi "hang doi ma so co khong doi"

Ba viec nguoi dung yeu cau: (1) hang phai lam doi CO VI THE cuoi cung, khong phai chi doi
% rui ro; (2) bieu do trong Planner phai la bieu do nen thuc nhu o phan co phieu rieng le;
(3) thay vi tu chon hang A-D, tich vao cac tieu chi va de hang tu tinh ra — cho khach quan.

### A. Loi that: hang doi ma so co khong doi

Nguoi dung bao: "hien tai thi toi chua thay shares, hay position thay doi". Day la LOI,
khong phai thieu tinh nang.

CHAT-22 muc B da dat phep nhan cua hang vao trong `riskBudget`, tuc la nhan vao % rui ro.
Nhung % rui ro chi la MOT trong BON gioi han cua mot vi the, trong `suggestSize`:

    shares = min(byRisk, byCash, byCap, byHeat)

`byCap` la gioi han tap trung 25% von. Voi mot cat lo hep — dung la truong hop cua mot
thiet lap tot — thi `byRisk` rat lon va `byCap` moi la cai quyet dinh. Khi do nhan hang
vao `byRisk` khong doi ket qua MOT CHUT NAO: `min` van chon `byCap`. O chon hang di
chuyen, so co dung im.

Nen lan nay DAO LAI quyet dinh cua CHAT-22 muc B: hang ra khoi `riskBudget`, `'rating'`
bi xoa khoi union `RiskCut`, va phep nhan chuyen xuong cuoi `suggestSize` — noi nao ap
dung cho BAT KY gioi han nao thang. Ly do nay duoc viet ngay tai cho quyet dinh, trong
phan dau `riskBudget`.

Da co test hoi quy truoc khi sua: `'SCALES A SIZE THAT A NON-RISK LIMIT DECIDED — the bug
that reached the user'`, voi `limitedBy === 'concentration'` va A=156 / B=117 / C=78 /
D=39.

### B. Tai sao san rui ro KHONG chan phan giam theo hang

`minRiskPct` ton tai de chan APP tu bop vi the ve gan 0 thay nguoi dung: che do thi
truong, chuoi lenh lo, bien do gian. Con hang la NGUOI DUNG tu chon nho lai.

Neu de san chan ca hang thi mot bac "dang hoc" 0.25% voi hang D se ra 0.1% (san) thay vi
0.0625% — tuc la 40% co day nhung tren nhan ghi 25%. Sai nghiem trong hon la khong giam.

Nen tach hai loai co lai: phep cat cua thang rui ro bi san chan, phep giam theo hang thi
khong, va hai cai duoc bao cao RIENG. `budget.pct` van la % cua co day du; `planLines` in
rui ro THAT (`size.riskPctOfEquity`) va chi nhac `budget.pct` khi hai so lech >= 0.01.

### C. `floor`, khong phai `round`, va khong bao gio ve 0

    let shares = Math.floor(fullShares * gradeScale);
    if (shares < 1) shares = 1;

`round` se lam mot hang C to hon mot nua; `floor` ve 0 se bien "dat cuoc nho hon" thanh
"khong dat cuoc", mot loi tu choi ma hang KHONG duoc phep dua ra. Nhung `fullShares === 0`
van tra ve 0 co: thi truong giam, hoac tien khong du mot co, la cau tra loi khac.

### D. `limitedBy` khong co 'rating', va do la chu y

`limitedBy` mo ta CO DAY DU bi cai gi chan. Hang khong phai mot gioi han nguoi dung dam
phai — no la mot phan so nguoi dung tu xin. Ghi no vao day se lam mot co da chon trong
giong nhu mot co bi tu choi.

`SizeSuggestion` vi the mang them `fullShares`, `fullPositionValue`, `gradeScale`, de the
in ra duoc phep tru: "co day 250 ($25,000) -> 75% -> 187 sh". Do chinh la cach nguoi dung
dien dat, va la cach duy nhat de biet o chon hang co tac dung gi.

### E. Bieu do nen thuc, thay cho hinh SVG

Truoc la `candleChart`, hinh SVG tu ve dung cho cac hinh minh hoa trong cam nang. Dung
cho mot hinh sau nen bia dat, sai cho day: mot thiet lap thuc duoc danh gia bang cach
zoom vao nen, doc khoi luong so voi trung binh cua no, xem gia nam dau tren cac duong
trung binh ma phan con lai cua app hien. Mot hinh khong tuong tac duoc se day nguoi dung
di mo bieu do o cho khac — va do la luc ke hoach thoi khop voi cai ho dang nhin.

Hai viec phai lam de `drawCandles` song duoc trong Planner:

1. `recalcPlan` chay MOI LAN GO PHIM. Ve lai bieu do moi ky tu la mot canvas moi, va
   thang gia se nhay duoi tay nguoi dung. Nen them `setOverlay(o)` vao `CandleChart`:
   xoa ba duong gia cu bang `removePriceLine` (lightweight-charts khong co "xoa het") roi
   ve lai, giu nguyen nen, zoom va vi tri cuon.
2. `planCharts: Map<string, CandleChart>` va `destroyPlanCharts()` goi o dau
   `computePlans`. Bo node di la khong du — `ResizeObserver` se song lau hon bieu do no
   dang chinh, roi goi `applyOptions` tren mot doi tuong da bi huy.

Va trong `applyOverlay`: dieu kien la `price != null && price > 0`. O Entry go nua duong
tra ve 0, va mot duong gia o 0 lam sup ca thang gia — nen thanh mot vach phang.

### F. Bang tieu chi: tai sao cham diem thay vi tu chon

Hang A-D truoc day la mot o chon, tuc la dat cai dau vao QUAN TRONG NHAT cua co vi the —
no la phep nhan cuoi cung tren so co — vao trong tam trang nguoi dung. Khong ai cham
hang D cho y tuong cua chinh minh dung luc muon mua no.

Cach gioi giao dich xu ly viec nay khong phai hoi mot ket luan ma hoi mot BANG KIEM, cham
diem truoc khi tien vao thi truong. Ca bon tac gia ma app nay dung deu da cong bo bang
cua ho. Nen hang bay gio la mot diem tren cac tieu chi co ten. Tich mot o van la mot phan
xet, nhung la phan xet ve MOT dieu nguoi dung tra duoc tren bieu do — va phan lon cac o
thi app tich ho tu du lieu gia da co.

`packages/core/src/planning/tradeGrader.ts` — MOI.

### G. Trong so, va nguon cua tung tieu chi

Moi tieu chi co truong `authority` va khong phai phat minh cua module nay:

- Mark Minervini, *Trade Like a Stock Market Wizard* — Trend Template (gia tren MA50;
  MA50 > MA150 > MA200; MA200 di len; cach dinh 52 tuan khong qua 25%), va dau chan VCP:
  cac lan nen thu hep dan, khoi luong can dan vao diem mua.
- William O'Neil, *How to Make Money in Stocks* — CAN SLIM. Cac chu app do duoc la S
  (khoi luong), L (suc manh tuong doi >= 80), M (huong thi truong). Con C, A, I can du
  lieu co ban nen thanh CAU HOI THU CONG, khong phai bi bo qua im lang.
- Stan Weinstein, *Secrets for Profiting in Bull and Bear Markets* — phan tich giai doan:
  chi mua giai doan 2. Do la dieu ma thu tu cac duong trung binh dang kiem tra.
- Kristjan Kullamagi (Qullamaggie) — dieu kien tien de ma cac tac gia mau hinh gia dinh
  ma it noi ra: mot da tang truoc do lon (>= 30%), va mot nguong thanh khoan cung
  (>= 20 trieu $/ngay), vi mot cai nen hoan hao tren co phieu khong ra duoc khong phai
  mot thiet lap giao dich duoc. Cong voi luat EP: nhay khoang >= 10%, khoi luong >= 3 lan,
  dong cua o nua tren bien do, co nguyen nhan ro rang.
- Cam nang cua chinh app (`setupPlaybook.ts`) — 2R toi thieu, va cat lo cach diem vao
  khong qua 10% de no la mot MOC chu khong phai mot hy vong.

Trong so co y khong bang nhau: thi truong an 14 diem, vi O'Neil de chu M cuoi cung trong
acronym roi danh ca cuon sach noi rang no quyet dinh ket qua. Va thi truong duoc cham
BANG HAI tieu chi (`regimeUptrend` 8 + `regimeNotHostile` 6) de mot thi truong di ngang
duoc diem mot phan, con mot thi truong giam thi khong — van la o tick, nhung co bac.

Cac nguong nam trong `GRADE_BARS` nhu hang so co ten, KHONG dua vao hop thoai cau hinh:
tung cai la mot cau trich dan, va nguoi keo `RS_STRONG` ve 50 khong phai dang tinh chinh
bang kiem, ho dang xoa tieu chi cua O'Neil va giu lai ten ong. Cai CHO PHEP sua la vi tri
cac vach A/B/C (`GradeThresholds`) — do la cau hoi ve do kho tinh cua chinh nguoi dung.

### H. Chua tra loi thi ra KHOI CA HAI phia cua phan so

Mot cau chua tra loi khong phai mot cau tra loi sai. Neu tinh nam tieu chi thu cong bang
0 cho den khi duoc tich thi moi the mo ra da la hang D, va bang kiem tro thanh mot vat
can. Te hon: "toi chua xem" va "toi xem roi, va xau" se cho ra cung mot hang.

Nen mau so la nhung gi TRA LOI DUOC, va `unknownWeight` noi ro con bo lai bao nhieu. Gia
cua lua chon nay la thuc va duoc ghi ra: mot the chi biet cac tieu chi tu dong co the doc
ra A tren khong den mot nua tong trong so cua bang. Do la mot chu A *tren nhung bang chung
app co*, va vi the `unknownWeight` nam trong ket qua chu khong phai bien noi bo — the
duoc yeu cau hien no canh chu.

### I. Hai ho tieu chi: nen va nhay khoang

Mot VCP khong co ngay nhay khoang, va mot EP khong co nen nen dan. Cham diem ca hai ho se
tru diem moi lenh vi no khong phai lenh loai kia.

Nen `qmGradeEvidence` biet thiet lap nguoi dung chon, va chi dua qua ho tuong ung:
'VCP' / 'Breakout' / 'Pullback' -> ho nen; 'EP' / 'Surge' -> ho nhay khoang;
'Mean Reversion' / 'Other' -> KHONG ho nao, va do la cau tra loi trung thuc — bang kiem
nay lam tu van chuong theo xu huong, cac tac gia do se khong cham diem mot lenh hoi quy
tan goc. No van duoc cham tren xu huong, suc manh, thi truong, thanh khoan va co che rui
ro cua chinh no.

### J. So 0 cua may quet la "khong do duoc", khong phai "do duoc bang 0"

`detectVcp` tra ve mot ket qua toan 0 khi khong tim thay gi: `baseDepthPct: 0`,
`atrContractionPct: 0`, `volumeContractionPct: 0`. Doc nhu do luong thi mot co phieu
khong co nen bi cham la co CAI NEN TE — mot cau khac, va nang hon.

Nen cac truong phan tram di qua `nonZero` va im lang khi bang dung 0. So AM van duoc
cham: mot ATR GIAN ra trong nen la mot do luong, va la mot do luong that bai. Chi so 0
chan la nhap nhang.

Ngoai le co y: `pctBelow52wHigh` KHONG nam trong nhom nay — 0 o day nghia la "dang o dinh
52 tuan", la so doc tot nhat co the.

Dau hieu con lai de nguoi dung thay app va ho khong dong y ve mau hinh la `contractions`,
mot PHEP DEM, noi 0 la mot so doc that va dang bi phe.

### K. `MIN_GRADE_WEIGHT`: khong dat ten chu khi biet qua it

Chu cai nhan vao co vi the. Mot hang D tinh tu ba tieu chi se cat lenh con mot phan tu
tren gan nhu khong co thong tin gi. Nen khi `possible < 50` thi `grade` la `null` — va
`null` nghia la CHUA XEP HANG, tuc la co day du, khong phai co nho nhat. Diem van duoc
bao cao, the hien mot dong noi ro chua du du lieu de dat chu.

Con mot ly do nua de `grade` la `null` chu khong phai 'C' nhu ban dau: 'C' se am tham
chia doi moi vi the cua moi the chua chon thiet lap. `null` la dung quy uoc cu cua app
(`rating: null` = chua xep hang = co day du), nen khong them mot nghia thu hai.

### L. Tieu chi tu dong KHONG phai o tick — nhung van co o ghi de

Hai muoi mot tieu chi do tu du lieu gia hien ra la dau tich, dau cheo hoac gach ngang KEM
THEO SO, va khong sua duoc. Mot o tick ma nguoi dung bo tich duoc khi khong thich cau tra
loi thi dung lai chinh cai van de ma bang kiem duoc mang vao de thay the; no chi rai van
de do ra thanh hai muoi mot quyet dinh nho hon, moi cai trong nhu nhap lieu.

Nhung KHONG de cho cho su khong dong y thi te hon: nguoi dung khong dong y voi diem ma
khong noi duoc se di tich mot tieu chi ho khong tin, va luc do bang kiem VUA sai VUA
trong nhu khach quan. Nen co mot o ghi de duy nhat, hien ro, va the ghi nhan "da ghi de
(diem ra B)" — de loi noi doi khong nam trong cac tieu chi.

Cac cau hoi thu cong thi BA TRANG THAI, khong phai o tick: Co / Khong / chua tra loi, vi
mot o tick bo trong khong noi duoc nguoi dung da xem hay chua. Bam lai dung cau tra loi
dang chon se xoa no ve chua tra loi.

### M. Cam nang chay hai lan trong `cardGrade`

Hai tieu chi noi ve LENH nay chu khong ve co phieu: R:R, va cat lo cach diem vao bao
nhieu. Hai so do ra tu cam nang — ma cam nang cung can hang, de nhan vao so co. Trong
nhu vong tron va khong phai: CAC MOC khong phu thuoc hang, chi CO VI THE moi phu thuoc.
Nen `cardGrade` chay mot luot KHONG hang de biet cat lo va muc tieu o dau, cham diem cai
do, roi `recalcPlan` chay luot co hang. Ca hai luot la phep tinh tren bars da nam trong
bo nho.

Va `cardGrade` uu tien cat lo cua CHINH NGUOI DUNG neu ho da go: tieu chi R:R phai mo ta
lenh ho sap dat, khong phai lenh cam nang goi y va ho da bo.

### N. Listener uy quyen chi duoc gan MOT lan

`paintGradePanel` thay the cac nut nay moi lan go phim, nen mot listener gan tren chinh
nut se mat truoc khi nguoi dung voi tay den. Nhung cai HOP thi song qua moi lan tinh lai
— `computePlans` chi ghi lai cac con cua no. Gan lai moi luot se chong listener len nhau,
va cai thu hai se dong ngay bang tieu chi vua mo. Nen co co `root.dataset.tpGradeWired`:
mot listener cho mot hop, suot doi hop do.

### O. Files

- `packages/core/src/planning/tradeGrader.ts` — MOI. Bang kiem, cham diem, nhom.
- `packages/core/src/qm/gradeEvidence.ts` — MOI. Cay cau duy nhat tu `QmScanResult` sang
  `GradeEvidence`. Huong phu thuoc di tu `qm` -> `planning`, KHONG nguoc lai: `tradeGrader`
  nhan mot tui so nguyen thuy va khong import gi tu `qm`, vi no phai cham duoc mot ma
  chung khoan may quet chua bao gio chay, va mot bo cham diem gan chat vao mot dang ket
  qua thi khong test duoc ma khong dung ca cai dang do len.
- `packages/core/src/planning/setupPlaybook.ts` — `'rating'` ra khoi `RiskCut`; hang ra
  khoi `riskBudget`; `SizeInput.rating`; `SizeSuggestion` them `fullShares`,
  `fullPositionValue`, `gradeScale`; phep nhan o cuoi `suggestSize`.
- `packages/core/src/planning/index.ts`, `src/qm/index.ts` — xuat ra.
- `apps/desktop/src/portfolio/gradeWords.ts` — MOI. Nhan hai ngon ngu cho tung tieu chi,
  co so nguong IN TRONG nhan ("Suc manh tuong doi tu 80 tro len"), vi khong co so thi mot
  dau cheo do la mot ket luan nguoi dung khong kiem tra duoc.
- `apps/desktop/src/portfolio/playbook.ts` — `riskBudget` khong nhan `rating`; `rating`
  chuyen xuong `suggestSize`; `pinnedBudget` gon lai.
- `apps/desktop/src/portfolio/planWords.ts` — bo `rating` khoi `CUT_SHORT`; dong so co in
  rui ro THAT; dong hang in phep tru.
- `apps/desktop/src/ui/charts.ts` — `setOverlay` tren `CandleChart`; `applyOverlay` giu
  `IPriceLine[]`.
- `apps/desktop/src/tabs/miscTabs.ts` — `planScans` giu lai cac ket qua quet; `PlanEdit`
  doi `rating` thanh `answers` + `gradeOverride` + `criteriaOpen`; `cardPlan(sym, rating)`;
  `cardGrade`; `paintGradePanel` + `criteriaRowsHtml`; `paintPlanChart` dung
  `drawCandles`; `wireGradeDelegates`.
- `apps/desktop/src/ui/playbookSettings.ts` — bo dong chu cho cat `rating`; viet lai chu
  thich "Co theo xep hang" vi hai cau cu da thanh SAI: no noi san rui ro con do duoc phan
  cat nay.
- `apps/desktop/src/ui/i18n.ts` — xoa `wl.plan.grade` / `wl.plan.nograde` (dong chu cu
  "— none (full size)" gio sai han: de trong o ghi de khong phai chua xep hang, ma la
  "dung diem tu tinh"); them 11 khoa moi.
- `apps/desktop/src/styles.css` — `.tp-grade*`, `.tp-gbar*`, `.tp-crit*`, `.tp-grade-math`.

### P. Kiem

- `tests/planning/tradeGrader.test.ts` MOI 31/31. `tests/qm/gradeEvidence.test.ts` MOI
  14/14. `tests/gradeWords.test.ts` MOI 7/7.
- `tests/planning/setupPlaybook.test.ts` 70/70. `tests/buyPlan.test.ts` 24/24.
  `tests/planWords.test.ts` 7/7.
- Core 751/751. App 69/69. `tsc --noEmit` sach ca hai goi, `npm run build` sach ca hai.
  `package-lock.json` khong doi.
- Bon test SAI ky vong cua chinh minh, ghi lai vi ba trong so do day lai code:
  1. `positionValue / fullPositionValue` ra 0.7419 chu khong phai 0.75, vi `floor(62 x
     0.75) = 46`. Noi long ky vong, khong sua code — lam tron xuong la co y.
  2. `riskPctOfEquity` cua hang D ra 0.24 chu khong 0.25 (15 x 8 EUR tren 50k). Dung
     `toBeCloseTo`.
  3. Fixture "khong bao gio ve 0 co" dung tai khoan 900 EUR, ma CO DAY DU cua no da la 0
     roi (0.25% = 2.25 EUR < 8 EUR/co). Doi sang 8.000 EUR.
  4. Test `gradeByGroup` khang dinh nhom khong do duoc bi AN di. Sai: nhom do van phai
     hien, mang trong so cua no trong `unknown` — an di se am tham lam bang kiem nho lai
     tren man hinh, nguoi dung thay tam dong gon gang va khong biet la cac cau ve co ban
     chua tung duoc hoi. Sua test, khong sua code.
- Mot loi THAT do test thu tu tim ra: `atrContractionPct` va `volumeContractionPct` chua
  co cho chan so 0, trong khi `baseDepthPct` thi co. Da gop ca bon vao `nonZero` — xem
  muc J.
- Van khong co test cho phan DOM: `environment: 'node'` co y (CHAT-21 muc K). Nen phan
  cham diem nam tron trong core, va phan chu nam trong `gradeWords.ts` — ca hai test duoc.
  `paintGradePanel` thi phai xem bang mat.

## CHAT-24 — Mua phai di qua ke hoach: mot ke hoach cho mot ma, tu cham diem, xac nhan da xem, va in ra duoc

Nam viec nguoi dung yeu cau: (1) Trade Planner cung phai co nut mo Cam nang; (2) chon tai
khoan thi o Account Equity phai xam di; (3) co nut in ke hoach ra HTML/PDF nhu phan Case
Study; (4) moi tieu chi phai duoc giai thich trong Cam nang theo tung thiet lap, de con hoc
va con sua khi kinh nghiem len; (5) "khi chon mot co phieu de buy thi no phai thong qua
trade plan de danh gia truoc roi xac nhan xem co ok khong thi moi buy", ke hoach cua ma do
phai TU DONG duoc cham diem khi nhap ma vao form Buy, va "Note cua buy form chinh la cai
note cua trade plan luon".

(1) (2) (4) da xong o lan truoc (75202da). Lan nay lam (3) va (5).

### A. Hai quyet dinh do nguoi dung chon, va tai sao

Hoi truoc khi lam, vi ca hai deu khong the sua lai ma khong lam mat du lieu cua nguoi dung:

- CHAN MEM, phai xac nhan. Nut Buy tat cho den khi ke hoach da duoc danh gia VA nguoi dung
  tich "Toi da xem ke hoach nay". Tich do TU RESET khi ma, gia hoac thiet lap doi. Mot hang
  D co chu y thi VAN mua duoc — cai chan nay xac nhan la DA DOC, khong phai la DA TOT.
- LUU KE HOACH THEO TUNG MA. Khoa plan:NVDA giu setup, answers, gradeOverride, note,
  reviewedAt. Trade Planner va form Buy doc va ghi CUNG mot ke hoach do, va no song qua lan
  tai trang.

### B. Mot bang tieu chi, hai man hinh — va tai sao khong copy

paintGradePanel + criteriaRowsHtml khoang 90 dong, han chet vao planEdits va data-tp-*.
Copy sang form Buy thi se dung lai dung cai van de ma muc 11 vua xoa di: HAI bang kiem co
the noi khac nhau ve cung mot lenh. Va lan nay hai ban do se la ban nguoi dung DOC va ban
QUYET DINH CO VI THE cua ho.

Nen tach ra gradeView.ts thanh ham thuan: gradePanelHtml(grade, opts). Ten class van la
tp- (doc la "trade plan", khong phai "trade planner tab") nen styles.css khong phai doi.

ns trong opts la thu GANH NANG, khong phai cho dep: hai bang co the cung nam tren trang mot
luc — bang cua Planner con song khi nguoi dung cuon xuong form Buy. Neu dung chung mot tien
to data-*, trinh xu ly click uy quyen cua Planner se tra loi tieu chi tren bang cua form
Buy, tuc la ghi cai tich cua nguoi dung vao mot ke hoach ma ho KHONG dang nhin, va khong co
gi nem loi.

### C. Tai sao mot khoa moi ma, khong phai mot cuc

plan:NVDA chu khong phai mot ban ghi plans. Ke hoach duoc ghi mot cai mot luc, tu hai man
hinh, va cho luu nay DONG BO. Mot cuc thi moi lan go mot chu vao ghi chu la mot lan
doc-sua-ghi toan bo cac ke hoach khac, va hai may sua hai ma khac nhau se ghi de len nhau
toan bo. Mot khoa moi ma lam cho dung do hep nhat co the: chi mat ke hoach khi sua CHINH ke
hoach do o hai noi.

Gia cua lua chon: khong co "liet ke moi ke hoach" gia re, nen co plan_symbols. Do la mot
GOI Y, khong phai mot ban ghi: listPlans bo cac ma ma ke hoach da bien mat, vi hien mot ke
hoach trong duoi mot cai ten con nho la noi rang cong viec cua nguoi dung van con.

### D. O tich la mot KET QUA VE, khong phai mot trang thai

reviewCurrent(plan, setup, levels, grade) duoc tinh lai o MOI lan ve lai panel. O tich
khong co trang thai rieng — no la anh cua ham do. Nen mot diem vao bi doi, mot thiet lap bi
doi, hay mot tieu chi vua duoc tra loi se lam no bo tich, ma KHONG can mot trinh nghe su
kien nao phai nho lam viec do. Va no dung ca sau khi tai lai trang.

Neu lam bang cach "cho nao sua thi cho do xoa reviewedAt" thi se co ba cho phai nho, va cho
thu tu la cho quen.

### E. Diem vao so co sai so, cat lo va muc tieu so chinh xac

Sai so la max(0.01, |entry| x 0.005) — "mot xu hoac 0.5%, cai nao lon hon". Ly do co ca hai
nua: sai so tinh theo phan tram thi vo nghia tren mot co phieu 2 do, con sai so tinh theo xu
thi vo nghia tren mot co phieu 900 do. O gia duoc DIEN LAI tu gia dong cua moi lan ve lai,
nen neu so sanh chinh xac thi cai chan nay KHONG THE mo duoc trong gio giao dich.

Cat lo va muc tieu thi so sanh chinh xac, vi hai so do chi doi khi co cai gi quyet dinh cho
no doi. Mot cat lo bi noi rong ra la mot khoan lo lon hon khoan da duoc xac nhan.

### F. Hang cung la mot phan cua cai da duoc xac nhan

So sanh thiet lap va ba muc gia bat duoc mot diem vao bi go lai, nhung KHONG bat duoc duong
con lai: tra loi them mot tieu chi. Tich "toi da xem ke hoach nay — hang B" roi bo tich hai
tieu chi thi o van con tich ben canh mot hang D, va D la MOT PHAN TU co vi the — hau qua don
le lon nhat trong app.

Nen SymbolPlan.reviewedGrade luu chu cai dang tren man hinh luc tich. Luu CHU CAI chu khong
luu cac cau tra loi, vi mot phep so sanh the la phu het moi duong dan den mot hang khac:
tieu chi, thiet lap, va o ghi de bang tay.

Mot ke hoach ghi boi ban truoc khong co reviewedGrade thi HET HAN chu khong duoc tin —
huong an toan, gia bang mot lan tich nua.

### G. Chan mem phai VAN mo duoc khi khong co ke hoach

Form Buy la cho lenh duoc GHI LAI — ke ca mot lenh da lam thang truoc, va ke ca mot thiet
lap ma Cam nang khong co dong nao cho no. Mot cai chan khong the mo ma khong co hang se day
nguoi dung den cho thoi khong ghi lenh nua, va luc do so sach moi la cai sai.

Nen buyGate khong he biet den chu cai nao ca, va ackLabel DOI CHU: khi chua co diem thi o
tich doc la "Mua ma khong co ke hoach duoc cham diem" — de mot lan tich khong bao gio khang
dinh la da doc mot ke hoach chua tung ton tai.

### H. Nut chon hang gio la o GHI DE, nen khong con doc duoc luc mua

O nay mac dinh de TRONG (= dung diem tu tinh). Doc no luc bam Buy se ghi MOI lenh da duoc
cham diem thanh lenh chua xep hang — tuc la mat dung cai thong tin vua tinh ra. Nen trinh xu
ly doc dataset.grade cua nut Buy, do paintGate viet ra.

### I. Cam nang chay HAI LAN, va do khong phai vong tron

Hai tieu chi di ra tu Cam nang (R:R, va cat lo cach diem vao bao xa), ma Cam nang thi lai
can hang de nhan co vi the. Khong phai vong tron: CAC MUC GIA khong phu thuoc vao hang, chi
CO VI THE moi phu thuoc. Nen luot mot (chua co hang) tim cat lo va muc tieu, cham diem cai
do, luot hai nhan co. Ca hai luot la phep tinh tren bars da nam trong bo nho.

### J. ChartSubject: mot bieu do, khong phai hai

Tham so thu hai cua caseSvgChart hep lai tu CaseStudy xuong dung cai hinh dang no THAT SU
doc (entry/stop/target/exitPrice/keyDate/exitDate/outcome/catalysts), de ke hoach giao dich
dung lai chinh bo ve do. Mot bieu do, nen ke hoach in ra va bao cao hau ky in ra khong the
noi khac nhau ve cho dat cat lo. CaseStudy van thoa man giao dien do ve mat cau truc.

Thu hep tham so lam lo ra mot loi co san o caseStudiesTab.ts:209: mot phep spread dang nhoi
windowMonths vao doi so, ma bo ve chua bao gio doc truong do — va windowBars ben tren thi da
ap cua so roi.

### K. Ghi chu la MOT ghi chu, va no khong bao gio bi ghi de

showNote chi ghi len o ghi chu khi o do dang TRONG hoac con dung bang noteSeed (dung cai ma
chinh doan code nay vua dat vao). Mot ghi chu viet nua ve lenh dang dat la thu dat nhat tren
form de mat, va no khong co Undo.

Ban ghi chu TU SINH thi co y KHONG luu: ca hai man hinh dung sinh no tu cung mot planLines,
nen luu lai chi de mot ban cu cua no quay ve. Chi ban nguoi dung TU VIET moi duoc luu
(noteEdited).

### L. i18n.ts khong con nem loi ngoai trinh duyet

document.documentElement.lang = resolved chay ngay luc nap module, nen MOI module import t()
deu nam ngoai tam voi cua bo test environment: 'node'. Da bao typeof document !==
'undefined', giong cai bao cho localStorage ngay dong tren. Do la mot vat can THAT cho viec
test, khong phai mot ro ri phai tranh: sua no mo duong cho test node hoa cho moi module dung
t().

### M. planReportHtml tu noi la ham thuan — va no chua phai

Header cua chinh file do viet "pure function of its input so it can be tested without a
DOM", nhung no goi sanitizeNoteHtml, ma ham do can DOMParser THAT. Test do xong 15/16 ngay
dong dau.

Khong sua bang cach tu viet mot bo lam sach bang regex — do se la mot bo lam sach TE HON
that su. Nen safeNote: co DOM thi lam sach, khong co DOM thi ESCAPE toan bo va in ghi chu ra
dang chu thuan. Mat dinh dang, va chi xay ra ngoai trinh duyet (tuc la trong bo test nay),
nhung khong bao gio phat ra mot doan markup chua duoc soi.

Ghi chu da duoc lam sach o MOI duong ghi roi, nen lan nay la lop thu hai — nhung la lop dang
co, vi mot ke hoach co the den tu dong bo, do mot may khac voi ban code cu ghi ra, va thu
dang dung o day la mot TEP nguoi dung se mo truc tiep bang trinh duyet.
