## CHAT-25 — Lich earning khong quet nua (bien nhan song lau hon thu no bao dam), nut Bo chon o form Buy, va ke hoach dong bang theo tung lo mua

Bon viec nguoi dung yeu cau: (1) "Event Calendar, toi khong thay quet earning nua"; (2) form
Buy can mot nut reset de bo chon ma dang chon; (3) trong watchlist thi in ke hoach ra PDF o
dau; (4) "save the trade plan for the transaction we made — mot nut xem day du se hien ke
hoach cua lan mua do hoac cua vi the da dong".

(3) khong phai viec phai lam: nut da co san tu 6afc3e7d. Lam (1), (2), (4).

### A. Muc (1) — khong phai loi giao dien, cung khong phai loi thuong nguon

Da loai tru tung cai mot truoc khi sua gi:

- calendarScan.ts KHONG phai duong nay — do la ban quet ~540 request VCP / mean-reversion /
  attention, khong lien quan earning sap toi.
- adapters/earningsDates.ts cung khong — do la ngay bao cao DA QUA, dung ve dau "E" tren do
  thi.
- Thuong nguon con song. Da goi that ngay 2026-09-28:
  api.nasdaq.com/api/calendar/earnings?date=... tra ve data.rows voi time
  (time-pre-market / time-after-hours), symbol, marketCap, epsForecast, noOfEsts — dung y
  hop dong rowsAt(json, ['data','rows']) cua parseEarnings. Dividends van o
  data.calendar.rows. Vay parseEarnings khong sai.
- Cac bo loc cung khong: kind earnings mac dinh bat, scope 'all', minCap 0, tat ca loc trong
  bo nho. buildWindow chi tra lai dung tham so cua no, khong co tran so su kien.

Con lai mot thu, va no la mot mau bat can doi do 3385e230 tao ra.

### B. Nguyen nhan that — mot bien nhan song lau hon thu no bao dam

calendar_sweep_log nho, nen no duoc luu duoi khoa RIENG de con song sot khi ghi snapshot
that bai. Va no DUOC DONG BO. Nhung cai no bao dam — snapshot calendar:<ngay> — lai nam
trong EXPENDABLE_PREFIXES, tuc la thu dau tien bi bo khi may khong con cho. Chinh bai test
co san "gives up day-stamped cache before it gives up syncing" chung minh dieu do.

Hau qua: decideSweep thay log.lastSweepDay >= today tren mot may CHUA BAO GIO quet (hoac da
mat snapshot), va tra ve 'swept-but-no-snapshot'. Khong co earning nao tren man hinh, khong
quet lai, ca ngay, ngay nao cung the.

Mot bien nhan khong duoc phep song lau hon thu ma no bao dam.

### C. Cach sua muc (1)

calendar_sweep_log vao LOCAL_ONLY_PREFIXES. Bien nhan tro thanh tung may mot — "may NAY da
dung ~60 request cua no hom nay" — dung nghia goc cua no, giong pf_autoupdate. Phan tiet
kiem xuyen may van con nguyen: nhanh 'use-snapshot' van chay o bat cu may nao snapshot that
su den duoc.

Khong can migration. Duong tai ve loc syncable() theo thoi diem doc, nen bien nhan cu tu
dong bi bo; va renderStatus dat mayResweep = stale && sweptDay < today roi tu dung lai —
tren may chua tung quet thi sweptDay === '', nen no tu hoi phuc.

### D. Muc (2) — nut Bo chon dat o dau va tai sao

Nut nam tren dau the Buy, canh nut Cam nang, KHONG canh nut Buy. Mot nut xoa trang moi thu
khong nen nam cach nut dat lenh mot be ngon tay.

resetForm phai nam TRONG wireBuyPlan. Xoa may o tu ben ngoai se de lai plan, grade,
lastBars, noteSeed, filled va token cua ma cu — va o tich xac nhan la mot BAN VE cua
reviewCurrent(plan, ...), khong phai cua may o input. Mot lan dispatch 'change' tren o ma
cho wirePriceHint tu xoa dong "gia dong cua gan nhat" cua no, khong phai voi tay vao closure
cua no.

Ngay thi CO Y giu lai: nhap nhieu giao dich cho cung mot ngay qua khu la viec nguoi dung lam
nhieu nhat, go lai ngay moi lan la cai gia.

Khong mat gi: ke hoach nam duoi plan:<MA>, go lai ma la bang tieu chi, hang va ghi chu tro
lai. Tooltip noi dung dieu do, vi chinh no lam nut nay an toan de bam.

### E. Muc (4) — tai sao la SNAPSHOT chu khong phai tra cuu

plan:NVDA la MOT ke hoach cho mot ma, va co the sua — dung thiet ke: no la ke hoach cho lan
mua NVDA TIEP THEO. Mua thang Mot, sua bang tieu chi thang Ba, thi lo thang Mot se hien ly
le cua thang Ba. Day la cho duy nhat trong app ma sai thi VO HINH, vi ca hai phia van trong
nhu nhau. Mot ban mo xe khong con gia tri gi neu ke hoach dem ra so sanh co the sua sau khi
da biet ket qua.

Nen luc mua thi SAO LAI: ke hoach, bang diem, hang dang co hieu luc, cac muc gia va so co
nhu da nhap. Khong bao gio ghi lai nua.

### F. Tai sao la plansnap:<lotId> chu khong phai lot.plan

Moi lo nam trong MOT hang accounts duy nhat, doc-sua-ghi lai moi lan giao dich, moi lan doi
cat lo, moi lan lam moi gia — va dong bo NGUYEN hang, da tung 912 KB. Mot bang 26 tieu chi
cong mot ghi chu cho tung lo se di thang vao do.

Ngoai nay thi snapshot ghi mot lan, doc khi nguoi dung hoi, va khong lam hang accounts to
len chut nao. Id cua lo CHINH LA khoa, nen khong phai them gi vao BuyLot va khong co chi muc
nao co the troi: lotIdsWithPlan liet ke tien to.

### G. Cai CO Y khong luu

Bars. Do thi ve lai tu lich su gia luc XEM, khong ton cho nao va lai la buc tranh tot hon —
luc do no cho thay co phieu da di the nao SAU khi ke hoach duoc viet, ngay ben canh muc cat
lo da du tinh cho no.

Nguoc lai, pctOfFull thi CO luu: nguoi dung sua duoc bang thang, tinh lai se lam mot giao
dich cu bi phat bieu lai theo luat cua hom nay.

### H. Tai sao nguoi xem la mot iframe srcdoc chu khong phai mot bo cuc thu hai

Ve lai ke hoach bang style cua app se la ban thu hai cua cung mot tai lieu, va hai ban se
troi: them mot tieu chi ben nay, doi ten mot muc ben kia, roi ke hoach nguoi dung doc tren
man hinh khong con la ke hoach ho in ra. srcdoc dat DUNG cai tep do truoc mat ho, nen chi co
mot bo cuc va xem cung la in.

sandbox khong co allow-scripts — nen nut in cua chinh tai lieu tat, va hop thoai tu dua nut
in cua no. Mot snapshot da luu la du lieu cu nhat trong app va co the do mot ban code khong
con ton tai viet ra; no duoc ve nhu mot tai lieu, nen khong cho no chay gi ca.

srcdoc gan SAU khi da chen vao DOM: gan luc iframe con roi se nap tai lieu hai lan tren
WebKit, ma tai lieu nay mang mot SVG trong.

### I. Nut tren hang giao dich: an truoc, hien sau

transactionHistoryHtml chay DONG BO tu accounts, khong the await mot lan doc storage cho
tung hang. Nen nut ve ra hidden, roi wire() mo bang mot lan lotIdsWithPlan(ctx). Mot nut mo
ra hop thoai trong tren moi giao dich cu se day nguoi dung den cho thoi khong bam nua.

Ca hang CLOSED va hang OPEN deu mang lotId, nen ca hai deu moi duoc.

Xoa lo thi xoa luon snapshot (void deletePlanSnapshot): id cua lo la duong vao duy nhat, nen
de lai chi la cho ma dong bo mang theo mai cho mot giao dich khong con ton tai.

### J. Doc lai snapshot: chuan hoa chu khong ep kieu

Blob nay dong bo va theo dinh nghia la du lieu cu nhat trong app.

- normalizePlan tach ra khoi loadPlan va export, de ke hoach dong bang di qua DUNG cai chan
  cua ke hoach song. Quan trong nhat la chan boolean tren answers: mot "false" dang chuoi la
  truthy, se cong diem cho mot tieu chi nguoi dung chua bao gio tich — o ben trong mot ban
  ghi cua quyet dinh da xong, cho khong ai kiem lai.
- validGrade bo CA CUC grade khi thieu outcomes hoac score khong phai so huu han. Mot
  GradeResult khong co outcomes khong phai mot hang bi thieu, do la mot chu cai khong co gi
  dang sau; in no duoi chu "Bang tieu chi" la tai lieu tu khang dinh dieu no khong trung ra
  duoc. Ma bao cao lai di qua grade.outcomes, nen tren man hinh no thanh mot nut bam khong
  lam gi.
- pctOfFull khong doc duoc thi ve 100, KHONG phai 0: 0% se in giao dich do nhu la khong duoc
  cap co nao ca, doc ra thanh mot loi tu choi ma app chua bao gio dua ra.

### K. Ghi snapshot o dau trong luong mua

Trong handler #b-go, NGAY sau buy() va sau khi chuan hoa gia, TRUOC save(ctx) va draw(ctx) —
vi draw() dung lai closure dang giu plan, grade va bars.

takeBuyPlanSnapshot la mot bien o cap module do wireBuyPlan gan vao, vi handler Buy nam
ngoai closure do va khong thay duoc ke hoach. Cung ly do voi viec chu cai hang di tren
dataset cua #b-go — chi khac la ca mot bang tieu chi thi khong nhet duoc vao mot attribute.

Ke hoach duoc sao SAU: { ...plan, answers: { ...plan.answers }, levels: {...} }. Dung chung
tham chieu se cho phep phim tiep theo ghi lai mot ban ghi cua qua khu.

That bai khi ghi thi BO QUA: mot cho luu day khong duoc lam nguoi dung mat giao dich ho dang
ghi. Lo da nam trong state roi, thieu snapshot chi la hang do khong co nut ke hoach.

### L. Tep da sua

## CHAT-26 — Ke hoach cho mot ma (o watchlist va o trang co phieu), chon ngay giao dich, va mua thang tu ke hoach

Yeu cau cua nguoi dung, bon phan: (1) moi dong co phieu trong watchlist co them mot nut trade
plan rieng — nut cu cho ca danh sach van giu; (2) mot nut trade plan tuong tu tren trang
individual stock, va ke hoach hien LUON tren trang do; (3) trong ke hoach co dropdown chon
account va mot nut Buy, giao dich duoc ghi vao account do; (4) them o chon ngay — khong chon
thi lay ngay hom nay, chon thi lay ngay da chon.

### A. Vi sao phai tach mot tep moi truoc khi lam gi

Ke hoach giao dich truoc day nam trong tabs/miscTabs.ts (dong 296-1274). Muc (2) doi no phai
mount duoc ben trong ui/stockModal.ts. Nhung miscTabs.ts DA import openStock tu stockModal.ts,
nen de stockModal.ts import lai miscTabs.ts la tao vong import — ESM se giao mot trong hai ben
mot binding chua khoi tao, va loi se hien ra o mot cho khong lien quan.

Nen toan bo panel chuyen sang portfolio/tradePlanner.ts (1412 dong), va no KHONG import
stockModal: cho nao can mo trang co phieu thi nhan ham onOpenSymbol tu ben goi.

Truoc khi chuyen, da grep tung import rieng cua panel (buildTradePlan, explainPlan, computeCash,
computeEquity, isSetupKey, isRating, SETUP_KEYS, RATING_KEYS, gradeTrade, qmGradeEvidence, num,
playbook, planWords, gradeView, planStore, planReport, prices, charts, playbookSettings,
richNote) de chac moi cai chi xuat hien dung mot lan ngoai khoi panel — tuc la chi o chinh dong
import cua no. Neu khong grep thi se de lai import chet trong miscTabs.ts, hoac te hon, chuyen
thieu mot ham.

### B. Bat bien: chi MOT panel duoc mount cung luc

Trang thai cua panel la bien o pham vi module, danh chi muc theo ma, va cach no tim DOM la tim
toan cuc: document.getElementById('tp-acct'), [data-tp-derived="NVDA"]. Hai panel mount cung luc
se tranh nhau cung mot id, va neu hai ben co chung mot ma thi ben nay se ve len the cua ben kia.

Nen openTradePlanner pha panel dang mo o host khac TRUOC khi mount:

  export async function openTradePlanner(ctx, mount) {
    if (mounted && mounted.host !== mount.host) closeTradePlanner();
    mounted = mount;
    await renderPlannerPanel(ctx);
  }

closeTradePlanner(onlyInside?) co tham so tuy chon vi mot so ben chi muon pha panel NAM TRONG
cho minh sap don: neu panel dang o noi khac thi de nguyen.

tradePlannerIsIn(host) de mot nut biet minh dang bat hay tat, tuc la bam lan hai thi dong chu
khong mount lai.

Giao dien mount:

  interface PlannerMount {
    host: HTMLElement;
    symbols: () => string[] | Promise<string[]>;   // doc lai moi lan tinh
    title: string;                                  // ten danh sach, hoac chinh ma
    onOpenSymbol?: (symbol: string) => void;        // bo trong khi khong co cho nao de di
    onClose?: () => void;                           // de host nha nut cua no ra
  }

symbols la HAM chu khong phai mang: mot ma bi xoa khoi watchlist khi panel dang mo thi nut
"Lap ke hoach" khong duoc tinh lai ma do nua.

onOpenSymbol bo trong khi panel nam ngay tren trang cua chinh ma do — mot link mo lai trang
dang xem thi doc ra nhu link chet. Khi khong co, ticker la <strong> chu khong phai <a>.

### C. Vong doi do thi: moi duong don host phai di qua closeTradePlanner

drawCandles tao mot canvas, mot ResizeObserver va toi chin chuoi du lieu. Tha node di ma khong
goi destroy() thi ResizeObserver con song, quan sat node khong ai thay duoc nua. Panel gio mount
o bon cho, nen da noi lai TAT CA duong lam trong host:

- renderWatchlist: closeTradePlanner(root) truoc root.innerHTML = ...
- doi tab watchlist: closeTradePlanner($('#wl-plan-panel'))
- openStock: closeTradePlanner(body) truoc body.innerHTML = ...
- closeModal: closeTradePlanner($('#modal-body')) ngay sau khi an modal
- computePlans: destroyPlanCharts() truoc khi ve lai cac the
- nut x cua chinh panel

### D. Muc (1) — nut 📋 tren tung hang watchlist

qmTable chi nhan MOT cot action, nen cot xoa cu thanh hai nut trong cung mot <span
data-row-action>:

  <button class="icon-btn" data-plan="...">📋</button>
  <button class="icon-btn" data-del="...">✕</button>

qmTable bo qua cu bam bat nguon tu trong [data-row-action], nen bam 📋 khong mo trang co phieu.
Handler van goi e.stopPropagation() vi day la hai lop bao ve khac nhau, khong phai mot.

planOneSymbol dung CUNG host #wl-plan-panel voi panel ca danh sach, chu khong mo rong trong long
hang bang. The cua panel rat cao (mot do thi, 26 dong tieu chi, phan tinh co vi the); mo trong
hang bang se day toan bo phan con lai cua danh sach ra khoi man hinh. Nen panel hien o cho quen
thuoc phia tren bang, roi host.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'start'}).

### E. Muc (2) — nut 📋 tren trang co phieu rieng

Trong renderDetail: nut #sm-plan o hang dau, va host rong <div id="sm-plan-panel"></div> ngay
duoi #wl-picker — tuc la sat ngay duoi nut, khong can scroll.

wirePlanButton bat/tat bang tradePlannerIsIn(host). Khi mo thi bam class active vao nut, va
truyen onClose de nut tu nha ra khi nguoi dung dong panel bang x.

Da phai them CSS button.btn-outline.active — truoc do khong co luat nao cho trang thai nay, nen
nut se bam xuong ma khong he thay doi gi.

### F. Muc (4) — o chon ngay giao dich

let planDate = today(); o pham vi panel, KHONG phai tung the: nguoi dung lap ke hoach cho mot
buoi, chu khong cho mot ma; bat ho dat lai ngay tren tam the la tam co hoi de quen mot the.

O trong hang dieu khien, canh dropdown tai khoan. Doi ngay thi computePlans (tinh lai) chu
khong chi ve lai, boi vi ngay khong phai thu trang tri:

- no la ngay lay ti gia EUR/USD, nen tren tai khoan EUR no doi gia von, doi von de tinh phan
  tram, va do do doi ca so co phieu;
- no la ngay lo mua duoc ghi vao;
- no la ngay in tren ke hoach va la tam do thi.

O rong co nghia la "hom nay" chu khong phai "khong co": planDate = dateEl.value || today(), roi
viet lai vao o. Mot ke hoach khong co ngay thi khong tinh duoc co vi the.

### G. Muc (3) — nut Buy, va nhung gi no tu choi

buyBlocker(symbol) tra ve mot khoa i18n hoac null. Chan khi: chua tinh ke hoach, chua chon tai
khoan, chua co gia vao, so co phieu bang 0, va cat lo >= gia vao.

Cat lo bang hoac cao hon gia vao khong phai loi nho: do la mot lenh da bi cat lo ngay tu dau, va
moi con so rui ro suy ra tu no se am hoac vo nghia.

CO Y KHONG chan khi thieu tien mat. core buy() chi kiem shares > 0 va buyPrice >= 0 — no khong
kiem tien mat. Va nguoi dung co the dang ghi lai mot lenh ho DA dat that (co the sau mot lan nap
tien ma app chua biet); khoa ho ra khoi so sach cua chinh ho la sai. Nen thieu tien la mot canh
bao thay duoc (tp-cash-warn, va dong "tien mat con lai" mau do trong hop thoai xac nhan), khong
phai mot ro chan.

Cung CO Y khong dat o tich "toi da xem ke hoach nay" o day. O tich do co ly o form Buy, vi o do
ke hoach la mot thu o cho khac. O day ke hoach CHINH LA man hinh dang xem, va hop thoai xac nhan
la lan nhin cuoi.

### H. Mot duong mua duy nhat: applyWrite, va lotId duoc dan ra

Nut Buy khong tu goi core. No dung applyWrite(ctx, {kind:'record_buy', ...}) — cung duong ma tro
ly trong chat dung — vi duong do da lam du: buy(), priceCurrency, fxRateAtBuy, seedPrice,
snapshotNow, appendAudit, va announce() (portfolioTab nghe qua onAgentWrite roi ve lai). Mot lo
do panel nay mua phai khong the phan biet duoc voi mot lo danh tay.

applyWrite gio tra ve WriteResult { lotId?: string }. Can cai nay vi ke hoach dong bang duoc
luu theo khoa plansnap:<lotId>, va id cua lo la thu duy nhat nam duoc no: hai lan mua cung mot
ma, cung ngay, cung gia la HAI lo hop le.

### I. Mot phep doi tien duy nhat: plannedPrice

Phep tinh doi gia USD sang tien tai khoan da duoc nhac tu toolExec.planPrice ra thanh
plannedPrice(accountCurrency, given, ccy, date) trong portfolio/writes.ts, va planPrice gio chi
con boc chu cho mo hinh doc.

Ly do nhac ra: neu panel tu viet phep tinh nay thi hai ben se lech nhau, va cai lech do khong
hien ra o dau ca.

plannedPrice TU CHOI chu khong doi o ti gia 1. eurUsdForDate buoc phai tra ve mot so cho moi
duong hien thi, nen khi khong biet gi no tra 1 — va "1.00" bien mot cai fill $232.50 thanh gia
von EUR 232.50, sai mot phan muoi vi the, sai vinh vien, khong man hinh nao phia sau phat hien
duoc. Tra ve ma loi ('no-rate' | 'not-eur-usd') thi con cuu duoc: bam Cap nhat, hoac nhap gia
bang tien cua tai khoan.

Ma loi chu khong phai cau chu, vi hai ben phai noi khac nhau: tro ly can van xuoi cho mo hinh
doc, panel can mot dong da dich duoi nut Buy.

Luat tien te trong panel khong doi: o Entry/Stop/Target giu gia dong cua USD tho (cat lo la con
so minh dua cho broker), con tien ra tu buildBuyPlan thi DA la tien cua tai khoan — nen planConv
doi tu tien tai khoan, khong phai tu USD.

### J. Mua CHINH LA xac nhan da xem

planReport.ts dong 180 in "NOT acknowledged" khi thieu plan.reviewedAt hoac plan.levels. Neu
buyFromPlan khong ghi hai thu do thi moi lo mua tu panel nay se co mot bao cao dong bang in cau
canh bao SAI.

Nen truoc khi cat snapshot, buyFromPlan dat reviewedAt = bay gio, levels = {entry, stop, target}
dang dung, va reviewedGrade = hang co hieu luc, roi savePlan.

### K. Tai khoan nao — hai mang, hai muc dich

Dropdown doc thang blob 'accounts' tu storage (planAccounts), vi loadAccounts co the dung ra mot
tai khoan khoi dau chi de hien thi, va tai khoan do khong bao gio duoc phep dem ra mua.

Duong GHI thi khac: goi ensureAccountsLoaded(ctx), roi tim lai id da chon trong mang accounts
song. Khong thay thi tu choi voi wl.plan.buyno.gone.

withAccounts tu choi truoc khi hydrate xong ("portfolio is still syncing — not safe to write
yet"). Nut Buy bat loi do va hien nguyen van len duoi nut.

### L. Sau khi mua thi VE LAI, khong render lai

renderPlannerPanel se fetch lai bars cua moi ma. Nen sau mot lenh mua chi lam dung nhung gi doi:
copy accounts song sang planAccounts, dung acctOptionsHtml() ve lai rieng cac <option> cua
#tp-acct (tien mat trong nhan la dung cai vua doi), paintPlanStatus(), roi recalcPlan cho tung
the (vi von doi thi co vi the doi theo).

Mot cho de sai: handler cua nut da tung viet .finally(() => paintBuyButton(sym)), va nhu the se
XOA mat dong ket qua ("Da ghi vao X" hoac cau bao loi) ngay khi no vua hien. Sua thanh
.finally(() => { b.disabled = buyBlocker(sym) !== null; }) — chi nut tro lai, dong chu thi khong.

  dung chuan cua form Buy, va khong tra lotId cho lenh khong tao lo nao.
- Hai loi bien dich, cung mot nguyen nhan: closeTradePlanner($('#modal-body') ?? undefined) va
  ban o miscTabs. $ co dang <T extends Element = HTMLElement>(...): T | null; khi co kieu ngu
  canh la HTMLElement | undefined thi TS suy T tu ngu canh, doi T | null khong khop duoc, nen no
  roi ve rang buoc Element. Sua bang cach ghi ro $<HTMLElement>(...).

## CHAT-27 — Chan cat lo toi da (EMA21 / ATR) cho moi thiet lap, gia nhap sua duoc bang EUR, va nut xem ke hoach

Yeu cau cua nguoi dung, bon phan: (1) giai thich hien tai entry, stop, target duoc tinh nhu the
nao; (2) hien VA SUA duoc gia bang EUR, vi "minh se mua bang gia EUR thuong xuyen nua, thinh
thoang mua ben USD nhung se it hon"; (3) "Stop should be maximun is EMA21 hoac la ATR dua vao
cai nao thap hon", va "Luat chan do cho tat ca cac setup di", kem cau hoi "lieu Stop, Target co
cho vao configurable dduoc khong nhi"; (4) "Ben Buy Form, lieu la minh co nut view trade plan
khong, doi khi minh chi muon xem thoi chu khong muon print. cu de ca 2 options la tot nhat".
Phan lam dep nut Reset tren mobile lam truoc do, ghi o muc G.

### A. Tra loi: entry, stop, target hien dang tinh o dau

Ba chang, khong phai mot:

1. Hat giong: computePlans goi buildTradePlan (core) cho tung ma. Ham nay cham diem thiet lap va
   dua ra entry/stop/target dau tien, chi de do vao ba o input lan dau.
2. Moi khi go vao o Entry hoac doi thiet lap: recalcPlan roi cardPlan roi buildBuyPlan (app) roi
   suggestLevels (core). suggestLevels moi la noi quyet dinh:
   - Cat lo neo theo CAU TRUC, theo tung thiet lap: signalBarLow (day nen tin hieu) hoac
     lowestLowN (day thap nhat N phien), roi noi them padPct phan tram.
   - Chi khi khong co day nao DUOI gia vao moi lui ve entry tru atrMult nhan ATR(14), va day
     canh bao fellBackToAtr.
   - Muc tieu: rMultiple (entry cong R nhan firstTargetR), measuredMove (chieu cao mau hinh),
     hoac ema (chot ngay tai EMA20, dung cho Mean Reversion).
   - So co: suggestSize, theo thang rui ro cong trang thai thi truong cong hang A/B/C.
3. Neu nguoi dung tu go cat lo hoac muc tieu thi ownStop/ownTarget bat len va so do khong bi ghi
   de nua, cho den khi xoa trang o do.

Diem quan trong: TRUOC chat nay stop KHONG co tran nao ca. Cau truc xa hon moc ATR chi sinh ra
mot canh bao stopWiderThanAtr roi giu nguyen cat lo va giam so co. Dung cai lo hong do la cai
yeu cau (3) lap vao.

### B. Tran cat lo toi da — packages/core/src/planning/setupPlaybook.ts

Them hai truong vao SetupRule, co trong ca 7 dong mac dinh:

- maxStopEma: number | null, mac dinh 21. De trong nghia la chi chan bang ATR.
- maxStopRef: 'deeper' | 'shallower' | 'off', mac dinh 'deeper'.

Cach tinh, dat ngay truoc cho kiem tra stop >= entry:

- Hai moc ung vien: EMA(maxStopEma) tai nen cuoi, va entry tru atrMult nhan ATR(14). Chi lay moc
  nao DUOI gia vao: mot EMA nam tren gia vao khong the la cat lo, va o che do 'shallower' no se
  sinh ra cat lo NAM TREN gia vao.
- maxStopPrice la min cua hai moc khi 'deeper', max khi 'shallower', null khi 'off' hoac khong do
  duoc moc nao.
- Chi keo LEN: stop bang max cua stop va maxStopPrice. Tran gioi han KHOANG CACH, nen khong bao
  gio duoc day mot cat lo dang gan xuong cho bang tran — lam the la tu tay sinh them rui ro.
- Khi tran an, day canh bao stopCappedByMax. LevelSuggestion tra them maxStopPrice va
  maxStopEmaValue de man hinh noi duoc "cat lo cua ban la 4.10, sau nhat duoc phep la 3.80".
- stopWiderThanAtr tinh SAU khi chan, vi canh bao phai noi ve cai cat lo nguoi dung dang nhan. O
  che do 'deeper' hai canh bao co the cung bat: EMA sau hon moc ATR thi tran an ma cat lo van
  rong hon moc ATR, va ca hai deu dung.

Vi sao min chu khong phai max: "thap hon" doc theo nghia GIA thap hon thi ra min, tuc moc sau
hon, tuc tran rong rai va it khi an. Nguoi dung KHONG tra loi cau hoi min hay max (xem muc J),
nen chon min va de 'shallower' trong dropdown cho ho bam mot cai la doi. Neu chon max thi mot
breakout co gia vao nam ngay tren EMA21 se bi chan thanh cat lo chung 2 phan tram, va cai bong
nen ke tiep quet mat.

Vi sao con giu 'off': doc comment dau tep goi "cat lo dat theo cau truc, khong theo so tien" la
luat khong duoc lam mem. Tran nay khong pha luat do — no chan bang hai thuoc do CUA THI TRUONG
(mot EMA va ATR), khong phai bang so tien — nhung no CO doi cat lo, nen doc comment da duoc viet
lai, them mot muc noi ro su khac nhau; va 'off' giu lai duoc hanh vi cu theo tung thiet lap.

Test: them 4 test trong packages/core/tests/planning/setupPlaybook.test.ts. Test cu "WARNS about
a structure stop wider than the ATR guide" doi thanh dung maxStopRef 'off' — no van la bang
chung cho luat cu, chi la gio luat cu nam sau mot lua chon chu khong bi xoa.

### C. Stop / Target configurable — da co san tu truoc, va da mo rong

Tra loi cau hoi cua nguoi dung: DA CO SAN. Trang Cam nang (apps/desktop/src/ui/
playbookSettings.ts) cho sua theo tung thiet lap: neo cat lo, N phien, dem phan tram, so lan
ATR, kieu muc tieu, R dau, EMA chot, EMA keo, han so phien. Chat nay them hai cot: "EMA chan"
(maxStopEma) va "Lay moc" (maxStopRef, ba lua chon). Luu y ky thuat: dung NULLABLE_FIELDS thay
cho danh sach viet thang trong ham Save, va maxStopRef phai nam cung nhom voi anchor/targetKind
vi no la chu chu khong phai so. Trang nay chi luu PHAN KHAC mac dinh, nen them cot khong lam
phinh du lieu dong bo.

### D. Gia nhap, cat lo, muc tieu bang EUR — apps/desktop/src/portfolio/tradePlanner.ts

Truoc: ba o gia luon la USD tho, planCcy chi doi cach hien thi TIEN. Gio planCcy doi ca ba o
gia. Doc lai doc comment "WHERE THE CURRENCIES SIT" o dau tep de thay day du.

- planRate() la eurUsdForDate(planDate), tra 0 khi chua co ty gia. Bo han bien planEurUsd (truoc
  lay ty gia MOI NHAT). Day la sua mot loi tiem an: man hinh quy doi theo ty gia hom nay con
  plannedPrice ghi so theo ty gia NGAY GIAO DICH, khong co gi phia sau bao ra su khac nhau do.
  Gio ca hai di qua mot ham.
- Ba ham quy doi co ten, va khong quy doi o dau khac: planConv (tien tai khoan sang tien hien
  thi), levelToUsd va usdToLevel (gia trong o doi voi USD, vi nen la USD tho), levelToAcct (gia
  trong o sang tien tai khoan, dung cho gia tri vi the va so tien rui ro).
- cardPlan truyen entryCurrency: planCcy. buildBuyPlan da ho tro san viec nay tu truoc (toUsd /
  fromUsd theo eurUsdForDate(date)) — Buy Form da lam dung tu lau, chi Trade Planner la cho lech.
- Do thi: overlay phai doi NGUOC ve USD, vi nen la gia tho. Mot duong 198 EUR ve tren do thi 232
  USD khong phai la nhan sai, ma la duong nam ngoai truc.
- rescaleLevels(factor): khi doi tien te hoac doi NGAY, ba o duoc quy doi lai de giu dung MOT
  giao dich (cung mot gia tren cung mot nen), chu khong giu nguyen chu so. Doi ngay thi nhan
  before chia after, nen so EUR doi con duong tren do thi dung yen.
- Khong co ty gia thi nut EUR/USD bi disable (kem title giai thich), va viec tu doi sang EUR khi
  chon tai khoan EUR cung bi chan. Nhan EUR de tren mot so USD dung la cai loi can tranh.
- buyFromPlan: plannedPrice(..., planCcy, planDate) cho ca ba muc. Snapshot va bao cao in ra ghi
  currency: planCcy.
- Nhan ba o gia co them (EUR) hoac (USD). Dong trang thai dung khoa moi wl.plan.levelccy co
  {ccy}, va khi la EUR thi noi luon ty gia dang dung.

### E. Do thi trong bao cao ke hoach — apps/desktop/src/portfolio/planReport.ts

PlanReportInput them fxRate?: number (1 EUR bang N USD tai ngay do). Truyen VAO chu khong doc tu
fx.ts, de planReportHtml van la ham thuan — day la dieu kien de test duoc no trong vitest khong
co DOM. Cac muc gia bang EUR duoc doi ve USD chi de VE do thi; tien trong tai lieu van la EUR.
Neu la EUR ma khong co ty gia thi BO do thi: mot do thi ve sai cho con te hon khong co do thi,
vi do la phan nguoi doc tin ma khong doc so. Loi nay Buy Form cung da mac tu truoc (o gia ben
do cho chon EUR tu lau), nen sua o day la sua cho ca hai duong goi. Them 1 test.

### F. Nut xem ke hoach o Buy Form

Giu ca hai nut, dung nhu "cu de ca 2 options la tot nhat": Xem ke hoach (#b-plan-view) canh In
ke hoach (#b-plan-print). Nut Xem dung lai openPlanReport da co san (dialog cong iframe sandbox
cong mot nut in o trong), tuc dang xem ma doi y muon in thi in ngay duoc. Mot ham reportNow()
dung chung cho ca hai nut, nen khong the co chuyen mot nut in ban cu va mot nut xem ban moi. Ca
hai disable khi o ma con trong. Khoa moi: plan.view, plan.viewtitle, plan.viewttl.

### G. Nut Reset o Buy Form tren mobile

Loi that nam o luat mobile: #tab-portfolio .card .row .btn-outline { flex: 1 1 120px } de cho
cac nut CUA FORM tu dan thanh hang deu, nhung no bat luon hai nut nho o dong tieu de (Reset va
Cam nang): 2 nhan 120px cong khoang cach la khoang 246px, nen cap nut bi day xuong dong. Sua
bang cau truc chu khong nhich pixel: them class .mini-btn (flex 0 0 auto, padding nho, nowrap)
va .card-head-actions cho nhom nut canh section-title, roi them :not(.mini-btn) vao HAI luat
mobile. Truoc day padding la inline style nen mien nhiem luat mobile; chuyen thanh class thi
phai loai tru bang tay — day la cai bay do sap xep do uu tien chon loc (mot id cong ba class
thang moi mot class don).

  tai khoan dung gia goc.

  keo len; vao Cam nang doi "Lay moc" thanh "Khong chan" thi cat lo phai tro lai day cau truc.
- Hai cot moi trong bang luat theo tung thiet lap: sua, Luu, mo lai, va bam ve mac dinh.
- Nut Xem ke hoach o Buy Form: mo ra doc duoc, nut in o trong chay, va no disable khi chua co ma.
- Cap nut Reset / Cam nang tren dien thoai: cung mot dong voi tieu de, khong bi keo rong.

## CHAT-28 — Vi sao tab Scanner bao "stale" va ngay du lieu la 25-09, cong thanh trang thai va thanh muc luc dinh

Nguoi dung hoi ba viec: (1) bao "Status is stale — the push cron has stopped (23h)" co dung
khong, co van de gi khong, va vi sao du lieu la 25-09-2026 khi phien gan nhat la 28-09-2026;
(2) "Phan Jump to, lam cho no noi bat hon khong la khong ai de y"; (3) "phan 1 to check,
refresh, read 0s .... lam cho no dep hon di nhe".

### A. Doc cai canh bao: MOT loi, khong phai hai

Canh bao dung nghia den. `healthNotes` bat khi `Date.now() - pushedAt > PUSH_STALE_SEC`
(600 giay), voi `pushedAt` la dau thoi gian CUA CLOUDFLARE luc VM ghi khoa
`scanner:status` vao D1. 23h nghia la ban chup moi nhat ma Cloudflare dang giu da 23 tieng,
tuc khoang hon 1000 lan cron phut khong de lai gi. Day khong phai mot cu truot nhip.

Ngay 25-09 KHONG phai loi thu hai, no la ket qua cua chinh loi tren, va con dung ca theo
mot ly do thu hai:

- Ban chup dung yen tu 23h truoc, tuc tu trong ngay thu Hai 28-09. Trong ngay thu Hai,
  phien DA DONG gan nhat la thu Sau 25-09. Vay ban chup ghi 25-09 la dung o thoi diem no
  duoc chup.
- `nightly.py` chay 08:00 gio New York, thu Hai den thu Sau (VM-4). No lam viec tren nen DA
  DONG. Nen suot ca ngay thu Hai, `bar` van la 25-09; nen thu Hai 28-09 chi vao so sau khi
  lan chay 08:00 ET thu Ba 29-09 xong. Luc doc trang nay la 08:06 gio Trung Au thu Ba, tuc
  02:06 ET — con truoc 08:00 ET. Vay dau mot he thong hoan toan khoe manh thi luc do cung
  van hien 25-09.

Nen: ngay du lieu khong phai van de. Van de la VM da im 23 tieng.

Suy ra duoc them mot dieu quan trong tu chinh trang nay: PHIA DOC VAN CHAY. Trinh duyet
lay duoc ban chup (cu) tu D1, nen ma truy cap, endpoint `/api/scanner`, D1 deu on. Loi nam
o phia VM ghi len. Va `push.py --status` la cron RIENG, khong lien quan main.py: neu main.py
chet ma cron con song thi van co ban chup moi, chi kem canh bao nhip tim cu. Im hoan toan
nghia la cron khong chay, hoac VM khong chay, hoac lenh ghi bi tu choi — va `push.py` nuot
loi (co ghi trong `functions/api/scanner/[[path]].ts`), nen mot token sai tao ra dung cai
su im lang nay chu khong tao ra thong bao nao.

Thu tu di kiem tra tren VM (chua lam duoc o may nay, khong co SSH):

1. VM con song khong — ssh vao. Oracle free tier co the thu hoi may dang ranh.
2. `crontab -l` duoi user `ubuntu`, KHONG `sudo` — con du hai dong `push.py` va dong
   `CRON_TZ` khong.
3. `cd ~/scanner && source .venv/bin/activate && python push.py --all` — chay tay de loi
   HIEN RA man hinh; chay qua cron thi loi bi nuot.
4. `python nightly.py --status` — dem qua chuoi nightly co chay khong. Day la cau hoi KHAC
   voi chuyen day du lieu, va khong the tra loi bang trang Scanner khi trang dang dong bang.
5. `sudo systemctl status scanner` — main.py con song khong (nhip tim). Nho: cung luc chi
   MOT tien trinh main.py duoc chay.

### B. Noi ro trong app rang moi ngay ben duoi dang dong bang

Them khoa `scan.warn.stale.tail`, noi ngay trong cung dong canh bao: moi thu ben duoi dung
yen o thoi diem do, nen cac ngay doc duoc la cua ban chup cu chu khong phai hom nay. Dung
cai nhap nhang da lam nguoi dung tuong co hai loi — trang van trong hoan toan binh thuong
khi cron chet, chi la moi con so tren no dong bang lai, va mot ngay dong bang doc y nhu mot
dem nightly bi bo.

### C. Thanh trang thai — apps/desktop/src/tabs/scannerTab.ts, ham statusStrip

Truoc: ba thu roi trong mot `.toolbar` — chip `status-chip--muted`, nut Refresh, va chu
"read 0s" mau xam 12px. Loi thuc su la MAU: "1 to check" duoc son cung mot mau xam voi cai
dau thoi gian ben canh, nen dong duy nhat tren trang noi rang co cai gi dang hong lai doc
nhu do trang tri.

- Mot the `.scan-bar` co `data-state` = load / bad / warn / ok / none. Mot diem tron
  `.scan-dot` doi mau truoc chu (mau doc nhanh hon chu), chu cung doi mau, va ca thanh
  nhuom nhe theo trang thai — de thay khi mat dang o cho khac tren trang.
- HAI tuoi, khong phai mot: "Ban chup cu 23h" va "Doc 4s truoc". Hai con so tra loi hai cau
  khac nhau — mot trang co the vua doc xong mot giay truoc ma van cu mot ngay. Tuoi ban chup
  chuyen mau canh bao tu no khi qua `PUSH_STALE_SEC`, khong cho ai doc dong canh bao ben
  duoi. Ca hai la cau co cho trong `{age}` chu khong phai nhan cong so tran: "read 23h"
  khong noi ro thoi gian chay huong nao.
- Moi khoa dung tooltip rieng (`scan.snapwhat`, `scan.readwhat`) noi ro "doc lai khong lam
  VM day du lieu moi" — cai nham lan tu nhien nhat khi thay mot nut Refresh canh mot so cu.
- Bo khoa `scan.read` (khong con cho dung). Nut Refresh giu nguyen id `#scan-refresh`, va
  giu padding to tren mobile bang mot luat rieng, vi luat cu gan vao `.toolbar`.

### D. Thanh Jump to — gio la muc luc dinh, co danh dau dang o dau

Mot hang 10 vien xam giong nhau nam tren dau trang: cho no nam la dung cho no vo dung, vi
luc thay duoc no thi nguoi doc DANG o dau trang roi.

- `.scan-jump` gio `position: sticky; top: 56px`, nen mo (thanh nav tren trong suot, noi
  dung chay phia sau — thanh trong suot se chong hai lop chu), co vien va shadow.
- Vien pill mang dung so 01..10 nhu tieu de tung muc, nen thanh va trang doc thanh MOT
  xuong song. Vien dang doc duoc to sang (`.active`, cong `aria-current`).
- `wireJump` theo doi scroll bang rAF, va co `jumpDispose` o muc module: moi `draw()` dung
  lai mot thanh moi, mot listener con tro vao thanh cu se chay mai mai moi khung hinh va
  giu luon cai DOM da chet.
- Hang pill cuon NGANG tren dien thoai (`overflow-x`, an scrollbar) chu khong xuong dong
  thu hai: mot thanh dinh ma cao len hai dong se day trang duoi tay nguoi doc. Vien dang
  doc duoc keo vao tam nhin bang `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest', inline:'nearest'})` —
  `nearest` ca hai truc de khong bao gio lam trang nhay doc.
- Them nut len trang dau (↑) va khoa `scan.top`.
- `.scan-sec { scroll-margin-top }` tu 72px len 116px: 56 thanh nav cong ~46 thanh dinh.
  So nay TRUNG voi `OFFSET` trong `wireJump` — mot muc duoc tinh la "dang doc" dung luc
  tieu de cua no thoat khoi thanh dinh. Sua mot cho phai sua cho kia.

Khong dung lai `ui/stickyToc.ts` (Learn tab) du no gan nhu du chuc nang: no tu do muc tu
DOM va giu MOT the hien duy nhat o muc module, nen mount o day se thao listener cua thanh
ben Learn va dung chung luon khoa bookmark `learn:bookmark`. Muoi muc viet tay thi khong
can do tim.

### E. Da chay

- packages/core: build sach (khong doi gi ben core).
- apps/desktop: tsc sach, 185 test pass (14 tep), npm run build sach. Khong co test nao cho
  `scannerTab.ts` — tab nay toan DOM ma vitest o day chay environment node.
- package-lock.json khong doi.

### F. Phai tan mat xem lai

- Thanh dinh khi cuon xuong: no dung duoi thanh nav, khong bi chu chay xuyen qua, va vien
  pill sang len dung muc dang doc.
- Bam mot vien pill: tieu de muc khong bi nam duoi thanh dinh.
- Tren dien thoai: hang pill cuon ngang, nhan "Den phan" an di, nut Refresh du to de bam.
- Doi ngon ngu va doi theme roi cuon lai — listener khong duoc nhan doi (vien pill khong
  nhay giat).
- Mau cua thanh trang thai o ca ba trang thai: binh thuong (xanh), co van de (vang), loi
  doc (do).

### G. Con no (giu nguyen tu CHAT-27)

- Loi nhan f0b6b0e9 viet "22 tieu chi" (dung la 26) — sua phai force-push, de nguyen.
- Ban build Pages cua lux-lookthrough van do: chay `wrangler pages deploy dist
  --project-name the-professional` tu apps/desktop tren may co dang nhap Cloudflare.

## CHAT-29 — Ke hoach giao dich tinh theo mot ngay trong qua khu, hoi ChatGPT cac tieu chi con lai, gia/ly do thoat, va luu thanh case study

Commit: 300aad7b, 1ed5199e, 56960ab9, 364105d7, 1579c7be, 3323b042.

### A. Yeu cau

Nguyen van cua user, gom bay phan:

1. Doi ngay giao dich ve mot ngay trong qua khu thi MOI THU phai tinh lai theo dung thoi
   diem do — cac danh gia, entry, stop, target, tat ca.
2. Luu duoc thanh case study, va "innovate cai case study saving luon".
3. Mot nut "Ask ChatGPT" chay mot prompt cho tat ca cac tieu chi con lai (loai yes/no) de
   user tu nghien cuu tai thoi diem do, ChatGPT giai thich va viet mot doan summary de copy
   vao ghi chu.
4. Do thi trong trade plan nen co them GIA THOAT de user dien khi muon luu thanh case study
   — con neu chi de MUA thi khong can.
5. Phan thoat nen co mot cho de bo LY DO vao.
6. Trang trade plan nen co nut XEM ke hoach, chu hien tai chi co in/tai file xuong.
7. Khi print to PDF thi khong giu duoc mau nen dep nhu tren HTML — phai giu duoc.

### B. Du lich thoi gian lam bang cach cat nen, khong phai tinh lai cong thuc

Diem then chot: khong co cong thuc nao bi sua. Chi co mot viec duy nhat — cat bo nhung nen
sau ngay da chon, roi cho toan bo may cham diem chay tren phan con lai.

- `asOfBars()` giu lai cac nen co `date <= planDate`.
- `planBars` giu phan da cat (day la thu duy nhat viec cham diem duoc nhin thay).
- `planAllBars` giu toan bo nen da tai ve.
- `planPeriod()` noi rong khoang tai ve, vi mot ngay cach day hai nam van can 8 thang nen
  phia truoc no de ve duoc cai nen gia.

Nhung gi KHONG the tinh lai, va da noi ro tren man hinh bang mot dong mau ho phach
(`.tp-asof-bar`): so tien trong tai khoan, so co dang giu, va cac muc cat lo dang cho o
scanner. Day la so du that o thoi diem HIEN TAI, khong phai o thoi diem trong qua khu; app
khong luu lai lich su so du theo tung ngay nen khong the dung giao dich do de tinh co vi the
"dang duoc phep mua bao nhieu" vao thang 5 nam ngoai. Noi ro thay vi doan la vi mot con so
co vi the sai se dan den mot ket luan sai ve ky luat cua chinh minh.

### C. Nut Hoi ChatGPT cho cac tieu chi con lai

- Chi gui nhung tieu chi con LAI: nhung cau yes/no ma may khong tu do duoc (tin tuc, cau
  chuyen, chat luong loi nhuan...). Nhung cai da do tu nen gia thi khong hoi, vi hoi lai la
  moi ChatGPT doan mot con so app da biet chac.
- Prompt mang theo ngay giao dich va yeu cau ChatGPT tra loi NHU THE dang dung o ngay do,
  khong duoc dung thong tin sau ngay do. Day la yeu cau kho nhat va cung la cai de sai
  nhat — nen prompt noi thang ra, khong an y.
- Tra ve: tung tieu chi mot dong yes/no kem giai thich ngan, cong mot doan summary cuoi de
  copy thang vao ghi chu ke hoach.
- Khong tu dong tick ho vao bang tieu chi. User doc, roi tu quyet dinh — mot cau tra loi cua
  may tu tick vao so tay se thanh "bang chung" trong ban post-mortem sau nay.

### D. Gia thoat va ly do thoat

- Khung "Ket thuc giao dich" tren the ke hoach: ngay thoat, gia thoat, ly do (chon tu danh
  sach 10 muc san), va mot o de viet ro bang loi cua minh.
- Tat ca KHONG bat buoc, va khong bao gio nam trong `buyBlocker`. Dat ngay hom nay thi khung
  gap lai san va co chu "khong bat buoc"; dat ngay trong qua khu thi mo ra san. Dung y
  "con neu ma buy thi khong can nhe".
- Vi sao co CA danh sach CO SAN va CA o tu viet: cau viet tay moi mang bai hoc, nhung mot so
  tay chi dang giu neu doc lai duoc theo nhom — cau hoi "nhung lan minh ban vi so thi ket
  qua the nao" khong the tra loi duoc tu muoi cau viet khac nhau.
- Tinh ngay tai cho: ket qua R, lai/lo phan tram, lai/lo tien, so ngay giu, ket luan.
  - R tinh theo CAT LO DA LEN KE HOACH, khong phai theo cho thuc su ban ra. Ban o 96 voi cat
    lo 90 thi do la mot lan thua 0.4R — vi R la so sanh ket qua voi phan minh CO Y dat ra de
    mat.
  - Hoa (`scratch`) khi chenh duoi 0.25%. Mot so tay ma moi lan +0.1% cung ghi la thang se
    bao mot ty le thang thuc chat la ty le tung xu.
  - So ngay giu KHONG bi chan duoi 0: thoat truoc ngay vao la user nhap sai, va hien -5 ngay
    la cach de thay.
- Du lieu thoat nam trong `PlanEdit` (tam thoi), KHONG ghi vao `SymbolPlan`. Vi `plan:NVDA`
  la ke hoach cho lan mua NVDA TIEP THEO; mot lan thoat ghi o do se con nam tren the do den
  sang nam, dinh kem cac muc gia da duoc tinh lai, ke ve mot giao dich dong cua ca nam
  truoc. Gia phai tra — dong panel truoc khi luu thi mat phan vua nhap — duoc chap nhan, vi
  nut Luu nam ngay canh cac o do.

### E. Luu thanh case study, va nut Xem ke hoach trong tab Case Studies

- Khong lam so tay thu hai. Mo rong chinh `CaseStudy` dang co, them hai truong tuy chon:
  `exitReason` (mot dong, ly do dong vi the) va `plan` (ban ke hoach dong bang).
- `CasePlan = Omit<PlanSnapshot,'lotId'>`: dung lai dung hinh dang cua ban dong bang theo lo
  mua, nen chi co MOT bo ma ve ban ke hoach trong ca app. Bo `lotId` vi mot case study co
  the duoc dung lai cho mot giao dich chua tung duoc ghi vao tai khoan nao — do chinh la ly
  do cho phep dat ngay giao dich vao qua khu.
- Doi cac muc gia sang USD khi luu: `CaseStudy` khong co truong tien te nao ca, va do thi cua
  no ve cac so nay nhu duong gia tren nen gia tho (tuc la dong do). Mot muc 198 EUR luu vao
  do se in ra "$198.00" ben canh mot cay nen 232 do.
- Trong tab Case Studies, ho so nao co `plan` thi co nut "Xem ke hoach": doc lai hang, bang
  tieu chi va cac muc gia dung nhu luc quyet dinh. KHONG tinh lai `pctOfFull` theo bang
  ladder hom nay — luu san chinh la de mot giao dich cu khong bi ke lai theo luat moi.
- Gia thoat doi nguoc ve tien te cua ke hoach bang ty gia cua NGAY LEN KE HOACH, khong phai
  ngay thoat: mot ket qua R ghep tu mot gia vao o ty gia nay va mot gia ra o ty gia khac thi
  mot phan cua no la bien dong ty gia. Neu khong biet ty gia nao ca thi de dau gach ngang,
  khong in con so do voi dau euro.
- Do thi chi duoc chay qua ngay giao dich khi da co ngay thoat, va dung dung TAI ngay do.
  Phan cham diem khong bao gio thay nhung nen nay. `planChartSpan` buoc ve lai ca do thi khi
  khoang ngay doi, vi `setOverlay` doi duoc duong ke chu khong them duoc nen.

### F. Nut Xem ke hoach, va giu mau khi in ra PDF

- Nut "Xem ke hoach" mo ban HTML ngay trong app bang mot iframe `sandbox` KHONG co
  `allow-scripts` — ban do la mot tai lieu, khong duoc chay bat ky thu gi. Do do moi dieu
  khien ben trong tai lieu phai lam bang CSS thuan.
- Mau nen bi mat khi in la do HAI thu, sua mot cai thi khong doi gi:
  1. Trinh duyet tu dong bo mau nen khi in de tiet kiem muc. `print-color-adjust: exact`
     (va ban `-webkit-`) la cach duy nhat xin lai.
  2. Chinh bang CSS cua ban in DANG tu doi trang: `@media print { body { background:#fff;
     color:#000 } }`. Dong nay thang moi thu, nen sua that la XOA no di.
- Muc in van la chi phi that, nen lua chon duoc chuyen cho nguoi doc: mot o tich "In tren
  giay trang (tiet kiem muc)", lam bang `:has()` va khong mot dong script. `display:none` o
  thanh cong cu khong ngan `#ink:checked` khop, nen o tich giu duoc trang thai trong khi bi
  an khoi trang giay.
- Ap dung y nguyen cho ban HTML cua case study (`caseStudies/report.ts`) — no van con dung
  dong bat trang cu.

### G. Da chay

- packages/core: build sach, 790/790 test pass (50 tep). Khong doi gi ben core ngoai
  `SETUP_KEYS`.
- apps/desktop: tsc sach, 220/220 test pass (16 tep) — tang 24 test, tat ca trong
  `tests/planExit.test.ts`.
- package-lock.json khong doi.
- Toan bo phan tinh toan co the sai duoc day vao `portfolio/planExit.ts`, vi vitest ben app
  chay environment node (khong co jsdom) nen chi ham thuan moi kiem tra duoc.

### H. Phai tan mat xem lai

- Doi ngay giao dich ve mot ngay trong qua khu: hang, diem, bang tieu chi va cac muc gia
  deu doi, va do thi dung lai o dung ngay do.
- Dong mau ho phach noi ro cai gi khong tinh lai duoc — phai thay no khi dat ngay qua khu,
  va khong thay khi dat ngay hom nay.
- Nut Mua van bam duoc khi chua dien gi vao khung thoat.
- Dien gia thoat: nam o thong ke hien ra, va duong thoat mau tim hien tren do thi.
- Dien ngay thoat sau ngay giao dich: do thi dai ra tan ngay do va KHONG di xa hon.
- Nut Luu thanh case study: hoi tieu de, luu xong mo tab Case Studies thay dung ho so.
- Trong ho so do: nut "Xem ke hoach" hien ra, bang tieu chi dung nhu luc luu.
- In ban ke hoach va ban case study ra PDF: mau nen con nguyen. Tich o tiet kiem muc thi ra
  ban trang, va chu mo van doc duoc.
- Doi ngon ngu sang tieng Viet: khung thoat, danh sach ly do va ban in deu doi theo.

  --project-name the-professional` tu apps/desktop tren may co dang nhap Cloudflare.

  kia.

- Ghi chu le ben canh tung muc cho sach Learn: chua lam.

## CHAT-30 — Chart 6 thang quanh ngay giao dich, gia thoat goi y, va bang ly do thoat tu them duoc

Commit: b16b5438.

### A. Yeu cau

Nguyen van cua user, gom bon phan:

1. Trong trade plan, neu user chon mot ngay trong qua khu thi chart khong nen chi show den
   ngay hom do, ma nen show 6 thang around ngay hom do (vi du 4 thang truoc, 2 thang sau neu
   du du lieu). Neu ngay do rat gan day, chua du 2 thang, thi show toan bo 6 thang cho toi
   ngay gan nhat, around cai diem entry do. Neu nam giu lau hon thi can khoang thoi gian
   lau hon.
2. Khi chon exit date thi exit price co the duoc goi y dua vao gia dong cua ngay hom do neu
   ngay do trong qua khu — tuong tu gia goi y buy trong buy form.
3. Danh sach "Why you got out" can them nhieu ly do: Price falls and touches EMA10, touches
   EMA21, Bearish Reversal Candlestick Pattern (thuat ngu chung) voi cac mo hinh cu the
   Bearish Engulfing, Shooting Star, Evening Star, Hanging Man, va climax top voi khoi luong
   lon. Cho nhung ly do do vao mot configurable table de user chon hoac tu tao them ly do
   moi, roi ly do moi xuat hien trong dropdown exit.
4. Nhung ly do nay integrate luon vao phan Sell trong Portfolio.

### B. Cai da lam — chart la BUC ANH, khong phai la PHAN XET

Cho toi CHAT-29, may thoi gian lam bang cach cat thanh nen: `asOfBars()` giu `date <=
planDate`, `planBars` la phan cat theo ngay, `planAllBars` la tat ca thanh nen tai ve. Loi
truoc day la gop hai thu khac nhau vao mot: chart cung bi cat tai ngay giao dich.

Cai phai bao ve la PHAN XET: diem chat luong, cac contraction, xep hang RS, stop goi y, va
hang chu. Tat ca nhung thu do van tinh tu `planBars` va CHAT-30 khong noi rong ra mot chut
nao. Chart la BUC ANH, va mot buc anh bi cat dung tai thanh nen vao lenh la buc anh duy nhat
khong tra loi duoc cau hoi ma case study sinh ra de hoi: cai nay co an khong.

`planChartWindow(bars, planDate, exitDate)` trong `portfolio/planExit.ts`:

- Bien phai = (ngay thoat neu no sau ngay giao dich, nguoc lai la ngay giao dich) + 2 thang.
- Bien trai = 4 thang truoc ngay giao dich; neu bien phai phai dung lai o thanh nen cuoi
  cung dang co thi bien trai lui them de cua so luon rong 6 thang.
- Mot chinh sach chung cho ca ba truong hop user neu: ngay xa trong qua khu ra 4+2; ngay gan
  day ra 6 thang ket thuc o thanh nen moi nhat; giu lau hon thi cua so rong ra theo ngay
  thoat.

Ham nam trong `planExit.ts` chu khong trong planner, vi tab Case Studies phai ap dung dung
cua so do cho dung ban ke hoach — hai ban sao cua phep tinh nay som muon gi cung se khong
dong y voi nhau ve chart cua mot ho so da luu.

Dieu nay THAY THE muc H cua CHAT-29 ("do thi dai ra tan ngay do va KHONG di xa hon").

### C. Gia thoat goi y

`closeOnOrBefore(bars, date)` tra ve gia dong cua phien do, hoac cua phien gan nhat truoc do.
Di LUI vi user chon ngay tren lich ma thi truong dong cua cuoi tuan: tra dung ngay se im lang
khong goi y gi cho moi ngay thu Bay va trong nhu app bi hong. Toi da 7 ngay cach, du cho cuoi
tuan va ngay le dai nhat; xa hon thi tra null chu khong lay gia cua thang khac ra goi y — mot
dau gach user tu dien la so con thieu, con mot gia cu nam trong o thoat la so SAI duoc luu.

`suggestExitPrice` chi ghi khi o gia con trong hoac dang bang chinh so app goi y lan truoc
(`PlanEdit.exitPxSuggested`), nen khong bao gio de len so user tu danh. Doi USD sang tien
hien thi bang ty gia cua NGAY GIAO DICH, khong phai ngay thoat, de nam cung mot khung voi moi
muc gia khac tren the.

### D. Bang ly do thoat

`portfolio/exitReasons.ts`: 30 ly do co san chia 7 nhom — plan (stop/target/trail/scaled),
ma (EMA10/21/50, mat chuoi MA), candle (nen dao chieu chung, engulfing, shooting star,
evening star, hanging man, dark cloud, gravestone, three crows, harami), volume (climax top,
churn, phan phoi, gap that bai), context (luan diem, thi truong, bao cao, tin, co hang tot
hon), discipline (het thoi gian, ban vi so, pha luat, khac). Dung het nhung ly do user ke ten.

`portfolio/exitReasonsDialog.ts` la bang cau hinh. Bon quyet dinh:

- `ExitReasonKey` la `string`, khong phai union, vi danh sach lon len luc chay.
  `exitReasonLabel` tra ve chinh cai ma khi khong tim thay, nen mot ly do bi xoa khong bao
  gio lam trong ly do cua lenh da luu.
- Ly do co san chi XEM, khong sua khong xoa: moi ly do la mot MA da nam tren cac lenh cu, doi
  ten "Stop hit" khong phai la them mot ly do, ma la viet lai am tham xem ca mot nam ghi chep
  noi rang da xay ra chuyen gi. Ly do user tu them mang tien to `my:`, bao dam khong bao gio
  trung voi mot ly do app phat hanh sau nay; `exitReasonsFrom` BO mot dong tu them neu ma cua
  no trung ma co san.
- Luu ca MA va ca CAU CHU. `CaseStudy.exitReason` / `SellRecord.note` giu cau chu nguoi doc,
  `exitReasonKey` la phan dem duoc. Khong can migration, va dem khong con phu thuoc vao app
  dang o ngon ngu nao.
- Ly do tu them nam trong `pf_playbook_cfg.exitReasons`, khoa da dong bo va da co mot ham nap
  ma moi noi dung deu goi — khong sinh them mot khoa nua de quen nap.

Hop thoai la modal tu viet chu khong phai `formDialog`, vi day la mot bang lon len trong khi
dang mo; dong moi vao mot mang cuc bo va chi ghi khi bam Luu, nen Huy la mot lenh hoan tac
that. Vao duoc tu hai cho: nut banh xe canh dropdown trong Trade Planner, va nut nhan trong
goc the Buy o tab Portfolio.

### E. Noi vao phan Ban cua Portfolio

Dung hop thoai Ban tren tung dong (duong user that su dong lenh), them mot o chon "Ly do ban
(khong bat buoc)". `ui/forms.ts` duoc mo rong de `Field.options` co `group`, roi render thanh
`<optgroup>` — gom nhom chu khong trai phang 30 ly do ra mot danh sach khong doc duoc. Moi ban
ghi ban duoc gan `exitReasonKey`, va bang giao dich hien mot vien thuoc tim trong o Ngay ban
chu khong them cot thu 16 (dong tien mat dang `colspan="4"` nen doi cot rat de vo).

Tab Case Studies van la o nhap tay tu do; `exitReasonKeyOfText` doi chieu cau chu ve lai ma,
hieu ca dang "Nhan — ghi chu" ma planner ghi ra, va doi chieu duoc ca hai ngon ngu.

### F. Loi da mac trong phien nay

- `exitReasons` da bi XOA am tham moi lan user bam Luu hoac Reset trong hop thoai Playbook,
  vi hop thoai do dung mot `PlaybookConfig` moi. Sua ca hai cho: mang `cfg.exitReasons ?? []`
  qua, kem ghi chu rang mot ly do user tu danh khong co gia tri mac dinh de tro ve.
- `shiftMonths` sai mui gio: `new Date(iso + 'T00:00:00')` la nua dem GIO DIA PHUONG con
  `toISOString()` bao theo UTC, nen o phia dong Greenwich mot phep dich 0 thang tra ve som
  mot ngay. Sua sang UTC ca hai dau (`'T00:00:00Z'`, `setUTCMonth`). Vo hai o mep mot chart,
  va sai o moi cho khac — dung loai loi bi copy sang noi no co y nghia.
- Tai lieu cua `closeOnOrBefore` ban dau noi nguoc voi code (bao tra null khi qua du lieu,
  thuc te tra gia cuoi cung). Them `MAX_STALE_DAYS = 7` va viet lai cho khop.
- `tests/planExit.test.ts` con import `exitReasonLabel` tu `planExit.js` sau khi bo tu vung
  chuyen di — sua lai danh sach import va viet lai phep kiem tieng Viet qua `exitReasonText`.
- `.pill` khong ton tai trong `styles.css`. Doi sang `.badge` cong mau inline.

### G. Kiem tra

- apps/desktop: 261/261 phep kiem trong 17 tep. packages/core: 790/790 trong 50 tep.

  chu ve ma, escape nhan user danh).

- package-lock.json khong doi.

### H. Phai tan mat xem lai

  khong dung lai o ngay do. Diem va hang chu khong doi so nao so voi truoc.

- Dien ngay thoat cach ngay giao dich 8 thang: chart rong ra theo, phu ca ngay thoat.

## CHAT-31 — Doi Trade Date thi tinh lai het, gia lam tron 2 so, va diem ngay bao cao tren do thi

Commit: e4f2dfdd, 23b87037.

### A. Yeu cau

Nguyen van cua user, gom ba phan:

1. Khi chon Trade Date, the entry price, target, stop co ve nhu khong thay doi va van lay theo
   ngay hien tai. Cac danh gia criteria phai dua vao du lieu truoc ngay do de xem co pass hay
   khong. Khi chon trade date thi moi thu phai duoc refresh.
2. Exit price nen rounded toi 2 decimal.
3. The graph nen co earning date as well neu trong timeframe.

### B. Loi thu nhat — khong phai o cho toi tuong

`computePlans` KHONG sai: no da quet lai `scanQm` tren phan cat as-of tu CHAT-29, va
`planRegime()` cung doc thi truong cua ngay do. Loi nam dung mot dong sau:

    const edit: PlanEdit = prev ?? { entry: usdToLevel(plan.entry), ... };

`prev` la `planEdits.get(sym)`, va ghi chu cu noi "keep any prior user edits". Nhung no giu
luon ba muc gia SUY RA tu ngay TRUOC, cong doan giai thich va phong doan setup cua screener.
Ket qua: diem chat luong, contraction va RS chay ve 2024 trong khi entry/stop/target dung o
gia hom nay.

Phan te nhat: hai tieu chi duoc cham TU chinh ba o do — `rMultiple` va `stopPct` trong
`cardGrade` — nen hai tieu chi cua bang 26 tieu chi da bi cham bang so cua nam sai. Nghia la
loi nay khong chi lam sai cai hien ra, no lam sai ca hang chu.

Cach sua: mot co `planDateMoved`, dat khi o ngay doi that (`planDate !== wasDate`), va dong
seed doc `planDateMoved ? undefined : carried`. Doi ngay = seed lai the tu bar moi.

Nhung gi user SO HUU khong mat, va khong phai vi toi cho phep ma vi chung khong nam o day:
setup, cau tra loi tieu chi, ghi de hang chu va ghi chu tu viet deu di qua `persistPlan` vao
`plan:<SYM>`, roi tro lai qua `stored` — dung nhu khi mo panel moi. Rieng `exit` duoc mang
tay qua (`carried?.exit`), vi no la thu duy nhat tren the khong duoc luu o dau ca, va vi mot
lan thoat la su that ve mot lenh that: doi ngay ke hoach khong xoa no.

Co duoc xoa sau khi `rows` dung xong, khong phai trong ham xu ly su kien: neu xoa som thi mot
lan ve lai giua chung se lai giu so cu.

### C. Loi thu hai — hai so thap phan

`usdToLevel` chi lam tron trong nhanh EUR. Gia dong tho trong cache la
198.44000244140625, nen o USD con so do vao thang o gia thoat. Sua: lam tron ca hai chieu.
Mot muc gia duoc bao bang cent; nhung so sau cent la dinh dang luu tru lot vao bieu mau.

### D. Diem ngay bao cao tren do thi

Khong phai viet moi tu dau — ha tang da co san het:

- `adapters/earningsDates.ts` → `fetchEarningsReports(symbol)`, goi
  `/company/<SYM>/earnings-surprise` qua `functions/api/nasdaqcal`, cache localStorage 3 ngay,
  khong bao gio nem loi (tra `[]`), ticker `.VN/.HN/.UP` thi bo qua.
- `ui/charts.ts` → `CandleChart.setEarnings(marks)` va `EarningsMark`. Da dung o tab co phieu
  tu truoc; ba noi khac goi `drawCandles` thi chua goi bao gio, ke ca planner.

Nen CHAT-31 chi la noi day vao: map `planEarnings`, `loadPlanEarnings` (khong await trong
`computePlans` — day la mot cu goi HTTP ben thu ba, giu ca panel sau no thi moi lan tinh lai
se cham nhu mang), va `paintPlanEarnings`.

Hai diem phai nho:

- Marker nam tren SERIES chu khong tren khung, nen moi lan dung chart moi la mat het. Vi vay
  `paintPlanChart` goi lai `paintPlanEarnings` sau khi dung, khong chi goi sau khi tai xong.
- Khong cat diem tai ngay giao dich. Cung ly do nhu cac thanh nen (xem CHAT-30 muc B): day la
  buc anh chu khong phai phan xet, va mot bao cao roi vao may tuan sau diem vao thuong chinh
  la cau tra loi cho "roi sao nua". Khong co gi duoc cham diem doc map nay.

Gioi han phai noi ra: Nasdaq chi cong bo 4 QUY GAN NHAT. Mot ke hoach lui hai nam se khong co
diem nao. Nen co dong chu thich duoi do thi: co diem thi giai thich chu E va ghi nguon, khong
co diem thi noi ro la het du lieu chu khong phai tinh nang hong. `.tp-earnhint:empty` an di
khi ticker khong co ngay nao (co phieu VN chang han) — user khong xin earnings o the do.

### E. Mot cau noi da thanh sai tu CHAT-30

`wl.plan.asofbars` viet "setup, muc gia, hang va BIEU DO deu tinh tu du lieu den het ngay do
— khong dung gi sau ngay do". Tu CHAT-30 nua sau cua cau do khong con dung voi bieu do nua.
Da sua lai: bieu do hien them giai doan sau de thay ket qua. Mot dong chu noi sai ve chinh
tinh nang chong nhin truoc la thu te nhat trong ca man hinh nay.

## CHAT-32 — Co "E" ngay bao cao tren ca do thi in ra, va tra cuu luc mo chu khong dong bang

Yeu cau cua user chi co ba chu: "lam luon di", tra loi dung muc "con no" o cuoi CHAT-31 — ban
in ke hoach chua co co ngay bao cao. Nen pham vi cua chat nay la: dua co "E" sang trinh ve SVG,
roi noi day du moi noi dang dung trinh ve do.

### A. Trinh ve SVG: mot hang co moi o dai tren

`caseStudies/svgChart.ts` la trinh ve dung chung cho HAI ban in: ban in ke hoach
(`portfolio/planReport.ts`) va ban in ho so (`caseStudies/report.ts`). Sua mot cho la ca hai co.

- `ChartSubject` them `earnings?: readonly string[]` — CO DAU HOI. `CaseStudy` thoa interface nay
  theo cau truc chu khong khai bao, nen mot truong bat buoc se lam vo moi cho goi hien co.
- `CaseChartColors` them `earn`, `DARK_CHART_COLORS.earn = '#a855f7'` — cung mau tim voi hang so
  `EARN` trong `ui/charts.ts`, de ban in va do thi tuong tac danh dau cung mot thu cung mot mau.
- Vong ve dat o `earnTop = padT + 11`, tuc mot hang DUOI hang cua chat xuc tac (`padT`/`padT+8`).

Vi sao la dai tren chu khong phai duoi than nen nhu do thi tuong tac: duoi than nen la cho cua
mui `▲ ENTRY` (`y(low) + 14`). Neu ve o do thi dung luc mot ky bao cao roi gan ngay vao, hai ky
hieu se de len nhau — ma do lai chinh la truong hop dang can nhin ro nhat. Dai tren la cho trinh
ve nay von da dat cac su kien co ngay, va them mot hang rieng thi bao cao va chat xuc tac cung
ngay khong dap nhau.

### B. Ngoai khung thi khong ve gi, va day khong phai chi tiet nho

`nearestIdx` tim nen GAN NHAT — no sinh ra de khi user go ngay cuoi tuan thi mui ENTRY khong bi
troi ve thu Sau. Nhung neu dua vao no mot ky bao cao cach khung sau thang, no se dan ky do vao
nen DAU hoac nen CUOI cua khung va ve mot co o do. Doc len thi day la mot su thuc, chu khong
phai mot sai so lam tron. Nen co kiem tra khoang truoc:

    if (date < bars[0]!.date || date > bars[n - 1]!.date) continue;

### C. Dong chu thich, vi ky hieu trong SVG khong hover duoc

Marker cua lightweight-charts khong co tooltip (da ghi o CHAT-31 §C), va SVG tinh cung vay. Nen
duoi do thi co mot dong: "E = ngay cong bo bao cao (nguon: Nasdaq, 4 quy gan nhat)".

Khong co co nao trong khung thi KHONG co dong chu thich. Giai thich mot ky hieu khong ton tai la
loi hua ma do thi khong giu. (Do thi tren the o Trade Planner thi nguoc lai: no NOI RO "khong co
ngay nao trong khung vi Nasdaq chi co 4 quy" — vi o do user vua bam doi ngay nen can biet tai sao
cac diem vua bien mat. Ban in thi khong ai vua bam gi ca.)

CSS them `.chart-note` vao ca hai ban in. Luu y khi viet test: `.chart-note` LUON nam trong
`<style>`, nen kiem tra phai la `not.toContain('<div class="chart-note"')`, khong phai
`not.toContain('chart-note')`.

### D. Ngay bao cao KHONG phai chat xuc tac

Cach nhanh nhat de co co la nhap ngay bao cao vao `study.catalysts`. Khong lam, va day la ly do
duoc ghi ngay trong `ChartSubject.earnings`:

- Mot `Catalyst` la thu NGUOI DUNG tu viet, va no co BANG RIENG trong bao cao.
- Mot ngay bao cao duoc tra cuu tu Nasdaq, khong phai mot cau user khang dinh, va khong thuoc
  bang nao.

Tron hai thu se vua them vao dong thoi gian cua user nhung dong ho chua bao gio go, vua lam cau
"minh co ghi nhan rui ro bao cao hay chua" thanh khong con tra loi duoc. Truong rieng, ky hieu
rieng, mau rieng.

### E. Noi day du: sau cho goi, khong phai mot

| Noi | Cho goi | Lay ngay bao cao o dau |
|-----|---------|------------------------|
| Trade Planner — ban in the | `tradePlanner.ts` `cardReportInput` | `planEarnings` da co san tren man hinh |
| Case Studies — do thi tren trang | `caseSvgChart(win, {...study, earnings})` | `fetchEarningsReports` sau lan ve dau |
| Case Studies — file HTML tai ve | `caseStudyHtml(study, bars, earnDates)` | cung bien do |
| Case Studies — nut Xem ke hoach | `openPlanReport({... earnings})` | cung bien do |
| Portfolio — ban in o Mua | `reportNow()` doc `lastEarn` | `Promise.all` trong `run` |
| Portfolio — ke hoach dong bang cua lo | `openPlanReport({... earnings})` | `Promise.all` canh `barsFor` |

`caseStudyHtml` nhan `earnings` la tham so THU BA co gia tri mac dinh `[]`, va `PlanReportInput`
.`earnings` la tuy chon — nen moi cho goi cu khong sua gi van bien dich va van ve dung nhu truoc.

### F. Tra cuu luc mo, khong dong bang vao ban ghi

`PlanSnapshot` khong luu ngay bao cao, va co y. Ban ghi ke hoach luu thu APP DA QUYET DINH — muc
gia, hang, bang tieu chi — de sau nay khong sua duoc nua. Con ky bao cao roi vao ngay nao la su
thuc cua thi truong: doc lai no khong phai doc lai mot quyet dinh. Cung ly do voi ly do `bars`
duoc tai lai chu khong luu kem.

Doi lai: `planReport.ts` va `report.ts` NHAN ngay bao cao chu khong tu goi mang — de hai module
do van la ham thuan cua dau vao va van render duoc trong test khong co mang. Giong het ly do
`fxRate` duoc truyen vao.

### G. Case Studies ve truoc, danh dau sau

`openDetail` KHONG cho ngay bao cao cung luc voi du lieu gia. Do thi la ly do trang do ton tai,
nen no khong duoc doi them mot vong mang moi hien; khi ngay bao cao ve thi ve lai do thi. Hai
cai chan:

- `detailToken` (bien module, tang moi lan mo): mot lan tra cuu ve muon khong roi vao ho so ma
  user da chuyen di khoi. Chi kiem tra `#cs-chart` con ton tai la KHONG DU — mo ho so khac thi
  `#cs-chart` van ton tai, nhung no la do thi cua ma khac.
- Van kiem tra `$('#cs-chart')` nua, vi bam Back thi khong tang token.

O Portfolio thi khac: `lastEarn` nam canh `lastBars` va duoc XOA CUNG voi no (ba cho: doi ma,
`resetForm`, va khoi dau). Mot do thi ve tu nen cua ma nay voi ngay bao cao cua ma khac la mot
phat bieu ve mot giao dich khong ai dat.

### H. Kiem tra

4 test moi trong `tests/planReport.test.ts`: co trong khung thi co `>E</text>` va co dong chu
thich; co ngoai khung (ca truoc nen dau va sau nen cuoi) thi khong co ca hai; khong truyen ngay
nao thi giong het truoc; va ban tieng Viet khong lo chu tieng Anh.

tsc sach. apps/desktop 265/265 (17 file). packages/core 790/790 (50 file). Commit 1fdd9af3.

Nguoi dung nen tu kiem:

- Mo Trade Planner mot ma My, bam 🖨 hoac 👁: do thi trong ban in phai co chu E mau tim o dai

- Mo mot ho so, bam Back, mo ngay mot ho so khac: do thi khong bao gio nhay ve ma truoc.

## CHAT-33 — Trang co phieu di theo Trade Date, nut ↺ ve hom nay, va reset khi doi ma

### A. Yeu cau

Nguyen van: "1 thu co the tiep tuc improve la tren cai trang invididual stock ay, cai phan MRVL:
no actionable setup, quality 62/100. / In a confirmed uptrend ... / No complete VCP yet (1
contraction(s), base 5 bars). la dua vao thoi diem hien tai, can duoc dieu chinh lai tai thoi diem
chon trade date chu. 2. Khi ma lam xong (chon State Date xong, nhan buy hoac save case study ay thi
khi sang xem co phieu khac, moi thu nen duoc reset lai chu .... toi ngay hien tai) va cung nen co
mot nut reset de tro ve hien tai nhu vay ...."

Hai viec: (1) phan danh gia tren trang tung ma phai tinh lai theo Trade Date; (2) doi sang ma khac
thi moi thu ve hom nay, va them mot nut reset.

### B. Cai narrative KHONG phai la loi

Doan chu "no actionable setup, quality 62/100 ..." do `explainPlan`
(`packages/core/src/planning/narrative.ts`) sinh ra, duoc `explainHtml` trong `tradePlanner.ts` ve
vao `PlanEdit.explain` roi thanh ghi chu cua the. Cho nay da dung as-of tu truoc: `computePlans`
quet `asOfBars(...)`, va `planDateMoved` cua CHAT-31 da buoc seed lai moi the khi ngay doi.

Cai cu la MOI THU QUANH panel: trang individual stock (`ui/stockModal.ts`) van dung so cua hom nay
— quality score, setup badge, cac muc gia, do thi, stat grid. Panel di ve qua khu con trang thi
khong, tuc la hai con so trai nhau ma ca hai deu trinh bay nhu su that. Do la loi thuc su.

May man la `openStock(ctx, symbol, asOf)` da co san che do as-of day du tu hoi lam cac tab lich su
cua screener: `sliceBars`, `fundamentalsAsOf`, earnings loc theo `r.date <= asOf`, co nhan "as of"
tren tieu de, khong chay `patchWhenEnriched`, loc ca chuoi fundamentals-trend. Khong phai tinh
them gi ca — panel chi thieu duong de YEU CAU.

### C. Duong di hai chieu

Panel -> trang: `PlannerMount.onDateChange?: (date: string | null) => void`. Modal tra loi bang
`openStock(ctx, symbol, date)`. Ngay tuong lai KHONG phai du hanh thoi gian (`isPastPlan()` la
`planDate < today()`), nen truong hop do gui `null` va trang o lai voi du lieu hien tai.

Trang -> panel: `PlannerMount.initialDate?: string | null`, chi duoc dung khi panel mo tren mot chu
the MOI. Chieu nay khong nam trong yeu cau nhung la cung mot loi soi guong: mo mot ma tu lan quet
lich su 2024 thi trang la 2024, con panel bat dau o hom nay, tuc la tinh ke hoach gia song duoi mot
do thi 2024. Khi chu the khong doi thi bo qua `initialDate`, vi luc do ngay tren man hinh la ngay
nguoi dung tu chon va no lon hon ngay cua trang.

Giu lai viec dang lam: `plannerWasOpen = tradePlannerIsIn(body)` doc TRUOC khi go panel, roi
`wirePlanButton(ctx, symbol, plannerWasOpen, asOf)` gan panel tro lai sau khi ve xong. Khong co no
thi ai chon ngay cung bi mat cai panel minh dang lam viec trong do — bi phat vi da dung tinh nang.

### D. Nut ↺ ve hom nay

O ngay gio nam trong `.tp-date-row` cung mot nut `#tp-today` han ngay ben phai, khong dat chung voi
cac nut khac: no noi ve DUNG cai o do, mot nut "reset" cach ba o khac doc ra thanh reset ca panel.

Mo di (disabled) khi ngay dang la hom nay, chu khong an di. An thi gon hon nhung te hon: mot nut
chi hien ra khi ban da di lac la mot nut khong ai biet la co cho den luc di lac. Mo san thi nhin
thay tu dau va doc ra duoc la "duong ve day".

Ly do co nut nay khong chi la tien tay: danh mot ngay thi de, xoa mot ngay thi khong, va mot panel
ket o ngay qua khu se am tham tinh gia va size cho moi ke hoach ve sau theo ngay do.

### E. `setPlanDate` — mot cho cho ca hai loi vao

Truoc day logic doi ngay nam inline trong handler cua o ngay. Gio gop thanh `setPlanDate(ctx,
next)` vi "ngay da doi" co bon hau qua va ba trong so do de quen: dat `planDateMoved`, quy doi lai
gia exit theo ty gia moi (chi rieng exit, vi ba muc gia kia sap duoc dan xuat lai tu bars moi),
to lai nut ↺, bao cho host, roi `computePlans`.

Thu tu quan trong: `onDateChange` duoc bao TRUOC `computePlans`, va host duoc phep ve lai chinh no
— viec nay lam rong cai host cua panel. Khi do `computePlans` khong tim thay `#tp-results` va tra
ve ngay, va lan mount lai sau khi host ve xong moi la lan tinh ke hoach cho ngay moi. Tuc la mot
lan tinh chu khong phai hai, nhung cung co nghia `setPlanDate` khong the hua la cac the DA duoc
dung lai; chi hua la SE.

### F. Reset khi doi ma — va tai sao khoa theo tieu de

`planDate` la state cua module, song lau hon panel. Truoc sua nay: len ke hoach MRVL theo thang
3/2024 roi mo plan cua NVDA thi NVDA cung duoc size theo thang 3/2024, voi mot o ngay ma nguoi dung
khong co ly do gi de doc lai. Mot ke hoach sai ma trong nhu dung.

`openTradePlanner` gio so `mount.title` voi `lastMountTitle`; khac thi ngay ve `mount.initialDate ||
today()`.

Khoa theo TIEU DE (ma co phieu, hoac ten watchlist), KHONG theo phan tu host. Host la mot node moi
moi lan trang co phieu ve lai, va trang ve lai chinh vi cai ngay nay — khoa theo phan tu thi se
reset dung cai ngay nguoi dung vua chon, roi reset trang lai, mai mai.

### G. Doc "nhan buy hoac save case study" nhu the nao

Doc dung chu: reset xay ra o luc SANG XEM MA KHAC, khong phai o luc nhan Buy. Reset ngay tai luc
Buy se xoa mat dong "✓ bought into <account>" va lam panel nhay ve dau (cho do co tinh dung
`recalcPlan` chu khong ve lai ca panel). Neu y nguoi dung la reset ngay sau khi Buy thi doi mot
dong trong handler cua Buy.

### H. Sua them mot loi tiem an

`openStock` truoc day fetch `asOf ? '5y' : '1y'`. Con so '5y' phang nay la mot trang trong dang cho:
slice cat het phan ma fetch voi ve phia truoc, nen mot ngay 2019 khong con bar nao. Rut cong thuc
trong `fetchPeriodFor` ra thanh `fetchPeriodForDate(date, yearsBack, livePeriod)` trong `asOf.ts` —
do la cho thu ba cong thuc nay sap duoc viet lai. Nut doi khoang thoi gian cung dung
`widerPeriod(fetchPeriodForDate(asOf), period)` lam san, vi do thi ve tu ca cai slice.

### I. Mot sai buoc trong luc lam, ghi lai de khong lap

Da chay `npx prettier --write` len nam file. Repo nay KHONG co `.prettierrc` o dau ca va cung khong
duoc format bang prettier, nen prettier lay mac dinh cua no va viet lai toan bo: diff tu 185 dong
bien thanh 9537 them / 4003 xoa, rieng `styles.css` la 8350 dong. Cach chua duy nhat sach se la
`git checkout --` ca nam file roi lam lai tung sua bang tay. Da lam vay, `tsc --noEmit` sach,
265 + 790 test xanh, diff cuoi cung 209 them / 32 xoa.

`npx eslint` cung vo ich o day: khong co `eslint.config.*`. Cong thuc that su la `npx tsc --noEmit`
va `npx vitest run`, chay o ca `apps/desktop` va `packages/core`.

## CHAT-34 — Do thi ke hoach ve theo tien cua cac o gia, va panel khong bi dong khi doi ngay

### A. Hai bao loi

Nguyen van: "gio toi lai thay cac figures khac nhau o cac inputs (entry, target, stop) so voi cac so
lieu tren do thi trong trade plan. 2. khi ma chuyen trade date hay click refresh, the trade plan just
close, that's not good".

### B. Loi 1 — do thi va cac o gia noi hai con so khac nhau

Cac o Entry / Stop / Target chay theo `planCcy`, va `planCcy` tu chuyen sang EUR khi chon mot tai
khoan EUR. Bars thi la gia dong USD nguyen ban. Code cu xu ly bang cach doi NGUOC: nhan muc gia len
ty gia (`levelToUsd` tren the, `chartPx` trong ban in) de ve duong len nen USD.

Duong nam DUNG CHO — 100 euro thuc su la 117 do — nhung nhan nhan mot con so ma cai form ngay ben
canh khong he noi, va truc gia cung the. Mot the noi hai gia khac nhau cho cung mot muc la mot the
khong ai kiem tra duoc. Do la dung cai nguoi dung nhin thay.

Sua: doi CA CUA SO BARS sang tien cua cac o (`planExit.inCurrency(bars, rate)`), muc gia di vao do
thi nguyen ban. Duong va o nhap luon doc ra cung mot so.

Mot ty gia cho ca cua so, la ty gia CUA NGAY GIAO DICH, khong phai ty gia rieng cua tung ngay. Ket
qua la hinh dang USD nhan mot hang so: y nguyen cay nen, y nguyen EMA, y nguyen cac doan co lai, chi
doi nhan tren truc. Neu doi theo ty gia tung ngay thi se "dung hon" ve mat mot chuoi tien te va SAI
ve mat mot do thi — price action ma setup duoc cham diem tren do se chua ca nhung buoc chan cua dong
euro, va mot cai nen co the that lai hay vo chi vi ty gia. Phan con lai cua the von da nam trong khung
ty gia cua ngay giao dich (`usdToLevel`, `plannedPrice`).

Ap dung cho ca hai do thi: do thi tren the (`paintPlanChart`) va do thi trong ban ke hoach in ra
(`planReport`). Khoa dung lai cache cua `paintPlanChart` co them `planCcy` va ty gia, vi doi tien hay
doi ngay giao dich lam moi gia tren truc thay doi — phai ve lai chu khong phai chi day cac duong.

Giu nguyen mot quy tac cu: EUR ma khong co ty gia thi KHONG ve do thi. Mot do thi voi cac duong sai
cho te hon la khong co do thi, vi do la phan nguoi doc tin ma khong kiem tra.

### C. Loi 2 — panel bi dong khi doi ngay hoac nhan ↺

`tradePlannerIsIn` so bang `===`, con `closeTradePlanner(onlyInside)` dung `contains`. Hai ham nay
luon duoc dung thanh mot doi (`if (tradePlannerIsIn(x)) closeTradePlanner(x)`) nen chung phai hieu
cung mot nghia cho cung mot doi so — va chung da noi khac nhau.

Trang co phieu hoi ve `#modal-body`, con panel nam trong `#sm-plan-panel` BEN TRONG do. Ham tra ve
"khong co panel o day", `plannerWasOpen` luon la false, trang van go panel di (qua `contains`), roi
khong biet la phai gan no tro lai. Nen doi Trade Date, hoac nhan ↺, lam mat cai panel dang lam viec.

Day la loi cua chinh CHAT-33: co che gan lai panel da viet dung nhung duoc hoi bang mot cau hoi luon
tra ve false. Sua bang cach cho `tradePlannerIsIn` dung `contains` nhu ham kia.

  in.

## CHAT-35 — Nut luu case study noi bat, note thanh danh sach, va case study mang day du tieu chi

Ba viec mot lan: "nut save case study should be highlighted", "phan note ... nen duoc structured
dep hon (table hay mot gi do that dep)", va "case study nen giong trade plan mot chut chua tat ca
cac criteria ..... de sau nay doc lai tot hon".

### A. Nut luu case study

Truoc: `class="btn ghost"`, tuc la trong y nhu cai nut ⚙ ngay ben canh o chon ly do thoat. Mot
hanh dong CO GHI du lieu lai duoc ve nhu mot nut phu tro, nen nguoi dung phai tim no.

Sau: nut chinh, mau tim cua so nhat ky. Khong dung mau xanh `--accent` cua app vi mot nut day mau
xanh trong khung ke hoach se bi doc thanh mot nut Buy thu hai. Cach lam la KHONG viet lai mau:

    .tp-case-btn { --accent: var(--violet); --accent-ink: #1b0a2a; padding: 8px 16px; font-weight: 700; }
    html.light .tp-case-btn { --accent-ink: #fff; }

`button.btn` lay mau nen, quang sang, hover va trang thai disabled tu `--accent` / `--accent-ink`,
nen chi tro lai hai bien do la thua huong het moi quy tac. Muc `--accent-ink` phai doi theo theme
vi `--violet` la #c084fc tren nen toi va #9333ea tren nen sang.

### B. Note khong the la table — va tai sao

Khoi nguoi dung dan ra ("Stop EUR167.19 (13.7% ...) · Target EUR251.43 (2.2R) / 4 sh / ...") la
mot doan dac nam ngay tren phan tuong thuat setup — ma phan tuong thuat do da co `<h3>` va bullet
san. Note trong nhu lam nua roi bo do theo dung tieu chuan cua chinh no.

Nhung day KHONG phai HTML duoc render. Day la HTML duoc LUU vao mot note nguoi dung sua tay, va
moi duong doc/ghi deu di qua `sanitizeNoteHtml`:

- `ALLOWED_TAGS` = B, STRONG, I, EM, U, S, STRIKE, BR, P, DIV, SPAN, UL, OL, LI, H3, H4, A.
  KHONG co TABLE. Mot cai table se bi "unwrap", vao note voi cai luoi thao ra — te hon doan van cu.
- Moi thuoc tinh bi xoa tru inline `color` / `font-style` / `text-decoration` / `font-weight`. Nen
  `class="muted"` trong note cu DA BI BO TU TRUOC — do chinh la ly do khoi do doc ra nhu mot khoi
  chu phang khong co thu bac.

Nen dap an la `<h3>` + `<ul>`, moi con so mot `<li>` co nhan dam — dung cai hinh dang ma nua sau
cua chinh note nay da dung. Mot note, mot kieu trinh bay. Ly do khong dung table duoc viet ngay
trong `planNoteHtml` de lan sau khong ai "sua lai cho dep".

Lam nhat chi tiet phu thi dung CA HAI mot luc, khong phai cho chac:

    <span class="muted" style="color:var(--subtext,#99a2b2)">

class la cai stylesheet cua app biet; inline style la cai song qua duoc bo loc. Va phai la
`var(--subtext,#99a2b2)` chu khong phai mot ma mau tran: `remapLegacyNoteColor` cho gia tri la
nghi den, con mot `#ffb648` tran se bi doi am tham sang mau cua theme sang.

`planWords.ts` gio tra ve `PlanRow[]` (`k` nhan, `v` gia tri HTML, `tone?: 'warn'`) va ba ham dung
chung mot nguon: `planLines` (goi y truc tiep tren man hinh), `planNoteHtml` (note), va cac noi con
lai. Chia dong: Stop va Target moi cai mot dong — truoc day chung chia nhau mot dong sau dau `·`,
tuc la hai con so ma mot giao dich thuc su duoc lam tu do lai nam giua mot doan van. Risk cung
tach ra khoi Shares vi do la hai cau hoi khac nhau: "vi the to bao nhieu" va "bao nhieu phan tram
tai khoan dang bi dat cuoc".

### C. Case study mang day du tieu chi

Truoc: tai lieu xuat ra co ket qua, do thi, cac muc gia va ghi chu — nhung khong mot chu nao ve LY
DO. Hang chi la mot chu cai tren tieu de, khong co gi dang sau. Tab Case Studies da co nut `#cs-plan`
mo ke hoach day du tren man hinh, nen cac tieu chi CO tren app; cho thieu la CAI FILE.

Gio them muc "Trade plan it was filed from": setup, trade date, hang kem diem, phan tram co duoc
phep, xac nhan da doc (kem muc gia doi chieu, vi "da xac nhan" mot minh la khong the kiem chung khi
gia da doi), bon o gia da dinh, toan bo bang tieu chi, va "Plan note, as filed" — chi khi note dong
bang da khac `study.notes`. Planner copy note vao `notes` luc luu nen binh thuong in ca hai la in
mot doan hai lan; cho nao chung khac nhau thi cai khac nhau do moi la thu dang doc.

Toan bo muc nay co dieu kien: phan lon ho so viet tay trong tab nay, hoac luu tu truoc khi planner
biet dong bang ke hoach. Mot tieu de "Trade plan" tren mot cai luoi rong doc ra nhu mat du lieu.

`loadCase` CAST chu khong chuan hoa (khac `loadPlanSnapshot`), nen `study.plan` co the la bat ky
hinh dang nao tu duong dong bo. Grade duoc kiem tung truong (`Array.isArray(outcomes)`,
`typeof score === 'number'`) truoc khi `scorecardTableHtml` di qua no: mot so nhat ky nem loi khi
mo la mot so nhat ky nguoi dung da mat. Co test rieng cho tinh huong do.

Tai lieu nay van chi co tieng Anh (tab khong truyen `vi`) — dich mot nua thi te hon khong dich.

### D. Mot noi ve bang tieu chi, mot noi ve bo loc note

`apps/desktop/src/portfolio/scorecard.ts` (moi): `scorecardBarsHtml`, `scorecardTableHtml`,
`scorecardWords`, `SCORECARD_CSS`. Hai tai lieu doc cung nhau — mot cai la "dinh mua gi", mot cai
la "ket qua ra sao" — nen ve bang hai lan la cach mot trong hai thieu mot cot, hoac dao nguoc ✓ va
✗, trong mot file luu tu nam truoc ma nguoi dung khong tao lai duoc. Ca hai deu la file doc lap
khong co CSS ngoai, nen style di kem markup: ai goi cung phai nhung `${SCORECARD_CSS}`.

`safeNoteHtml` chuyen tu ham private trong `planReport.ts` ra `ui/richNote.ts` va duoc dung cho ca
hai. `sanitizeNoteHtml` can `DOMParser` that; test cua app chay `environment: 'node'` khong co jsdom
nen `DOMParser` la undefined va sanitizer nem loi. Ngoai browser thi escape nguyen van: mat dinh
dang, nhung khong bao gio phat ra markup chua duoc kiem. Tu viet mot bo loc bang regex thi te hon
that su.
