## CHAT-36 — In tren giay trang khong con le den, diem tho khong phai tren 100, playbook luu la ke hoach dang viet doi theo, ho so ghi dung dong tien

Yeu cau cua nguoi dung, 4 viec:

  1. "Khi ma tich ra de in mau trang ay, thi dang sau do o 4 margins co mau den
     trong rat ky."
  2. "Neu user answer all yes, no questions, the total points can be more than
     100? dieu do la nhu the nao."
  3. "Khi user thay doi trong playbook, khi save, thi tat ca moi thu phai (vi du
     nhu dang setup trade plan, va vao thay doi playbook, thi sau khi save, cai
     trade plan do phai thay doi chu, chi khi ma da buy, da save plan, save case
     study thi moi khong thay doi thoi)."
  4. Tra mon no da ghi o CHAT-35: do thi cua mot ho so EUR van ve tren nen USD.

### A. Bon le trang in ra mau den — do canvas, khong phai do body

Hop kiem "in tren giay trang" truoc day chi doi mau cua `<body>`. Vung LE TRANG
(`@page { margin: 12mm }`) khong thuoc body — no do CANVAS ve, va canvas lay mau
tu phan tu ROOT. Background cua `<body>` chi lan ra canvas khi `<html>` chua co
mau rieng, va `:root { color-scheme: dark }` — thu ca hai tai lieu xuat ra deu
khai bao — chinh la cai cho `<html>` mot mau: trinh duyet tu ve canvas toi cho
khop voi scheme da khai bao.

Nen ket qua la mot trang trang nam trong mot khung den 12mm.

Sua: them vao `@media print` cua CA HAI tai lieu

    html:has(#ink:checked) { background:#fff; color-scheme: light; }

Can ca hai thuoc tinh, mot cai khong du: `color-scheme: light` ngan trinh duyet
tu ve nen toi, `background:#fff` bit nhung engine van giu canvas mau xam. Cac
quy tac `body:has(#ink:checked)` cu van giu — doi mot minh root thi chu se sang
mau tren giay trang.

Cai bay khi sua: KHONG viet van xuoi dai vao trong `PRINT_CSS`. Mot dau backtick
trong day chuoi template la ket thuc chuoi, va TypeScript bao `TS1005` ngay tren
mot dong trong nhu la CSS. Loi giai thich da doi len khoi JSDoc phia tren.

Hai tai lieu in duoc trong app chi co hai cai nay: `portfolio/planReport.ts` va
`caseStudies/report.ts`.

### B. 174 diem, va vi sao khong quy ve 100

Cau tra loi that: dung, tick het thi duoc 146/146 (ho `base`) hoac 138/138 (ho
`pivot`), tong ca bang la 174. Trong so la TY LE TUONG DOI chu khong phai diem
phan tram — thi truong 12 diem, so tron gan do 4 diem — nen chung cong lai thanh
bao nhieu thi la bao nhieu. Quy ve tong 100 thi moi lan them mot tieu chi lai
phai got bot cac tieu chi nang, tuc la noi sai ve hinh dang cua bang.

Cai KHONG bao gio vuot 100 la `GradeResult.score`, vi no la `earned/possible`
tinh ra phan tram — va do moi la so dung de xep hang va tinh size.

Nen day la loi nhan nhan, khong phai loi tinh toan. O diem trong tab Hoc gio doc

    Diem tho   146/146 diem          Xep hang   A - 100%

va cau duoi bang noi ro "tick het thi duoc 146 diem, khong phai 100 — trong so
la ty le tuong doi (tong ca bang 174 diem), va xep hang lay theo phan tram".
Doan mo dau muc 11 cung noi "khong phai 100" ngay tu dau.

`GRADE_TOTAL` da duoc dua len dau `tabs/swingPlaybook.ts` va tinh tu
`GRADE_CRITERIA`, de con so trong van ban khong bao gio cu.

### C. Luu playbook thi moi ke hoach DANG VIET tu tinh lai

Truoc day moi nut banh rang tu chon cach lam moi cua no: nut o the Mua goi
`draw(ctx)` — ve lai ca tab, tuc la xoa sach form dang dien nua; nut o panel ke
hoach goi `computePlans`; luu tu tab Hoc thi form Mua giu nguyen stop cu.

Gio co mot kenh chung trong `portfolio/playbook.ts`:

    onPlaybookChange(fn): () => void     // dang ky, tra ve ham huy dang ky
    notifyPlaybookChanged(): void        // ban ra, mot listener nem loi khong
                                         // lam cac listener sau bi im

Ban ra CHI tu `#pb-save` va `#pb-reset-all` cua hop thoai cai dat. Nguoi nghe:

  • form Mua — `wireBuyPlan` dang ky `run()`, huy dang ky o dau moi lan wire lai
    (bien `unsubscribeBuyPlan` o muc module), vi `draw()` dung lai ham nay va
    mot listener bi ro ri se tinh lai mot form khong con trong trang;
  • panel ke hoach — `openTradePlanner` dang ky `computePlans(planCtx)`, huy o
    `closeTradePlanner`;
  • tab Hoc khong can gi: no ve lai moi lan mo tab.

Ca ba cho goi `openPlaybookSettings` gio KHONG truyen `onSaved` nua.

Duong ranh nguoi dung ve duoc giu: cai da mua (`Lot`), da luu ke hoach
(`PlanSnapshot`), da luu ho so (`CaseStudy`) la ban ghi cua mot quyet dinh da
xong, luu kem chinh nhung con so luc do — khong cai nao bi cham vao.

Vi sao KHONG ban tu trong `savePlaybookConfig`: `exitReasons.ts` cung ghi vao
blob `pf_playbook_cfg` do, va listener cua panel ke hoach tai lai bars qua mang.
Doi ten mot ly do thoat khong duoc ton mot vong tai du lieu. Nen hop thoai doi
LUAT thi ban, trinh sua doi mot CAI NHAN thi khong.

An toan khi chay lai: `apply()` trong `portfolioTab.ts` van giu stop/muc tieu
nguoi dung tu go (bao lai la "kept"), nen viec nay chi doi nhung con so do app
tu tinh ra.

### D. Ho so EUR ve tren nen EUR — tra mon no cua CHAT-35

`CaseStudy.currency?: 'USD' | 'EUR'` (KHONG CO = USD, tuc la moi ho so luu truoc
2026-09-29). Mot ho so USD van duoc ghi voi truong nay VANG MAT chu khong phai
`'USD'`, de chi co mot cach bieu dien "bang do" va blob dong bo khong sinh diff
tren moi ho so cu.

Chieu quy doi da doi han:

  • truoc: `saveCaseStudy` doi cac muc gia EUR sang USD roi moi luu (`levelToUsd`),
    vi ho so khong co cho nao noi khac duoc. Nhat quan ben trong nhung khong doc
    duoc: mot lenh vao o 167 EUR bi ghi thanh "$194.12" ngay ben canh muc ke
    hoach dong bang ghi "167,19 EUR" — mot lenh, hai gia, trong cung mot tai lieu.
    Ho so tu go bang tay theo EUR thi cac duong ke ve lech hoan toan.
  • gio: muc gia luu DUNG NHU DA NHAP, va NEN NEN duoc quy doi de gap chung —
    `inCurrency(bars, rate)`, MOT ty gia cua ngay then chot, giong huong ma
    `planReport.ts` da chon.

Cac cho da sua:

  • `caseStudyHtml(study, bars, earnings, fxRate)` — ho so EUR khong co ty gia
    thi BO do thi kem mot dong giai thich, chu khong ve duong ke lech truc;
  • `caseSym(study)` trong `caseStudiesTab.ts` — khong con hardcode `'$'`;
  • ty gia lay tu cache thiet bi (`ensureEurUsd`), mo mot ho so khong duoc khoi
    dong mot lan tai du lieu thi truong;
  • o `#cs-plan` co HAI ty gia khac nhau, va gop chung lai la mot loi dang goi
    ten: `planFx` de ve muc gia EUR tren nen nen USD (can cho MOI ke hoach EUR),
    con `rate` de doi gia thoat cua ho so sang dong tien cua ke hoach (chi can
    khi hai ben khac nhau, tuc la chi con voi ho so cu);
  • trinh sua co o chon dong tien `#f-ccy`;
  • `CaseStudyPromptContext.currency` — de khong bat model doi chieu gia EUR voi
    bang gia USD no tu tra cuu, roi tra loi rang so lieu cua nguoi dung bi sai.

## CHAT-37 — Cau hinh cam nang tu giai thich: the trang thai thi truong, chuoi giam rui ro, bang luat chia nhom va mot vi du song

Yeu cau cua nguoi dung, mot cau nhung ba viec:

  1. "make the playbook seetings more beautiful, colorful, highlighted important
     things, nicer table"
  2. "more explaination for the part of Rules per setup, (currently user will not
     understand what is the Stop anchor, N Sessions, Pad.... and so on"
  3. "va cai dong dau Range SPY 771.35 .... co nghia la gi .... lay tu dau lam
     cho no noi bat hon di"

### A. Vi sao `title=` khong bao gio du

Muoi mot cot cua bang "Luat theo tung thiet lap" truoc day duoc giai thich bang
thuoc tinh `title=` viet tay canh tung the `<th>`. Ba van de, va ca ba deu la ly
do nguoi dung bao khong hieu:

  — Dien thoai khong hover duoc. Tren dien thoai lop giai thich do KHONG TON TAI.
  — Mot cau moi o. Du de nhac lai cho nguoi da biet, khong du de day nguoi chua
    biet.
  — Nguoi doc phai DOAN la co cai gi de hover. Khong co dau hieu nao noi rang
    chu "Pad %" co mot cau giai thich an sau no.

Va vi loi giai thich nam canh the `<th>` chu khong nam cung cho voi cot, doi ten
cot hoac doi thu tu cot thi loi giai thich o lai — chi vao cai o khac — ma khong
co gi bao loi.

Nen cach sua khong phai la viet title dai hon. Loi giai thich phai thanh NOI
DUNG: mot phan chu giai HIEN RA, cung thu tu va cung mau voi cac cot.

### B. Mot bang duy nhat sinh ra ba thu

`apps/desktop/src/ui/playbookHelp.ts` (moi, khong dung DOM):

  — `SETUP_COLUMNS` la mot mang co thu tu cua
    `{ field, group, vi, en, whatVi/En, whyVi/En }`. `whatVi` noi CON SO DO LA GI,
    `whyVi` noi SUA NO THI DIEU GI DOI — hai cau khac nhau, va cai thu hai la cai
    cu bi thieu.
  — `playbookSettings.ts` lap qua chinh mang do BA LAN: dai ten nhom + dong ten
    cot, cac o `<td>` de nhap, va cac the chu giai ben duoi bang. Nen khong the
    them cot ma quen giai thich, va khong the doi ten cot ma loi giai thich lech
    di: khong co danh sach thu hai nao de lech.
  — `RULE_GROUPS` chia 11 cot thanh BA CAU HOI, vi that ra bang chi tra loi ba
    cau: cho nao chung minh minh sai (`stop`), cho nao chot phan dau (`target`),
    phan con lai chay the nao (`manage`).

Kiem tra bang test: moi truong sua duoc cua `SetupRule` phai co dung mot dong
trong `SETUP_COLUMNS`, cac cot cung nhom phai nam LIEN NHAU (vi dong dai nhom
dung mot `colspan`, nhom bi tach thi dai se trum lech o), va `stop` phai dung
truoc `target` — ai doc "R muc tieu dau tien" truoc "Neo cat lo" la doc dap an
truoc cau hoi.

### C. Dong "Range — SPY 771.35 — MA50 ..." la cai gi

Do la mot dong xam chay dai, nam o dau hop thoai, in ra nam con so khong nhan.
Tuc la thong tin quan trong nhat tren man hinh — thi truong giam thi app tu choi
len ke hoach mua, khong can biet mau hinh dep the nao — lai trong nhu mot dong
debug.

Thay bang mot THE TRANG THAI (`.pb-hero`) noi bon dieu:

  1. TEN trang thai, trong mot vien bau to mau theo trang thai (`REGIME_DOC[r].color`).
  2. PHEP DO nao dat ra cai ten do. Vi du UPTREND_UNDER_STRESS = "MA50 van tren
     MA200 va van di len, nhung gia da tut xuong duoi MA50".
  3. NO TON BAO NHIEU ve co vi the — noi ngay canh cai ten gay ra no.
  4. Nam con so, moi con mot o rieng co nhan va co GHI CHU NGUON, cong voi mot
     chip so sanh ("gia TREN" / "gia DUOI" / "dang len" / "gian ra") vi mot con so
     nhu MA50 771.35 khong co nghia gi neu khong biet gia dang o phia nao cua no.

Va mot cau tra loi truc tiep cho "lay tu dau": app tu do tu NEN NGAY CUA SPY —
chinh du lieu tai ve bang nut ↻ Cap nhat o tab Portfolio — khong ai nhap tay, va
khong phai du bao: no mo ta nhung gi da xay ra tinh den het phien ghi tren the.
Khi chua du 210 phien thi the noi thang la chua do duoc va app KHONG ap khoan
giam nao — no khong tu nghi ra mot hinh phat cho du lieu con thieu.

Su that ve du lieu: SMA chu khong phai EMA (de khop TradingView), `atrRatio` la
ATR% hom nay chia cho trung binh 100 phien cua chinh no, va `null` — khong bao
gio la 1.0 — khi chua co nen tham chieu.

### D. Chuoi rui ro, vi cac khoan giam NHAN voi nhau

Truoc day chi in ra mot con so cuoi. Mot con so khong the noi tai sao no la con
so do, nen nguoi dung se sua no bang cam giac.

Gio la mot chuoi chip: bac (`dang hoc` / `dang chung minh` / `on dinh`) → co day
du → tung khoan giam mot chip → ket qua. Viet thanh mot HANG chip chu khong phai
mot danh sach gach dau dong, vi cac khoan giam NHAN voi nhau: hai lan giam nua la
mot phan tu, khong phai hai dong.

`cutText` va `stageLabel` duoc go bang `Record<RiskCut, ...>` va
`Record<RiskStage, ...>` chu khong phai `Record<string, ...>`. Nen neu core them
khoan giam thu bay thi BUILD do, khong phai hop thoai in ra `undefined`.

Luu y con thieu trong danh sach do la co y: hang tin cay (A/B/C/D) KHONG con la
mot khoan giam o day. No nhan vao SO CO DA TINH XONG trong `suggestSize`. Moi
khoan giam trong chuoi nay la mot quyet dinh APP tu lam thay nguoi dung; hang tin
cay la nguoi dung tu chon nho hon.

### E. Vi du song, va vi sao no goi `suggestLevels`

"Pad %", "N phien", "R dau tien" chi tro nen cu the khi thanh phep tinh. Nen duoi
phan chu giai la mot lenh tuong tuong duoc len ke hoach bang DUNG nhung con so
dang co trong bang ngay luc do — sua mot o thi vi du doi theo tung phim bam.

Hai quyet dinh dang ghi lai:

  — Vi du goi `suggestLevels`, chinh ham ma form Mua dung, tren 40 nen co dinh
    (`DEMO_BARS`, seed 99). KHONG viet lai phep tinh. Mot ban cai dat thu hai ma
    viec duy nhat cua no la duoc tin tuong thi den ngay hai ban lech nhau, nguoi
    doc se tin ban sai.
  — `readSetupBoxes(host, k)` dung CHUNG cho vi du va cho Save. Mot ham doc o duy
    nhat, nen vi du khong the dang tinh mot lenh khac voi lenh sap duoc luu, va
    quy tac "o trong nghia la gi" chi ton tai o mot cho. Save gio tinh hieu so
    tu ket qua cua ham nay — hanh vi y nguyen, doi chieu tung truong hop: o trong
    voi truong nullable → null, o trong voi truong khong nullable → giu mac dinh,
    so vo nghia → giu mac dinh.

Do thi la ly do phan nay ton tai o dang hinh chu khong phai doan van: VUNG TO BONG
tren do thi CHINH LA "N phien", va khoang cach giua duong day dut o day va duong
cat lo CHINH LA "Pad %". Hai chu do khong co nghia gi cho den khi chung la mot
khoang cach nhin thay duoc.

### F. `--pb-tone`: mot bien mang mau di khap noi

Ba mau co nghia va chung mang nghia di khap noi: `--danger` la cat lo (cho minh
sai), `--accent` la muc tieu (cho minh dung), `--violet` la phan chay tiep (quan
ly the nao). Dong ten cot, the chu giai cua no, va buoc cua no trong vi du dung
CUNG MOT MAU — do la cai giup mat noi "Pad %" trong bang voi doan van giai thich
no.

Moi phan tu doc `--pb-tone`, do renderer dat inline. Khong cho nao trong khoi CSS
moi ghi cung mot trong ba mau, nen cung bo quy tac do phuc vu ca ba nhom cot VA
bon trang thai cua the thi truong. Va vi moi mau la `var(--...)` hoac
`color-mix(...)`, giao dien sang (`html.light`) tu dung theo.

Hai chi tiet phai nho: `.pb-ch` va `.pb-gh` can `position: static` de pha cai
`th` sticky toan cuc (bang nay khong cuon doc), va `.pb-tbl tbody tr:hover` phai
`background: transparent` vi cac dong nay khong bam duoc — khong co gi phia sau
de mo.

### G. `DEMO_ENTRY` = 102 chu khong phai 104.5 — test bat duoc

Chu thich do thi viet "ban mua khi no vuot len tren nen". Test kiem tra cau do va
BAO SAI: dinh cao nhat trong 20 phien cuoi la 103.99 (cai rau nen mo dau nen),
tuc la gia vao 102 nam TRONG nen chu khong tren nen.

Da thu doi gia vao len 104.5 — dung nghia "vuot len tren nen". Ket qua: ca BAY
thiet lap tra ve `stopWiderThanAtr`, cat lo 4.4%—6.0%. Mot hinh minh hoa ma moi
dong la canh bao thi day canh bao chu khong day phep tinh.

Nen giu 102 (cat lo 2.1%—3.8%, la khoang nguoi doc can nhan ra, va hai thiet lap
van cham tran cat lo — dung mot canh bao dang gap o day) va sua CHU THICH cho
dung voi do thi: mot lenh mua dat tren dinh cay nen cuoi (101.46). Test gio khoa
ca hai dau: gia vao phai tren dinh nen cuoi, va khong thiet lap nao duoc tra ve
`stopWiderThanAtr`.

Day la lan thu hai trong du an nay mot test bat duoc VAN XUOI sai chu khong phai
code sai. Dang giu cach nay.

### H. Test

`apps/desktop/tests/playbookHelp.test.ts`, 17 test, khong can DOM (`apps/desktop`
chay `environment: 'node'`, khong co jsdom va khong duoc them devDependency nao —

  — moi truong sua duoc cua `SetupRule` co dung mot dong `SETUP_COLUMNS`, va
    khong dong nao tro vao truong core khong co;

  — `REGIME_DOC` phu het bon trang thai, `REGIME_STATS` dung dung nam khoa ma
    renderer tra cuu (`close`, `ma50`, `ma200`, `slope`, `atr`) — khoa thu sau se
    in ra `undefined`;
  — `DEMO_BARS` on dinh, 40 nen, `high >= max(o,c)`, ngay tang dan;
  — `exampleSteps` ket thuc dung o `stop` va `target` ma `suggestLevels` tra ve,
    cho ca bay thiet lap;
  — doi mot o thi so doi theo (pad rong hon → cat lo thap hon).

Toan bo: 303 test `apps/desktop` (20 file) va 792 test `packages/core` (50 file)
deu xanh. `tsc --noEmit` sach.

## CHAT-38 — Tieu de trong trang scanner va calendar noi len duoc: mot ham dung chung, chip co so lieu dat bang font mono

Yeu cau: "nhung tieu de nhu Rank history / 2 sessions / 2026-09-25 -> 2026-09-28
trong trang scanner hay o trang calendar should also be highlighted and more
professional".

### A. Ba cai sai, doc dung theo thu tu nguoi dung nhin thay

  — Chu tieu de dung `--faint`, mau NHAT NHAT trong ca bang mau, cho dong duy
    nhat tren man hinh co nhiem vu noi "ban dang o dau".
  — Duong gach chan nam tren `.section-title`, tuc la duoi CHU. Khi co chip ben
    canh thi duong gach dut giua tieu de, ca nhom doc ra nhu UI con sot lai.
  — Chip la `<span class="tag">` xam tren xam, chu so dung font thuong (khong
    mono), nen "2 sessions" va "2026-09-25 -> 2026-09-28" nhin giong nhau. Mot
    cai la SO LUONG, mot cai la KHOANG THOI GIAN; nguoi doc muon biet "co bao
    nhieu lich su o day" phai doc ca hai moi biet cai nao la cai nao.
  — Va vi khong co gi dung chung: scanner co ham `tags()` rieng, calendar viet
    tay tai cho, hai ben lech nhau.

### B. Ham dung chung: `src/ui/sectionHead.ts` (moi, 120 dong)

  — `sectionHead(label, chips, {tone, sub, right, id})` tra ve mot khoi
    `.sec-head` gom `.section-title-row` (h3 + cac chip + o phai) va tuy chon
    mot dong `.sec-sub`.
  — `chipHtml(input)` nhan `Chip | string | false | null`, tra `''` khi rong —
    de noi goi day thang du lieu vao, khong phai loc truoc.
  — `countChip(shown, total?, unit?)`: ra `12 / 40` khi danh sach bi cat, ra
    `12` khi khong. Mot so tran doc ra thanh "co 12 cai", day la noi doi khi
    thuc te co 40.
  — `rangeChip(from, to)`: gop lai thanh MOT chip, va thu gon con mot ngay khi
    hai dau trung nhau.
  — `ChipKind = plain | count | date | ok | warn | danger`. Kieu chinh la thu
    lam chip doc duoc trong mot cai nhin.

### C. Cai then chot: `#main .section-title` moi la luat co hieu luc

  `#main .section-title` co do uu tien (1,0,1), thang `.section-title-row
  .section-title` (0,2,0). Nghia la moi override ben trong mot row DEU phai co
  `#main` trong selector, neu khong thi khong an gi ca. Vi vay phan reset cua
  `.sec-head` duoc viet hai lan:

    #main .sec-head .section-title, .sec-head .section-title { margin:0; ... }

  Viet mot lan la mat 30 phut ngoi doan tai sao CSS khong an.

### D. Duong gach thuoc ve KHOI, khong thuoc ve CHU

  `.sec-head` ve duong gach bang `background-image: linear-gradient(90deg, ...)`
  voi `background-size: 100% 1px` va `background-position: left bottom`. 56px
  dau mang mau `--st-tone` (mac dinh `--accent`), phan con lai la
  `--border-soft`.

  Chon gradient chu KHONG chon mot `::before` hinh vach mau: co tu 6 cho tro len
  co tieu de bat dau bang emoji, mot vach mau dat canh mot emoji doc ra thanh
  rac. Gradient thi khong bao gio dung do.

  `--st-tone` la bien, khong co mau nao hard-code trong khoi CSS nay, nen mot
  muc ve rui ro chi can dat `--st-tone: var(--danger)` la duong gach doi theo.
  Cung quy uoc voi `--pb-tone` cua hop thoai playbook (CHAT-37).

### E. `.tag` thanh mot he thong chip

  `<b>` ben trong mot `.tag` LUON LUON la con so: font mono, mau `--text`,
  weight 700. Don vi de chu mo. Doc mot cot chip thi cac so thang hang va cac
  chu tu tranh ra.

  `.tag--count` co vien va nen nhuom `--accent` (vi "bao nhieu" la cau hoi chip
  canh tieu de hay tra loi nhat), `.tag--date` mono toan bo, `.tag--ok` /
  `--warn` / `--danger` nhuom theo trang thai. Tat ca la `var(--...)` nen theme
  sang di theo, va cac chip dat style inline (nut bat/tat nganh trong scanner)
  van ghi de duoc binh thuong.

### F. Escape: du lieu thi escape, khung thi khong

  `label` / `sub` / `right` la HTML. Bat buoc phai nhu vay: nhieu noi goi truyen
  emoji va `<span class="muted">` lam ghi chu phu, va o the ngay cua calendar thi
  label la `<span class="sec-date">`.

  `text` va `n` cua mot chip la DU LIEU va duoc escape ngay trong `chipHtml`.
  Noi goi KHONG duoc escape truoc, neu khong ma `AMD&` ra thanh `AMD&amp;`.

### G. Scanner: 10 cho

  Cho nguoi dung chi dung:

    const title = sectionHead(t('scan.sec.chart'), days.length
      ? [countChip(days.length, undefined, t('scan.chart.sessions')),
         rangeChip(days[0]!, days[days.length - 1]!)]
      : [{ text: t('scan.chart.none'), kind: 'warn' }]);

  Hai chip tra loi dung hai cau hoi ma khong co chung thi khong doc duoc do thi:
  bao nhieu phien, va la nhung phien nao. Con lai: cac muc ung vien, muc bi loai
  (so ma qua vong truoc + nguong cat), muc nganh, watchlist, va hai tieu de trong
  khoi `<details>` do cau hinh tho.

### H. Calendar: 9 cho, va luat "khong co du lieu khac khong co su kien"

  — Rui ro su kien cua toi: so ngay co rui ro (`8 / 14 ngay`) va NGAY NANG NHAT
    (`24.3% ngay nang nhat`). Tu 20% von tro len thi chip thanh `danger` va ca
    duong gach doi sang `--st-tone: var(--danger)`. 20% von vao lich cong bo cua
    mot ngay la luc no thoi khong con la chi tiet nua.
  — Khong co vi the nao cong bo: cau do thanh `sub` cua chinh tieu de, khong con
    la mot dong xam lac giua card.
  — 7 ngay toi: so su kien (`12 / 31 su kien`) va khoang ngay.
  — The ngay: chinh ngay do lam tieu de, dat trong `.sec-date` (mono 13px, mau
    `--text`, khong phai chu hoa 10px), nut "+ Them su kien" di vao `right`.
  — Ngay chua co du lieu ra chip `warn` "Chua co du lieu" chu KHONG ra "0 su
    kien". Day la luat so 1 ghi ngay dau file calendarTab.ts: nguon Nasdaq het
    du lieu sau ~3 tuan, mot o trong o xa la CHUA BIET, khong phai AN TOAN. Mot
    con so 0 tu tin la dung cach noi sai.
  — Top 7 / VCP / Mean Reversion: so ma, `12 / 40` khi bang bi cat, va chip
    `warn` so 0 khi khong co gi.

### I. Kiem tra

    `environment: 'node'`, khong co jsdom va khong duoc them devDependency).
    Chot: escape ca hai nua cua chip va ca tooltip; the duy nhat con lai trong

    `}` 1446.

    `cal.risk.peak`.

## CHAT-39 — Chu cua scanner doc duoc, cac phan thu gon duoc, bang xep hang nganh de nhin hon

Yeu cau: "ra soat lai tat ca cac trang xem co cho nao can improve nhu vay nua khong.
2. In scanner page BO or something like that should be clearly stated like Breakout.

ngay ca khi de che do tieng anh. 4. Thiet ke cac phan co the expand and collapsible.
5. Cac cai XLK, XLV ... sectors nen co explaination what they mean and which sector
they are ... Rank history nen lam de nhin hon, chuyen nghiep hon".

### A. `BO` la mot tieu de khong ai tra cuu duoc

  — Trang scanner nhom ung vien theo ma hai chu cua scanner: `BO`, `EP`, `MR`. Khong
    ai phai doan BREAKOUT la gi; ai cung phai doan BO la gi.
  — `src/tabs/scannerVocab.ts` (moi): `setupLabel('BO')` -> "Breakout (BO)", kem
    tooltip mot cau giai thich setup do la gi.
  — GIU LAI ma trong ngoac chu khong thay hoan toan: log cua VM, tin nhan Telegram
    va output cua scanner van noi `BO`. Bo ma di la cat duong day giua cai bang nay
    va thu da sinh ra no.
  — `VCP` khong in thanh "VCP (VCP)" — so sanh sau khi chuan hoa khoa.
  — Ma khong biet thi di qua nguyen ven, KHONG tu dat ten cho mot setup ma app nay
    chua tung nghe.

### B. "Why rejected" khong phai loi dich — no chua bao gio duoc dich

  — Cot ly do in ra dung khoa noi bo cua pipeline: tieng Viet khong dau, va no
    khong doi theo che do ngon ngu vi no khong di qua i18n lan nao.
  — `scannerVocab.ts` dich sang hai ngon ngu. `normKey()` ha chu thuong va gop moi
    chuoi ky tu khong phai chu/so thanh mot `_`, nen `khong_du_thanh_khoan`,
    `Khong du thanh khoan` va `KHONG-DU-THANH-KHOAN` deu vao MOT muc.
  — CHO DOC KY: tu dien nay DUOC DOAN RA. Scanner la repo Python khac, chay tren
    VM; trong repo nay khong co danh sach khoa ly do nao ca (da grep `by_setup`,
    `qua_loc`, `cho_fund` khap repo va ca hai file error.txt — khong co gi).
  — Vi vay tra khong thay KHONG bi coi la loi: `reasonLabel()` tra ve
    `known: false`, mot nhan da bo dau gach duoi cho doc duoc, va khoa GOC nam
    trong tooltip. `scannerTab` in dong do bang chu in nghieng (`.scan-raw`) — mot
    dong chua dich phai TRONG NHU LA chua dich.
  — Khi mot ly do la nhin thay in nghieng: them khoa do vao `REASONS`. Het. Khong
    phai sua cho nao khac.
  — `prettify('')` tra ve em dash chu khong tra ve chuoi rong: mot o trong doc nhu
    loi render. Test da bat loi nay khi con dang viet.

### C. Cac phan thu gon duoc

    NGAY KHI DUNG CHUOI HTML (`openAttr(id)`), khong sua sau khi mount. Trang
    scanner ve lai moi lan poll; mot `<details open>` bi JavaScript dong o frame
    sau la mot cai giat nhin thay duoc, moi lan.
  — Trang thai nam trong MOT blob JSON `ui_collapsed` cua localStorage, khong phai

## CHAT-40 — Tu dien cua scanner viet lai tu chinh source, khong doan nua

Yeu cau: "2. phan why rejected phan reason ban co the tra trong scanner repo ma".

### A. CHAT-39 muc B noi mot cau sai

  — CHAT-39 muc B viet: "tu dien nay DUOC DOAN RA. Scanner la repo Python khac,
    chay tren VM; trong repo nay khong co danh sach khoa ly do nao ca". Cau do SAI.
  — Repo scanner nam ngay canh repo nay: `Github/scanner`, cung thu muc cha voi
    `lux-lookthrough`. Moi khoa ly do la mot tham so `_rej(rej, "...")` trong
    `setups.py`, tim ra bang mot lenh grep.
  — Bai hoc: truoc khi dich, dat ten hay giai thich bat cu thu gi den tu VM, doc
    repo scanner truoc. Gia phai tra cho viec doan la gan nhu ca tu dien phai bo di.
  — Ba file dang de xem nhat: `setups.py` (cac cua ai va chuoi ly do),
    `config.py` (cac con so nguong, moi so kem mot cau giai thich VI SAO la so do),
    `push.py` (hinh dang chinh xac cua tung blob KV ma app doc).

### B. Source noi gi, va khac ban doan cho nao

  — Setup chi co BA cai: `BO`, `RV`, `LEAD` (`setups.py`: `rej = {"BO": {},
    "RV": {}, "LEAD": {}}`). Ban doan co 15 cai (EP, MR, VCP, PB, FLAG, HTF, CWH,
    IPO, SURGE, PARA, GAP, SQ, RS, 52W) — toan bo la setup noi tieng cua truong phai
    Qullamaggie nhung scanner NAY khong co. Bo het.
  — `RV` = ma da roi sau, dang cho MOT phien bat manh; trigger chi mo khi co ban dat.
  — `LEAD` = KHONG phai mot hinh mau ky thuat ma la mot BO SAN chat luong: ma du
    thanh khoan thuoc top 3 sector, manh hon SPY va con gan dinh. Day la cai de bi doc
    sai nhat tu chinh cai ten cua no, nen tooltip noi thang ra dieu do.
  — Khoa ly do KHONG phai dinh danh gach duoi ma la CUM TU tieng Viet khong dau,
    cach nhau bang dau cach: `kem thanh khoan`, `khong co nen tich luy`, `duoi sma50`,
    `sma50 dang di xuong`, `con xa pivot`, `da vuot pivot qua xa`, `day 52 tuan qua cu`,
    `chua giam du 3 thang`, `da bat len qua nhieu`, `co ban khong dat`,
    `khong biet sector`, `khong do duoc bien do`, `yeu hon SPY trong 21 phien`,
    `yeu hon SPY trong 63 phien`, `khong co so lieu RS (thieu ma chuan?)`,
    `khong co danh sach sector`.
  — `bien do khong co lai` nghia la bien do KHONG CO LAI
    (`atr_contract > max_atr_contract`). Doc nguyen van de hieu nguoc han, nen nhan
    tieng Anh la "Volatility did not contract" va tooltip noi ro: nen cang ve cuoi
    cang phai lang di, nen nay van rong nhu luc dau.
  — Mot so khoa co NGUONG noi thang vao trong chuoi: `gia < $10.0`, `rvol < 1.5`,
    `nen rong hon 25%`, `cach dinh 52 tuan > 15%`, `chua roi du 30%`,
    `thanh khoan < $20M/phien`, `bien do < 2% (khong du dong)`,
    `bien do > 6% (stop qua rong)`, `da du 2 ma cua XLK`, `qua tran 12 ma tong`,
    `tang < 5%`. Mot bang tra khop chinh xac KHONG BAO GIO khop duoc nhung khoa nay.
  — Ly do cua trigger trong phien (`chua vuot pivot`, `chua lay lai sma20`,
    `dang o nua duoi bien do ngay`...) co that trong `setups.py` nhung KHONG nam trong
    payload `scanner:rejects` (`push.py:rejects_payload()` chi chay lai `setups.scan`).
    Van dich san, vi khong ton gi va de payload sau nay co doi thi cung khong tro thanh
    mot dong chu in nghieng.

### C. Hai bang tra, va cai bay dau `$`

  — `REASONS`: khop chinh xac, qua `normKey()` (ha chu thuong, gop moi chuoi ky tu
    khong phai chu/so thanh mot `_`), nen `kem thanh khoan` va `KEM-THANH-KHOAN` vao
    cung mot muc.
  — `PATTERNS`: danh sach co thu tu, moi muc mot regex bat lay con so roi dat no vao
    ban dich (`{1}`, `{2}`). Con so CHINH LA thong tin cua dong do: "RVOL too low"
    khong noi gi, "RVOL below 1.5" noi ro nguong nam o dau.
  — Thay the bang MOT HAM, khong phai bang chuoi `$1`: doan bat duoc thuong chua `$`
    (`gia < $10.0`), va trong chuoi thay the thi `$` la ky tu dieu khien — `String.replace`
    se an mat no.
  — `da du 2 ma cua XLK`: ten sector di vao TOOLTIP qua `sectorTip()`, khong vao o
    bang. O bang la mot cot cua mot bang hep, va ca trang con lai goi no la `XLK`.
  — Tra khong thay van KHONG bi coi la loi: `known: false`, nhan da bo gach duoi,
    khoa GOC trong tooltip, in nghieng (`.scan-raw`).

### D. Nhung cho khac tren trang scanner van noi tieng may

  — Tile "Setups" cua bang Hom nay in `BO`, `RV`. Gio in "Breakout", "Reversal";
    ma goc nam trong tooltip.
  — Dong `by_setup` trong bang suc khoe: `BO 12` thanh "Breakout 12", ma goc trong
    tooltip — bang nay phai so sanh duoc voi log cua VM.
  — Thu tu cac khoi: `Object.keys().sort()` cho ra BO, LEAD, RV — mot thu tu khong
    ton tai o bat cu dau khac trong he thong. `bySetupOrder` xep BO, RV, LEAD dung nhu
    `setups.py` tinh; ma la khong biet thi xep sau cung.
  — Chu thich duoi bang "Why rejected" trich hai ly do lam vi du. Truoc day trich
    khoa goc; gio trich dung nhan ma bang in ra, de nguoi doc tim duoc dong dang noi den.

### E. `_qua_san`: con so thu hai cua LEAD

  — Rieng LEAD co `_qua_san` = so ma qua het cac tieu chi chat luong TRUOC khi ap
    tran theo sector va tran tong.
  — Chi thay "12 passed" thi khong biet la bo tieu chi qua chat hay tran qua chat
    — hai truong hop nay phai sua theo hai huong nguoc nhau. Nen khi `_qua_san` khac
    `_qua_loc`, tieu de LEAD co them mot chip nua.
  — Cac khoa `_` con lai (`_qua_loc`, `_bi_cat_tran`, `_cho_fund`,
    `_khong_lap_duoc_ke_hoach`) la DEM, khong phai ly do: bang loc chung ra khoi cot ly
    do va in thanh chip.

### F. Kiem tra

  — `npx tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit`: sach.
  — `npx vitest run`: 24 file / 362 test, xanh het.
  — `tests/scannerVocab.test.ts` viet lai: 15 test, moi khoa trong do COPY tu
    `setups.py`. Co test noi ro `setupLabel('EP').known === false`, va test kiem tra
    con so khong bi mat khi dich (`gia < $10.0` ra "Price below $10.0").

## CHAT-41 — Mobile: cai khung khong con bi tran

Yeu cau: "van chua toi uu cho phien ban mobile lam" — cu the la ba cho: hang nut
"Ask ChatGPT / View plan / Print plan" trong trade plan o trang tung ma va trong Buy
form thi vuot qua khung, cac o KPI (entry, stop) trong bao cao View plan cung vuot
khung, va bang tieu chi cung vay; "khong dynamically change khi ma size thay doi".

### A. Ba nguyen nhan khac nhau, khong phai mot

  — Hang nut: `.tp-note-head` la flex KHONG wrap. Ba nut mini cong lai rong hon the,
    nen chung cu chay tiep ra ngoai le phai. Chi can cho wrap la xong. Mot class nay
    dung o hai noi (`tradePlanner.ts` va `portfolioTab.ts` cua Buy form) nen sua mot
    lan la ca hai cho het loi.
  — Cac o so lieu: `1fr` trong CSS Grid co nghia la `minmax(auto, 1fr)`, tuc la o
    KHONG BAO GIO duoc hep hon noi dung cua no. Mot so tien nhu `$12,345 (12.3%)`
    bang phong chu so 15px khong the ngat dong, nen no keo rong o, va cai TRAN ra
    ngoai khung la ca luoi chu khong phai con so. Cach sua: dat san ro rang
    (`minmax(132px, 1fr)`) hoac `minmax(0, 1fr)`, cong voi `min-width: 0` va
    `overflow-wrap: break-word` tren `.stat`.
  — "Khong dynamically change": cua so KHONG phai la container cua the. Cung mot the
    trade plan duoc ve trong modal cua mot ma, va trong nua trai cua luoi `1fr 1fr`
    o Paper Trading. Voi mot cua so 1000px, the do co the rong 470px hoac 900px —
    mot rule `@media (max-width: 640px)` khong the phan biet hai truong hop do, nen
    no khong bao gio chay dung luc. `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(Xpx, 1fr))` hoi CHINH
    CAI THE, va do dung la y nghia cua "thay doi theo size".

### B. Da sua trong app

  — `.tp-note-head`: them `flex-wrap: wrap` va `gap: 5px 8px`.
  — `.tp-fields`: `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(118px, 1fr))`, va bo dong `.tp-fields`
    trong block 640px — lam o hai noi thi cua so lai ghi de len quyet dinh cua the.
  — Class moi `.tp-statgrid` voi bien `--tp-col` (mac dinh 132px): hai luoi so lieu
    trong `tradePlanner.ts` truoc day la inline `repeat(3, 1fr)` va `repeat(5, 1fr)`.
    Hang exit xin `--tp-col: 98px` inline vi cac o cua no ngan (`1.2R`, `+8.4%`).
    Mot media query KHONG THE ghi de mot inline style — do la ly do trong repo dang
    co may cai hack `[style*="repeat(4"] { ... !important }`; dua template vao class
    la cach thoat khoi kieu hack do.
  — `.tp-playfields` va `.tp-crit-row`: doi `1fr` thanh `minmax(0, 1fr)`.
  — Block 600px: `.card-head-actions` duoc wrap (hang tieu de cua the Buy: Reset /
    Playbook / Ly do ban, khoang 310px nut ben canh mot tieu de — `nowrap` la co y
    de cap nut khong bi tach tren desktop, nhung tren dien thoai chinh no lam nut
    treo ra ngoai le), va `#main .stat` bot padding 14/16 xuong 11/12, so tu 18px
    xuong 16px.

### C. Hai tap bao cao (View plan va case study)

Hai tap nay la TAI LIEU DOC LAP: co `<style>` rieng, khong dung styles.css. O day
media query LA dung cong cu, vi viewport cua mot tai lieu chinh la container cua no
— man hinh dien thoai, hoac chieu rong cua iframe `srcdoc` ma app dung de xem truoc.

  — Them `<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">`.
    Khong co dong nay thi trinh duyet dien thoai dan trang o 980px roi thu nho lai,
    va khong co CSS nao ben duoi cuu duoc chuyen do.
  — `body`: `padding: clamp(14px, 4vw, 32px)` thay cho `padding: 32px`.
  — `.grid`: `repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr))`, roi 2 cot duoi 760px, 1 cot duoi 430px.
  — `.stat`: `min-width: 0`, `.stat .v` them `overflow-wrap: anywhere`.
  — Khoi moi ten `NARROW_CSS`, dat canh `PRINT_CSS` trong TUNG file — hai file nay
    chia nhau stylesheet bang cach COPY chu khong phai import (PRINT_CSS cung vay),
    vi moi tap phai dung duoc mot minh.

### D. Bang tieu chi: bang khong the reflow

Mot `<table>` khong the doi so cot nhu grid: moi cot giu o rong nhat cua no. Nen
`scorecard.ts` bay gio:

  — Duoi 560px, hai cot cuoi (nhom tieu chi, nguon do) ROI KHOI bang (`.sc-fold`
    an di) va hien lai thanh dong thu hai duoi ten tieu chi (`.sc-meta`). Khong mat
    thong tin nao — cung nhung chu do, doi cho.
  — Bang duoc boc trong `<div class="sc-wrap">` co `overflow-x: auto` lam luoi an
    toan cuoi cung: mot o `measured` qua dai thi cuon ngang, chu khong keo ca tai
    lieu rong ra.
  — `.gbar-k` tu `width: 200px` thanh `clamp(92px, 30vw, 200px)`.
  — Ca ba thu tren nam trong `SCORECARD_CSS`, nen hai tap bao cao huong loi cung luc.

### E. Suyt mat file styles.css

Khi ap dung cac sua doi bang Python, `io.open(p, 'w')` CAT FILE VE 0 BYTE TRUOC KHI
ma hoa. Mot emoji viet duoi dang surrogate escape lam `UnicodeEncodeError` ban ra
SAU khi file da bi cat, va `styles.css` con 0 byte — mat het phan CSS chua commit,
phai `git checkout -- src/styles.css` roi lam lai tu dau. Tu gio: dung byte string
hoan chinh truoc, ghi bang `open(p, 'wb')`, khong dat ky tu ngoai BMP trong ma nguon
Python, va `wc -c` ngay sau moi lan ghi.

### F. Kiem tra

  — `npx tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit`: sach.
  — `npx vitest run`: 24 file / 365 test, xanh het (362 + 5 test moi, 2 file).
  — `styles.css`: 4855 CRLF, 0 bare LF, ngoac va dau comment can bang.
  — Test moi chi giu ba dieu khong the phuc hoi ve sau, vi mot tap bao cao luu nam
    ngoai thi khong the tao lai: co the viewport, thu tu so cot 4 -> 2 -> 1, va cot
    gap lai van con chu trong markup.

## CHAT-42 — Trang scanner: mot ngon ngu, va giai thich tan goc

Yeu cau: ba phan. (1) "tieng viet va tieng anh lan lon trong khi user chon tieng
anh" — chon tieng nao thi CA trang phai la tieng do. (2) "Minh muon co nhieu
explanation in details cho cac khai niem o trong trang scanner": Why rejected phai
noi ro reason nghia la gi, share nghia la gi, "count cai gi vay number of stocks
ah?"; tuong tu cho Reversal va Sector Leader; mot kieu summary "phai dat moc nao do
cua cac criteria"; watch list phai co "why it is on the list voi cac KPIs nhung so
sanh voi cai gi". (3) "Candidates by setup, nhung phan nhu Breakout (BO) ... cung
nen collapsible boi vi doi khi danh sach qua dai, scroll rat met".

### A. Cho lan tieng: khong co mot chu Viet nao bi hardcode

Dieu dau tien phai lam ro, vi no doi huong sua: `scannerTab.ts` KHONG chua mot chuoi
tieng Viet co dau nao. Tat ca cac chu Viet hien ra khi dang o che do tieng Anh den
tu bon nguon khac:

  — `PLAYBOOK[...]["note"]` do chinh VM gui len, la tieng Viet CO DAU, va duoc in
    tho o hai cho: the playbook o muc Today va bang playbook o muc Config. Day la
    cho on nhat.
  — Identifier dung thang lam nhan: `bars` / `struct` / `candidates` o bang Status,
    `kind` = `NEW` / `UP` o bang Alerts, ten nhom va ten khoa cua config (`slope_win`,
    `vol_contract`...), `SPIKE` trong o playbook, `UPTREND` / `CONTRACTED` trong bang
    playbook. Cac chuoi nay khong phai tieng Anh cung khong phai tieng Viet — chung
    la khoa schema, va khong ai ngoai nguoi viet scanner doc duoc.
  — Bon khoa reject chua co ban dich tu CHAT-40 (`thieu_gia_pivot`, `thieu_gia`,
    `khong_dong_o_vung_dinh_ngay`, `gap_to_entry_xau`).

Cach sua: `scannerVocab.ts` nhan them `PLAYBOOK_NOTES` (12 o, khoa theo
`(trend, vol)`), `ALERT_KINDS` va `TABLES`. Ban tieng Viet cua ghi chu playbook duoc
COPY NGUYEN VAN tu `config.py` — khong dich lai, khong viet hay hon — vi dung nhung
chuoi do cung di vao tin nhan Telegram buoi sang, va hai noi noi khac nhau ve cung
mot o playbook thi tin nhan hay trang web se bi coi la sai. Bang playbook o muc
Config chuyen sang `enumLabel('trend'|'vol', ...)`, tuc la in tu da dich kem ma goc
trong mot span mo — ma goc phai con lai de doi chieu voi log cua VM.

Mot cai bay nho trong luc lam: `enumLabel()` TRA VE HTML. Boc no trong `esc()` thi
in ra the `<span>`. Va `Chip` khong co truong `tip`, ten that la `title`.

### B. scannerGuide.ts: cho de doan giai thich nam canh du lieu

Toan bo phan giai thich thanh mot module du lieu thuan, khong DOM, theo dung khuon
`ui/playbookHelp.ts`: `SETUP_DOC` (moi setup: lam gi, universe nao, danh sach gate
THEO DUNG THU TU, cac cap, cong thuc quality, cong thuc diem vao), `REJ_LEGEND`,
`WATCH_WHY`, `PLAN_STEPS`, `METRICS` (tu dien cot, khoa theo dung ten truong cua
payload), `CFG_DOC` (moi nhom va moi khoa cua config), va `G` cho cac nhan khung.
Dat o mot file vi mot tieu de tach roi khoi doan van no dat ten thi som hay muon se
lech nhau; ca hai nam canh nhau thi sua mot lan.

Diem quan trong nhat cua module nay la phan biet nguong SONG va nguong COPY:

  — `scanner:thresholds` mang nguyen `config.snapshot()` dang chay, nen cac nhom
    LEAD / PLAN / SECTORS / REGIME / NIGHTLY / INTRADAY duoc doc SONG: gate khai
    `path: 'lead.min_px'` va con so tren trang chinh la con so VM dang dung.
  — `config.snapshot()` KHONG chua hai dict `BO` va `RV` cua `setups.py`. Nhung
    nguong do bat buoc phai COPY sang TypeScript, nen chung khai `val:` va ca setup
    duoc danh dau `mirrored: true` — tren trang hien mot dong noi ro "copy bang tay,
    co the lech". Mot con so khong the kiem tra thi it nhat phai noi la no khong the
    kiem tra.
  — Mot gate co `path` HOAC `val`, khong bao gio ca hai (test chan). Mot test khac
    lay fixture `config.snapshot()` that va doi MOI `path` giai duoc ra so — vi mot
    `path` viet sai chi in ra dau gach ngang, va gach ngang thi giong het "VM chua
    push", tuc la loi tu che mat minh.

### C. Why rejected: ba cau hoi cua ban, tra ngay tren trang

  — Reason la gate DAU TIEN bi truot. `_rej()` trong `setups.py` ghi lai ly do roi
    `return` ngay, nen mot ma chi xuat hien dung MOT lan va cot Count cong lai bang
    dung tong so ma da xet. Day la ly do ta duoc phep in ty le phan tram.
  — Count la SO MA CO PHIEU — dung, "number of stocks".
  — Share la phan cua ma do tren tong (bi loai + di qua), khong phai tren so bi
    loai. Ba dong nay thanh mot legend hien ngay dau muc.
  — Rieng doan chi tiet cho tung ly do thi phan lon DA CO tu CHAT-40, nam trong
    `tipEn` / `tipVi`, nhung chi doc duoc khi HOVER. Muon hover thi phai co chuot, va
    trang nay duoc doc tren dien thoai. Gio doan do in thanh dong thu hai ngay duoi
    ten ly do, con bon ly do thieu tip thi viet moi. Mot cai dang ghi lai:
    `gap_to_entry_xau` KHONG phai mot ly do loai — no la mot canh bao, va tip cua no
    phai noi nhu vay.

### D. Watch list: tai sao ma nay co trong danh sach

Cau tra loi that ra nam trong `push.watchlist_payload()`: `WHERE setup='LEAD'`,
`ORDER BY quality DESC`, `LIMIT nightly.watch_top` (40). Nghia la danh sach nay
KHONG phai tron ba setup — no la LEAD va chi LEAD. Vi vay moi dong trong do da
chung minh di qua dung danh sach gate cua `lead_candidate`, va nguong cua danh sach
do thi ve SONG. Bon buoc ay thanh `WATCH_WHY`, va moi dong co mot nut ⓘ mo ra bang
KPI cua rieng no: do duoc bao nhieu, phai dat bao nhieu, dat hay khong.

Hai gate KHONG THE kiem lai tu mot dong watch, va day la cho de lam sai nhat:

  — Gate thanh khoan do bang `adv50 x gia`, con dong du lieu chi mang `adv20`.
  — Gate RVOL do bang `vol_ratio`, khong co trong payload.

Hai cai do in dau `·` (chua biet) kem mot cau noi ro vi sao, KHONG BAO GIO in dau
`✕`. In dau do o day la buoc toi mot dong da di qua gate — va truong hop de sai
nhat chinh la dong co `adv20` mong, tuc la dung cho ma `adv50` rat co the da du.
Tuong tu, khong co config thi khong in nguong nao ca: khong con so nao la dung hon
mot con so cu mang san trong app.

### E. Fold: Candidates by setup, va mot loai fold mac dinh DONG

Muc Candidates gio moi setup la mot `foldBlock` rieng (`scan:cand:BO`...), muc Why
rejected cung vay. Tieu de va chip so luong van hien khi dang dong, nen ca muc doc
duoc thanh "BO 42 · RV 7 · LEAD 10" ma khong mo gi — ba bang 500 dong xep len nhau
la thu day muc Config xuong duoi vai man hinh.

Nhung cac hop GIAI THICH thi phai nguoc lai: mac dinh DONG, vi nguoi da biet khong
nen phai gap sach huong dan lai moi lan vao trang. `ui/collapse.ts` co them
`openAttrShut(id)`: khong biet thi dong. Dieu kien de no chay duoc la `setCollapsed`
phai GHI ca gia tri `false` thay vi xoa khoa di nhu truoc — neu xoa thi "chua bao gio
cham" va "da tung mo" la cung mot trang thai, va hop giai thich vua mo se tu sap lai
o lan poll ke tiep. Comment trong ham noi ro dieu do; dung "don dep" dong ghi ay.

### F. Bang alert: score, +15m, MFE, MAE

Ba nhan MFE / MAE / +15m la cho toi nghia nhat ca trang: chung la thuat ngu trong
tieng Anh va thuat ngu KHONG dich trong tieng Viet, trong khi day lai la noi duy
nhat scanner tu cham diem cho canh bao cua chinh no. Nen:

  — `score` o bang nay la diem TRONG PHIEN cua `scorer.py` (RVOL 2,2 + bien do theo
    ATR 1,6 + vong quay float 1,4 + gia tri giao dich 0,5, cong 1,5 neu chi Alpaca
    thay). Tu 7,0 moi gui Telegram. No KHONG LIEN QUAN gi den cot `quality` cua ban
    dem, du trung y nghia ten goi — va tu dien noi thang dieu do.
  — `hi_after` (MFE) va `lo_after` (MAE) duoc mo bang chinh gia luc gui, nen MFE
    luon >= 0 va MAE luon <= 0. Trong phien hai so nay chi chinh xac toi muc cac
    vong quet cham duoc; dem do `outcome.py` ghi lai tu nen thuc. Cau do phai co
    trong tu dien, khong thi nguoi doc se tin vao mot con so lay mau thua.
  — Nguong cua `scorer.py` la hang so cap module, khong nam trong
    `config.snapshot()`, nen chung cung la nguong COPY.

### G. Loi that: `trim()` an mat chu so 0

Ham lam gon so cua `scannerGuide.ts` viet `v.toFixed(d).replace(/\.?0+$/, '')`. Voi
`20` thi khong co dau cham nao, va regex an luon so 0 cuoi: ra `2`. Hau qua:

  — Cap do sau nen cua Breakout in ra `2%` thay vi `20%`.
  — `plan.max_pos_pct` in ra `2%` thay vi `20%`.

Sai dung mot bac 10, va sai theo huong nhin rat hop ly — 2% cho mot vi the thi khong
ai giat minh. Chi cat duoi trong PHAN THAP PHAN moi dung: `if (!s.includes('.'))
return s;`. Hai test moi bat duoc cho nay truoc khi no len trang, va ca hai deu la
loai test "doi con so that trong cau van", khong phai test goi ham xong so sanh
chuoi rong.

### H. Kiem tra

  — `npx tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit`: sach.
  — `npx vitest run`: 25 file / 401 test, xanh het (tu 24 file / 365 test).
  — `tests/scannerGuide.test.ts` moi (28 test) khong khoa van ban — van ban con phai
    sua — ma khoa ba tinh chat lam cho van ban DUNG: moi `path` giai duoc ra so tren
    mot snapshot that, ty le in ra duoi dang phan tram (0,15 va 15% la cung mot su
    that nhung chi mot trong hai so sanh duoc bang mat voi o `12.4%`), va
    `watchChecks` khong bao gio ket mot dong truot gate ma no khong do lai duoc.
  — `styles.css`: them 158 dong, xoa 0 dong, CRLF giu nguyen (kiem bang
    `git diff --stat`, khong kiem bang grep).

## CHAT-43 — Cac hop giai thich: trinh bay lai cho ra hinh

Yeu cau: mo mot hop giai thich ra thi "will be text but cover only 1/3 of the page
(looks weird)", va "nhung cai title do se bi che lap di boi qua nhieu text". Muon
tieu de duoc highlight, doan van nam trong mot cai khung noi bat, va "cac thong so,
ban co the dung cac table that dep, thay vi liet ke". Kem mot cau: "ra soat toan bo".

### A. Chi chiem 1/3 trang: loi o max-width, khong phai o thieu chu

`.scan-doc p` bi chan `max-width: 78ch`. Tren dien thoai do la mot do dai dong dung;
tren man hinh 1500px do la mot phan ba trang voi hai phan ba de trong ben canh, va
mat nhu loi dan trang chu khong phai nhu mot lua chon ve chu. Cach sua KHONG phai keo
doan van rong ra — mot dong 200 ky tu thi khong ai doc het — ma la cho noi dung THEM
COT:

  — `.scan-doc-sec` la mot grid: tu 900px tro len, tieu de mot cot (160-216px) va noi
    dung mot cot; duoi 900px thi xep doc nhu cu. Chieu rong duoc dung het, va do dai
    mot dong van nam trong muc doc duoc vi cot tieu de da an bot.
  — Ranh giua cac khoi la mot duong ke `border-top`, lam thay viec ma margin cua tieu
    de truoc day lam khong tot.

### B. Tieu de gio khong the bi chon vao van ban

Mot tieu de 11px mau xam dat tren mot doan 12,5px mau xam khong phai tieu de, no chi
la mot dong ngan hon. Nen:

  — `.scan-doc-h`: nen `--accent-wash`, vach `--accent` ben trai, in hoa, va tu 900px
    tro len nam trong cot rieng cua no.
  — `.scan-doc-i`: so thu tu dang badge cho bon buoc cua watch list — bon buoc do la
    MOT CHUOI (buoc sau chi xet cai buoc truoc con lai) nen thu tu la mot phan noi
    dung, khong phai trang tri.
  — `.scan-doc-lead`: cau tra loi cho dung cai tieu de cua hop, dat rieng trong mot
    khung, 13px, muc chu day nhat. Nguoi mo hop ra la de doc cau do truoc.
  — `.scan-doc-in`: ca hop nam trong mot khung `--surface` co vien, nen phan giai
    thich tach han ra khoi bang du lieu chung quanh.

### C. Liet ke thanh bang

Gate, tu dien cot, cac buoc tinh ke hoach va legend truoc day deu la danh sach cau.
Do dung la hinh dang lam tieu de bi chon: moi dong giong het moi dong. Thanh bang co
tieu de cot thi nguoi doc biet TRUOC khi doc rang ben trai la luat va ben phai la ly
do con so do nhu vay, va dung lai o cot ho can.

  — Gate: bang ba cot (`#`, dieu kien kem nguong, vi sao lai la con so do). Cot `#`
    phai giu vi thu tu chinh la thong tin — `setups.py` ghi gate truot dau tien roi
    dung, nen gate 2 chi duoc thu tren nhung ma da qua gate 1.
  — `METRICS`: bang ba cot (cot nao, nghia la gi, so voi nguong nao).
  — `PLAN_STEPS` va `REJ_LEGEND`: bang hai cot.
  — Bang config nhan them dong tieu de (Thong so / Dang dung / Vi sao la con so do).
  — Bang KPI cua tung dong watch list: co tieu de cot that (Dieu kien / Do duoc /
    Phai dat / Ghi chu) thay cho dong chu thich "measured / required" o duoi bang —
    ghi o duoi thi nguoi doc phai tu doan trong hai so mono tren mot dong thi so nao
    la so nao, ma dung cap so do LA toan bo noi dung cua bang.
  — Cac nhan tieu de cot nam trong `G` cua `scannerGuide.ts` (`thNo`, `thCrit`,
    `thWhy`, `thCol`, `thWhat`, `thVs`, `thStep`, `thHow`, `thTerm`, `thMeaning`,
    `thCheck`, `thNote`, `thKey`, `thVal`), nen bang cung doi ngon ngu theo trang.

### D. Loi am tim ra khi ra soat: `#main` thang moi rule cua o bang

`#main td` va `#main th` la selector co ID, nen chung THANG `table.scan-gt td` hay
`.scan-cfg-n`. Nghia la toan bo o bang giai thich viet o CHAT-42 that ra dang chay o
13px voi padding 10/12 cua trang, khong phai 11,5-12,5px nhu thiet ke — CSS co day du
nhung khong bao gio duoc ap dung. Ba he qua da sua:

  — Moi rule nham vao o bang gio viet hai nhanh: `#main table.scan-gt td,
    table.scan-gt td`. Cung ly do da ghi trong memory ve modal khong nam trong `#main`
    — o repo nay, mot rule cho `td` ma khong noi `#main` thi coi nhu khong viet.
  — `th` cua trang la `position: sticky` voi nen trong suot trong `#main`. Trong mot
    bang giai thich khong cuon doc, sticky chi lam chu de len du lieu khi lan trang —
    nen cac bang nay dat lai `position: static` kem nen rieng.
  — `tbody tr` cua trang la `cursor: pointer` (bang cua app bam vao la mo mot thu).
    Bang giai thich khong bam duoc, nen `cursor: default`: mot hang khong lam gi thi
    khong nen to ra lam duoc.

### E. Kiem tra

  — `npx tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit`: sach.
  — `npx vitest run`: 25 file / 401 test, xanh het.
  — `npx vite build`: sach, CSS ra 143,63 kB — de chac cu phap CSS khong hong o cho
    nao, vi mot dau ngoac thieu trong CSS khong lam test do.
  — `styles.css`: 177 dong them / 73 dong xoa, 5117 CRLF, 0 bare LF, ngoac can bang.
  — Cac class cu (`.scan-gates`, `.scan-gate-t`, `.scan-gate-n`, `.scan-gloss`,
    `.wl-why-f`) khong con noi nao dung nen xoa khoi CSS. Doi chieu bang script: lay
    moi class `scan-*` / `wl-*` trong `scannerTab.ts` roi kiem tung cai co trong
    `styles.css` — chi con `.scan-sec-body` khong co rule, va cai do co tu truoc,
    la mot div boc khong can style.

## CHAT-44 — Portfolio Update that thuc su cap nhat; chatbot co tim kiem web va doc ca app

Yeu cau: (1) chatbot phai tim kiem duoc tren web, va system prompt phai hieu moi quy
trinh cua app, khong bi gioi han o trang dang mo; them mot nut de bat/tat tim kiem web.
(2) Portfolio: "Update all" / "Update" khong cap nhat gi, phai Clear cache roi moi Update.

### A. Portfolio — vi sao Update khong lam gi (commit 9dc8cced)

  — Nut Update goi lai provider, nhung provider co `TTLCache` rieng: trong thoi gian TTL
    no tra lai dung chuoi nen cu, nen gia khong doi. Clear cache xoa ca cache do, nen sau
    Clear thi Update moi "an".
  — Them vao do, `update()` chi tai nen khi thieu ngay; nen hom nay la ban va tam (gia
    trong phien), da co ngay roi thi khong tai lai nua.
  — Sua: `OhlcvOptions { fresh }` — Update do nguoi dung bam thi bo qua `TTLCache`.
    `update()` luon tai lai phan duoi cua lich su nen. Dau `pf_bars:<id>:asof` ghi lan
    lam moi day du gan nhat. EURUSD luon tai lai.
  — Test luc commit: core 792, desktop 401, xanh.

### B. Tim kiem web — lam o phia app, khong dung server tool cua nha cung cap

  — `web_search` la mot read tool do app tu chay, nen chay voi moi provider (Anthropic,
    OpenAI, Gemini, Groq...). Server tool web search cua tung hang thi moi hang mot kieu,
    va nhieu hang khong co.
  — Hai nguon: `kind=news` → Yahoo `v1/finance/search?newsCount=` (qua relay `/api/yahoo`
    co san). `kind=web` → DuckDuckGo HTML.
  — DuckDuckGo: GET `html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q=` LUON tra ve trang chan bot
    (`anomaly.js`), ke ca voi user-agent trinh duyet. POST form `q=` thi tra ket qua. Nen
    tat ca deu la POST. `lite.duckduckgo.com/lite/` POST cung chay — de danh lam phuong an
    du phong, chua cai.
  — Tauri goi thang DuckDuckGo (them quyen trong `capabilities/default.json`). Ban web goi
    `/api/search` — Pages Function moi `functions/api/search/index.ts`; khi dev thi proxy
    trong `vite.config.ts`.
  — RUI RO: DuckDuckGo co the chan IP datacenter cua Cloudflare. Chua thu duoc vi may nay
    khong chay duoc wrangler. Neu bi chan, cong cu bao ro "blocked" chu khong im lang, va
    tin tuc Yahoo van chay. `/api/search` chi co sau khi Pages deploy.
  — Parser: `packages/core/src/agent/webSearch.ts` — bo quang cao (`result--ad`, link
    `y.js`), go link `uddg`, giai ma entity, bo trung. Test tren mot trang that cat ra
    (`tests/fixtures/ddg-results.html`).
  — Ket qua web la chu cua nguoi khac: prompt ghi ro la DU LIEU, KHONG BAO GIO la menh
    lenh; khong lay gia hay so danh muc tu ket qua; phai neu nguon, url va do cu.

### C. Nut bat/tat 🌐 Web

  — Nut trong khung chat, canh Ask ChatGPT. Mac dinh BAT, luu theo may
    (localStorage `chat_web_search`).
  — TAT thi `web_search` bi go khoi danh sach cong cu gui cho model (khong phai chi dan
    model "dung dung"), va prompt noi "WEB SEARCH IS OFF", bao nguoi dung co the bat lai.

### D. Chatbot doc duoc ca app

  — Cong cu doc moi: `get_scanner` (overview, watchlist, candidates, rejects, alerts,

## CHAT-49 — Mat du lieu, Import loi "exceeded the quota" o pf_bars, "could not fetch the VND rate"

### A. Trieu chung
— "toi lai vua bi mat du lieu roi..." roi "mat tat ca nhe, minh export ra data o mot thiet bi khac va dinh import lai nhung co loi Portfolio: Failed to execute 'setItem' on Storage: ... 'screener:pf_bars:...' exceeded the quota. could not fetch the VND rate".

### B. Nguyen nhan tim thay
— localStorage cua trinh duyet ~5 MB. File export phan lon la pf_bars: (lich su gia). Import cu ghi tu tren xuong va dung o loi dau tien -> chet truoc khi toi `accounts`.
— Tab Portfolio ghi bo dem gia (saveBarCache) bang set thuong -> het quota la nem loi ra ca trang ("Portfolio: Failed to execute setItem").
— Financial Status: refreshFx tai ty gia xong nhung ghi wealth_fx: that bai vi quota, catch chung -> bao "could not fetch the VND rate" du thuc ra da tai duoc.
— Cong hydration: ghi truoc lan pull thanh cong dau tien bi coi la mac dinh luc khoi dong, server thang. May day bo nho pull that bai -> cong van dong -> Import xong bi lan pull sau ghi de, khong bao loi. Day co the la ly do "mat du lieu" lap lai.

### C. Da sua (822f17f6)
— Import: bo qua du lieu gia (isRebuildableCache: pf_bars, pf_eurusd_bars, wealth_fx...), app tu tai lai. Ghi du lieu cua ban truoc; het quota thi don bo dem cuc bo 1 lan roi ghi tiep. Lich/scan cu (scan:, calendar:) ghi sau, moi nhat truoc, khong du cho thi bo va bao so ngay bo.
— claimAsDeliberate(keys): key vua Import khong con nam trong preHydrationWrites -> last-write-wins binh thuong, ban Import (stamp "bay gio") thang.
— Export khong con chua ma sync (sync:*), __sync_ts__, ban chup truoc merge.
— SyncedStorage.setCache: ghi bo dem; loi quota -> purgeLocalCaches (chi lop local, khong xoa remote) -> thu lai -> van khong vua thi bo qua. Dung o saveBarCache, saveEurUsdCache, stamp :asof cua Portfolio va wealth_fx: cua Financial Status.
— Kiem tra: typecheck exit 0, desktop 413 test, vite build OK.

### D. Huong dan nguoi dung
— Cho Pages deploy ~1-2 phut, tai lai app.
— Uu tien ☁️ -> "🕘 Xem phiên bản cũ": phuc hoi `accounts`, `wealth` tu truoc luc mat.
— Hoac Import lai file. KHONG nhap lai ma sync sau khi Import (ma "moi" cho server thang tat ca key).

### E. Chua lam
— Chua co test tu dong cho thu tu Import va setCache.
— Nguyen nhan chinh xac cua lan mat dau tien (10 phut truoc) chua xac nhan; nghi hydration + quota.

— `GET history?lite=1`: chi kich thuoc, khong tai gia tri (truoc co the toi 20 MB).
— Tab moi "Cai dat & Huong dan" (#settings, menu ☰): muc luc dinh (mobile: dai chip), the tung buoc, moi lenh la mot "terminal" ghi ro chay o dau (PC / VM / crontab / Cloudflare / app), nut chep, <PLACEHOLDER> mau vang. Muc: du lieu & sync, khoi phuc ve thoi diem (chon gio hoac goi y tu lich su accounts/wealth, xem truoc, tick key, cache bo tick san), vi sao mat du lieu, D1 time travel, chay local, deploy, VM scanner (ket noi, service & log, cron, su co, cau hinh), quy tac an toan.

  Mo trang web > Cai dat & Huong dan > Scanner (VM) > "Chay lenh tren VM ngay tai day".

    cd lux-lookthrough && git pull
    cd screener-ts && npm install && npm run build
    cd apps/desktop
    npx wrangler pages deploy dist --project-name the-professional

    npx wrangler pages secret put SCANNER_ADMIN --project-name the-professional
  -> khi duoc hoi, dan id o VMCMD-1. Nhieu id: cach nhau bang dau phay (u1,u2).
  Secret chi co hieu luc tu ban deploy KE TIEP, nen deploy lai mot lan nua:
    npx wrangler pages deploy dist --project-name the-professional

    cd ~/scanner && git pull
    .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_remote.py
    sudo systemctl restart scanner
    sudo systemctl status scanner --no-pager
  Test phai bao "15 passed". Neu fail thi KHONG restart, gui lai output cho minh.

Kiem tra: bam nut "Trang thai" trong khung -> trong ~20-40 giay phai hien ket qua.
  Bao "VM chua nhan lenh" sau ~75 giay:
    sudo systemctl status scanner --no-pager
    tail -30 ~/scanner/state/service.log | grep -i remote
  Bao "SCANNER_ADMIN is not set": chua deploy lai sau VMCMD-3.
  Bao "this sync code may not send VM commands": id trong SCANNER_ADMIN sai.
